```
=== GPT-Image-2 提示词 - 万物生第1集 沙猎部落 机械赛加羚羊 兽形态设计图 v1 ===

【格式】
- 16:9横版+最高分辨率
- 4格均等2x2 = 侧视图 + 正视图 + 顶视图 + 3/4视角
- 同棚拍背景+单侧轮廓逆光环境（继承沙猎部落拍摄DNA）

【兽设定】
精英沙匪搭档——**机械赛加羚羊（Mechanical Saiga）**——独立形态。本张是兽自身的兽形态人设，合体外骨骼形态后续做。

- **种属**：纯机械（古代失落科技遗物+无生物组织+半智能AI）
- **战术**：群体冲撞+蹄踏+奔驰颠簸
- **标志性视觉记忆点**：奇特球状长鼻（机械化）+螺旋竖角
- **体型**：肩高1.2米+体长1.5米（真实赛加放大约1.5倍——保留比例不变成马）
- **DNA**：和合体外骨骼+人物机改+沙艇全部同青铜锈色

---

【提示词】


一张生物设计参考图。**横版16:9宽高比。最高分辨率输出。**纯净均匀的中性偏暖浅灰色棚拍背景。单侧的侧轮廓逆光，光源位于相机右后方45度，无补光、无正面光、无其他光源——富有戏剧性的电影感棚拍样片用光。与沙猎部落人物设计图和沙艇设计图采用相同的棚拍环境——观感上属于同一场拍摄。

【布局】

**2x2均等网格**：
- 左上：侧视图（完整的全侧面轮廓，主视图）
- 右上：正视图（正对镜头，突出展示面部特征——球状鼻和角）
- 左下：顶视图（从正上方俯视的上帝视角，展示占地轮廓与背部机械布局）
- 右下：3/4透视视角（四分之三角度，展示纵深与立体感，主形象镜头）

四格之间用细的中性浅灰色分隔线。每格中生物居中，四周留出均匀边距。

【主体——所有4格中为同一生物】

一头**机械赛加羚羊**——一具古代失落科技的机械战兽，以中亚赛加羚羊为原型改造，被沙猎部落的精英劫掠者改装为战骑。它不是生物，也不是带有生物组织的电子人——**纯机械构造，无任何有机组件**，但具有被部族重新激活的半智能AI意识（一件早已失落的机械文明遗物，如今效命于新主人）。

**种属辨识（关键）**：
**赛加羚羊**（学名 Saiga tatarica）——原产于隋唐边疆领地的中亚草原荒漠物种。极其特定的解剖结构：
- **球状超大鼻吻状鼻子**，下垂超过嘴部，活体时柔软下垂（本机械版本中：呈现为青铜机械鼻吻，鼻身可见空气过滤进气缝隙与若干小排气口——本物种的标志性特征，最关键的辨识标记——若此鼻缺失或被替换成普通羚羊吻部，整个设计即告失败）
- **竖直螺旋角**，公兽特有——笔直朝上的角，带有半透明的环状螺旋（本机械版本中：呈现为青铜机械角，保留原有的螺旋环状结构，角尖磨锐用于战斗）
- **紧凑敦实的躯体**，腿相对较短（不像鹿或瞪羚那样长腿，也不像普通羚羊那样纤瘦——赛加体型像是肌肉发达的绵羊—羚羊混合体）
- 不是普通羚羊、不是鹿、不是瞪羚、不是山羊、不是绵羊——赛加有以球状鼻为主导的独特而无可混淆的剪影

**尺度**：肩高约1.2米，鼻到尾的体长约1.5米。比真实赛加略大（约1.5倍），以满足战骑用途，但保持赛加真实的躯体比例——不能放大到马的尺寸（必须看上去是赛加，而非马形的羚羊）。

**构造（关键）**：
**纯机械，无生物组织。**躯体由以下构成：
- **青铜机械骨—肌骨架**：在原本应是生物骨骼的位置，可见由青铜杆、关节、齿轮、液压式活塞（钟表机械驱动而非蒸汽驱动）构成的机械"骨骼"——赋予该生物其解剖比例
- **青铜板甲"皮毛"**：表面覆盖层叠的青铜板片，近似模拟皮毛的剪影——并非真正的皮毛，也不是生物皮肤——分节的青铜板带有风化锈色，接缝处的缝隙露出底下的机械运动结构
- **机甲深红青铜 #7A6845 + 陈旧金 #846035 锈色**，接缝凹处带铜绿——与沙猎部落的战斗外骨骼、义肢、球体伙伴、沙艇驱动单元完全一致（统一世界观DNA）
- **部族几何符文图记**蚀刻于较大的青铜板上（与青铜球体及部族装备的图记美学一致——抽象的前文明机械符文几何，不是中式装饰纹样，不是真实文化的符文）

**球状机械鼻（关键标志性特征）**：
一只硕大下垂的青铜鼻吻，下垂超过嘴线，长约25厘米，以机械形态复现标志性的赛加鼻剪影：
- 由分节青铜环构成，朝鼻尖逐渐收窄
- 上表面可见**百叶式进气口**（赛加的生物功能是过滤沙漠尘埃——机械版本以机械滤网保留此用途）
- 后侧有小型**排气口**，散发出隐约可见的热浪扰动或蒸汽残痕
- 略微向下垂坠，符合赛加活体的姿态
- 深凹处带铜绿锈色

**角**：
头上装有一对竖直螺旋的机械角，长约30厘米，几乎笔直朝上并略带前倾角度。每只角为青铜质，保留赛加特有的环状螺旋纹（可见棱脊扭转）。金属角尖磨锐用于战斗。螺旋边缘可见轻微的战斗磨损。

**眼睛**：
小而圆的机械眼，**内部青色发光 #4FC3D9**（与青铜球体伙伴的眼部光辉一致——刻意暗示本世界所有机械生物共享的古代失落科技起源）。每只眼周有青铜边框，外覆薄薄的玻璃状镜片罩。眼神显得警觉、半智能、注视着外界。

**腿与蹄**：
四条机械腿，敦实比例与赛加活体相符（相对躯体而言腿较短——不像马或鹿那样长腿）。每条腿在髋、膝、踝处有可见的青铜关节枢轴。脚部为**分趾机械蹄**——两趾青铜蹄套，专为抓握沙地而设计。蹄面可见磨损与沙蚀痕迹。小腿部位展现出驱动奔驰的活塞的精细机械细节。

**尾巴**：
短而粗的机械短尾（约10厘米）——与赛加活体的小尾巴相符。青铜分节构造。

**驾驶舱/合体接口（关键——在顶视图与3/4格中可见）**：
在**背部（脊背）**两肩胛骨之间，有一处清晰可见的**机械驾驶舱舱门 / 驾驶员整合接口**——一个约人体躯干大小的矩形—椭圆接缝，周边围有青铜锁扣，沿一侧边缘可见铰链，侧腹一侧有用于登乘的小踏阶。这是精英劫掠者搭档钻入并与战兽合体之处。本人设图中舱门当前处于关闭状态（展示设计，但呈休眠/独立配置）。舱门接缝下方隐约可见的内部机械结构暗示着内里的吊带/承托座椅。

**部族战利品装饰与搭档绑定信物**：
战兽身上系有适量的战利品装饰：
- 颈/胸部的皮带挽具，编缀有珠串、打磨过的骨片
- 一只小皮囊系在靠近驾驶舱舱门的鞍座区域（内装搭档劫掠者的个人信物——一枚可见探出的小型骨雕护符）
- 3-5件小战利品（被击败敌人的装备残片）以皮绳系于侧腹
- 部族纹样染色布带缠绕于小腿部与角根处
- 机甲深红青铜 + 陈旧金 + 干血红的点缀色

**风化做旧**：
饱经战火的风化质感——深凹处大量铜绿锈色，青铜表面有沙蚀斑点，往昔战斗留下的小凹痕，色调略有差异的拼凑修补块（部族用所能找到的任何青铜，将这件古代遗物维护了许多世代）。不干净、不光亮、不是出厂崭新的。

【4格细节】

**左上格——侧视图**：纯全侧面轮廓视角。兽朝向相机右侧（头朝右）。全身可见，从鼻到尾。球状机械鼻吻醒目地自头部下垂可见。竖直螺旋角可见。敦实的腿部比例可见。从此角度可见背部的驾驶舱舱门。背部战利品装饰可见。侧轮廓逆光照亮上背、角顶与后侧腹。

**右上格——正视图**：兽头正对镜头。**球状机械鼻吻是本格的主导特征**——居中、下垂，可见进气口与排气口。角可见竖直升起并略带前倾角度。两侧青眼发光。前腿可见分立于胸部两侧，分趾蹄踏地。

**左下格——顶视图**：从正上方俯视该兽的上帝视角。展示完整的占地轮廓与背部机械布局。**两肩胛之间背部的驾驶舱舱门在此视角中最为清晰**——带锁扣的矩形—椭圆接缝。部族纹样青铜板的完整布局可见。角从上方看呈成对的竖直尖塔。敦实的躯体比例清晰可读。

**右下格——3/4透视视角**：四分之三角度，展现纵深，约从正—侧偏30至45度，视点略微抬高。侧面+正面+略带顶面皆可见。标志性球状鼻在此角度可见。背部驾驶舱舱门从抬高的角度可见。青眼光辉接住轮廓光。四视图中的"主形象镜头"——动感站姿，一条前腿略抬起，仿佛迈步途中。

【关键一致性】
所有4格中为同一头生物——完全相同的躯体比例（敦实的赛加体型）、相同的球状鼻吻、相同的角、相同的驾驶舱舱门位置与设计、相同的战利品装饰排布、相同的青铜板纹样、相同的风化做旧图案。所有4格采用同一棚拍背景、同一单侧侧轮廓逆光布光设置。

【用光——关键】
**单侧侧轮廓逆光，光源位于相机右后方45度。无补光。无正面光。无环境光。无其他光源。**强烈而戏剧化的边缘光在每一格中照亮生物剪影的右侧，使左侧陷入深度衰减阴影，仅有微妙的环境反射光。青眼光辉在眼周相邻的青铜板上微微自发光。

【风格】
超写实摄影感生物设计渲染——拍摄手法如同为一部电影的制片设计板，在棚内拍摄一具高端实拍机械生物道具。不是插画，不是明显的3D CGI渲染。

**参考美学**：
- **真实世界的赛加羚羊野生动物摄影**（用于准确的物种解剖——球状鼻、竖直螺旋角、敦实比例为必须）
- **吉卜力工作室《幽灵公主》中的灵兽美学**（用于古代神话—机械生物的气质）
- **古代青铜祭祀礼器与汉代墓葬机械人偶**（古代青铜机关——用于失落科技的青铜锈色与环状图记美学）
- **《环太平洋》机甲饱经战火的战斗美学**（用于由原始之手维护的战损打捞质感）
- **《怪物猎人荒野》/《地平线 零之曙光》中的机械沙漠巨兽**（用于当代机械动物的美学参考）

叠加轻微的柯达 Vision3 35mm 胶片颗粒质感。

【画质】
最高分辨率输出。8k超净渲染。锐利干净的边缘。每一道青铜板接缝、每一颗可见关节上的齿轮齿、鼻吻上的每一道进气百叶、角上的每一圈螺旋环、战利品装饰上的每一根皮带都清晰分明。真实实拍机械生物道具的摄影美学。

【格标签】
每格下方小而干净的等宽无衬线黑色文字：
- 左上："SIDE"
- 右上："FRONT"
- 左下："TOP"
- 右下："3/4 VIEW"

画布顶部居中的标题（小而干净的黑色无衬线字体）："MECHANICAL SAIGA — WAR-MOUNT DESIGN SHEET"

【约束】

**物种准确性（最关键——失败风险高）**：
必须是赛加羚羊——球状鼻吻状鼻子是绝对必备的辨识特征。不是普通羚羊、不是鹿、不是瞪羚、不是山羊、不是绵羊、不是北山羊、不是马、不是牛。竖直螺旋环状角（不是弯角，不像捻角羚那样螺旋弯曲，不像鹿角那样分叉）。紧凑敦实的躯体比例（不像马或鹿那样长腿）。

**机械纯粹性**：
纯机械构造，无任何生物组织——不是电子人，不是生物加装甲，不是皮毛与血肉加机械部件。所有表面必须是机械青铜与金属的板片和组件。无真实皮毛、无真实皮肤、无真实肌肉、无真实眼睛（只有带青色光辉的机械眼）。

**美学锚定**：
不是精密的现代机器人，不是出厂崭新的干净金属（只能是饱经战火的风化锈色），不是闪亮的镀铬（只能是带铜绿的陈旧青铜），不是蒸汽朋克维多利亚风（这是古代失落科技，而非工业革命），不是赛博朋克霓虹，不是明显的CGI 3D渲染，不是卡通，不是动漫，不是奇幻精灵工艺的优雅感，不是纯军事化机甲（必须在机械表面之下保留有机生物的剪影）。

**驾驶舱舱门的呈现**：
两肩胛之间背部的驾驶舱舱门必须在顶视图与3/4格中可见（这是战骑系统的关键叙事要素）。不是一头完全封闭的生物——搭档整合接口必须清晰可读。

**布局与用光**：
不是1:1正方形比例（必须横版16:9），不是9:16竖版，不是彩色背景（只能是纯净中性偏暖浅灰色），不是柔和均匀的中性光（必须只有单侧轮廓逆光），不是正面光，不是补光，不是色彩鲜艳饱和的配色（只能是低饱和的沙漠青铜色调），除了四个格标签与标题外不出现任何文字。


=== END ===

【实操注意】

1. 跑出来重点检查（按风险优先级）：
   - **奇特球状鼻（MAX风险）**——赛加的灵魂特征，AI模型很可能跑成普通羚羊吻部。如果没跑出明显下垂的鼻吻状鼻，整图失败重做+按"the bulbous proboscis is the mandatory recognition feature, MUST be visibly oversized and pendulous, NOT a normal antelope muzzle"加强
   - **竖直螺旋角**——容易跑成弯曲羚羊角（如捻角羚）或鹿角分叉。必须是竖直+螺旋+环状
   - **紧凑敦实体型**——容易跑成长腿马或苗条鹿
   - **纯机械（无生物组织）**——容易跑成"皮毛加装甲"的生物机改。必须全是青铜板片
   - **背部驾驶舱舱门**——这是和合体外骨骼系统的视觉钩子+必须在顶视图/3-4格可见
   - **同沙猎部落青铜DNA**（#7A6845/#846035 锈色+铜绿+部族纹）
```

```
@[07ae94325c90b98537a237a49e4de16a]

---

【prompt】


一张人物设计参考图。**竖向 9:16 画幅。最高分辨率输出。**纯色中性偏暖浅灰摄影棚背景。仅单侧侧逆光，光源位于相机右侧、被摄主体身后 45 度方向，无补光，无正面光，无其他光源——极具戏剧张力的电影感摄影棚 lookbook 布光。

【布局】

**2x2 网格，各格高度不等**：
- 上排：两个较小的格子（各约占整幅画布高度的 1/3）——头部/面部特写
- 下排：两个较大的格子（各约占整幅画布高度的 2/3）——全身照
- 四个格子之间以细的中性浅灰分隔线相隔
- 四个格子共用同一纯色背景、同一套布光

【4 格】

**左上格——正面面部特写**：
酋长面部的紧凑特写，取景约从锁骨线向上到头顶。主体正对相机。侧逆光打亮面部/头发的右侧边缘（即画面中相机的左侧），左侧面部则处于更深的阴影中，带有微妙的环境反射光。**左眼**空眼眶疤痕部分处于阴影中但仍清晰可见（轮廓光打亮疤痕组织的隆起）。面部完全可见，无头巾遮住鼻子或嘴巴，头布松松地垂落在肩膀周围。以仅存的右眼平静、直视、冷峻地望向相机。

**右上格——侧面面部特写（正侧面）**：
酋长面部的纯正侧面紧凑特写，取景从锁骨线向上到头顶。主体面朝相机右侧（因此他面部的右侧朝向相机右方，而面部的**左侧**朝向相机——以正侧面完整展现左眼疤痕/空眼眶，由轮廓逆光戏剧性地照亮后脑勺、并将侧面轮廓衬托在背景之上）。面部完全可见。

**左下格——颈部以下全身，正视（无面部）**：
从锁骨线向下到脚的全身照，正对相机。**面部/头部刻意被裁切出本格画面之外的顶端——只有颈部、肩膀、躯干、手臂、髋部、双腿和双脚可见。**侧逆光打亮右肩和身体右侧。**两手都空着——右侧机械手垂在身侧或勾在腰带扣区域，左手垂在身侧。两只手都没有握任何武器。**武器可见地绑缚在身上（战刃斜挎在背后、飞刀挂在腰带上），但他没有握持或抓握任何武器。

**右下格——全身背视（含后脑勺）**：
从头到脚的全身照，背对相机（主体背面朝向相机）。**含后脑勺，可见短而随性的黑色头发，头巾松松地围在颈部/肩膀周围。**斜挎在背后的战刃完整可见。腰带背面的战利品装饰可见。侧逆光打亮右肩轮廓。

【主体——四格中均为同一角色】

一名男性沙漠劫掠者酋长——饱经风霜、伤痕累累的古代失落科技部族猎手美学，沙猎部落的首领。

**角色核心**：
约 **35-45 岁**、经验老到的老兵战士。精瘦结实精悍的体格（不臃肿、不肥胖——沙漠淬炼出的格斗体魄）。

**族裔——关键（与所提供的参考照片相符）**：
**东亚/汉人边塞血统，可能带有丝绸之路混血影响，但以东亚特征为主**。在历史上对应隋唐边塞"杂胡"混合部族中那种由汉人逃兵转为部族军阀的原型。不是纯粹的高加索欧洲人，不是纯粹的中亚突厥人，不是纯粹的蒙古人——**与所提供参考照片相符的东亚特征**。

**面部——细节特征（必须与参考照片相符）**：
- **眼睛**：**单眼皮或极浅重睑（单眼皮）的杏仁形眼睛**，狭长的横向形状，外眼角略微上扬，深棕/黑色虹膜，平静、直视、冷峻的目光（这是关键的东亚特征——不是圆润的西方双眼皮眼睛）。仅存的右眼完好。
- **左眼缺失——即便使用面部参考也必须保留的结构性特征**：一道深的斜向疤痕从左眉之上向下划过空眼眶、延伸到上脸颊；无眼罩，裸露着粗糙的疤痕组织，深黑色的眼眶凹陷。在每一个面部可见的格子里这都是强制的。
- **眉毛**：浓密的黑色眉毛，**略微不对称**（左眉略高且更平直，右眉略微更上挑），自然而不加修饰的生长状态，外端略微下垂。
- **鼻子**：**直挺的中等鼻梁**，既不高挺尖锐也不扁平，鼻尖略圆，鼻孔宽度正常（不是西方高加索人那种细尖的鼻子，也不是夸张的扁鼻梁东亚鼻——与参考相符的中等真实比例）。
- **嘴唇**：**上唇薄、下唇略丰满**，自然放松的状态（不微笑、不皱眉），平直的水平嘴线。
- **下巴**：略尖但不至于明显收窄，下巴前突适度。
- **脸型**：椭圆偏略方，比例均衡。**自然而微妙的面部不对称**（左侧略高于右侧，右眼略大于左眼）——与参考照片相符。
- **皮肤**：**相对白净的东亚肤色**（不是深度晒黑/古铜色，也不是苍白的高加索肤色——中等偏白的亚洲皮肤），但带有沙漠暴晒留下的风霜痕迹，眼周和额头有细纹，鼻梁上偶有愈合的小印记。皮肤读起来像"一个在沙漠烈日下度过多年、但底层仍保留着原本东亚白净肤色的东亚男子"。
- **干净剃须——无胡须、无胡茬、无小胡子**：面部新近刮得干净，脸颊、下颌和下巴皮肤光滑。在每一个面部可见的格子里都是强制的。

**头发**：**短黑发**，两侧保持短，顶部头发短而随性凌乱（前端以一种随性自然的方式略微向前/向上挑起——不做造型，没有现代发胶，只是沙漠生活带来的天然短而乱）。黑色，无白发。

**头饰**：饱经风霜的棕黄与铁锈色沙漠布料制成的部族头巾，部分缠在后脑勺和后颈周围，但在面部特写格里**面部完全不被遮挡**（布料松松垂落在肩膀周围，不遮住鼻子或嘴巴，不遮住顶部头发）。

**上身**：饱经风霜的深色皮革无袖背心，肩膀和前臂裸露，露出战斗伤疤和肩膀及胸部褪色的部族纹身刻记。

**下身**：饱经风霜的宽松深色皮革长裤，腰间有多条皮带和小袋子。

**右前臂——本地机械改造**：右前臂和手被替换为一具**粗糙原始的青铜机械义肢**——不是精密赛博，不是现代。伤痕累累的黄铜与皮革替代品，可见螺栓、绑到上臂残肢的皮带捆扎、腕部裸露的齿轮机构、轻微的铜绿包浆（机改铜青绿 #7A6845，古代暗金 #846035 作点缀色——与沙猎部落战斗外骨骼相同的包浆）。沙漠铁匠拼凑修缮的美学。

**战利品装饰**：动物骨骼（小头骨、爪子、椎骨碎片）和金属碎屑系在腰带和肩带上。被击败敌人装备的残片用皮绳悬挂着——酋长把战斗履历当作装饰来佩戴。数量适中（多于普通劫掠者，又不至于花哨张扬）。

**鞋履**：粗犷的沙漠皮靴，小腿处缠着布带捆扎。

【武器——在身上但不在手中】
- **主武器**：巨大沉重的双手战刃——弯曲的、布满沙蚀坑洼的铁制砍刀-马切刀混合体，凶悍而非优雅，骨与皮缠裹的握柄。**斜挎在背后**（不握在手中）。
- **副武器**：3-4 把短飞刀挂在腰带上，盘绕的抓钩绳/流星锤挂在髋部
- **远程**：复合反曲弓斜挎在背后，附带小箭袋
- **关键：所有格子里两手都空着。**无武器握持，无武器在手，无手搭在武器柄上。右侧机械手自然垂在身侧或随意勾在腰带扣区域。左手自然垂在身侧。

【色板】
沙漠砂黄、深焦赭皮革、做旧青铜包浆（机改铜青绿 #7A6845 + 古代暗金 #846035）、布料捆扎上的干血红部族点缀色。所有材质都饱经风霜、伤痕累累、覆满灰尘，不干净也不抛光。

【姿势——四格统一】
默认站姿，带微妙的力量感站位——重心略微后置，双脚与肩同宽，**两手都空着、垂在身侧或勾在腰带区域**（右侧机械手和左侧生物手都明显没有武器）。仅凭气场流露出微妙的威严/威胁感——不是侵略性的动作姿势，不是放松的平民，不是炫耀式。肢体语言读起来是"一位老兵酋长处于安静的自信中，武器入鞘，因为此刻并不需要"。

【关键一致性】
四格中是同一个角色——相同的面部结构（与参考相符的东亚特征），相同的**左眼**疤痕/空眼眶，相同的干净剃须下颌和下巴（无胡须、无胡茬），相同的服装单品，相同的右前臂机械义肢，相同的战利品装饰，相同的武器位于相同的挂载/位置（全在身上，无一在手）。四格中相同的摄影棚背景、相同的单侧侧逆光布光。

【布光——关键】
**仅单侧侧逆光，光源位于相机右侧、被摄主体身后 45 度方向。无补光。无正面光。无环境光。无任何其他光源。**强烈而戏剧性的边缘光在每一格中都打亮主体轮廓的右侧，使主体左侧落入深度衰减的阴影中，带有微妙的环境反射光。高端电影感时尚 lookbook 摄影棚摄影（彼得·林德伯格 / 安妮·莱博维茨人像风格，单一轮廓光源）。

【风格】
超写实摄影级人物设计渲染——像为电影美术设计板在摄影棚里拍摄的高端实物戏服那样拍摄。不是插画，不是明显的 3D CGI 渲染。

**参考美学**：
- **与所提供参考照片相符的东亚面部结构**——汉人边塞特征
- **《长安十二时辰》（The Longest Day in Chang'an, 2019）**——用于隋唐边塞沙漠民族的部族美学，以及边塞部族语境下东亚面部的准确度
- **《疯狂的麦克斯：狂暴之路》战争男孩首领（用作部族军阀戏服的视觉美学——但换成东亚面孔）**
- **《怪物猎人：荒野》沙漠猎手美学**——用于部族甲胄/风霜皮革/骨制战利品的基因
- 轻柔的柯达 Vision3 35mm 胶片颗粒质感叠加。

【画质】
最高分辨率输出。8k 超干净渲染。锐利干净的边缘。每一条皮带、每一道疤痕、每一件骨制战利品、每一颗机械螺栓都清晰界定。

【格标签】
每格下方一行小而干净的等宽无衬线黑色文字：
- 左上："FRONT FACE"
- 右上："SIDE FACE"
- 左下："BODY FRONT (NO FACE)"
- 右下："BACK"

画布顶部居中的标题（小而干净的黑色无衬线字）："SAND HUNTER CHIEFTAIN — DEFAULT FORM CHARACTER SHEET"

【约束】

**面部准确度（关键）**：
必须与所提供参考照片所示的东亚/汉人特征相符。**单眼皮或极浅重睑的单眼皮眼睛**（不是圆润的西方双眼皮眼睛），浓密自然的眉毛，直挺的中等鼻子（不是西方那种细尖鼻），上唇薄而下唇丰满，下巴略尖。**干净剃须——无胡须、无胡茬、无小胡子**（在每一个面部可见的格子里）。不是纯粹的高加索欧洲人，不是纯粹的中亚突厥人，不是纯粹的蒙古人，不是纯粹的华北北方人，不是现代 K-pop 美学，不是现代华语剧演员美学——**与参考相符的写实东亚边塞老兵面孔**。

**必须保留的结构性特征（在面部参考替换过程中也要保留）**：
- **左眼缺失，空眼眶上有一道深的斜向疤痕**——在每一个面部可见的格子里都是强制的，无眼罩
- **35-45 岁饱经风霜的老兵**——不是 20 多岁的年轻人，不是 60 岁以上的老人，成熟但仍然活跃
- **右前臂粗糙青铜机械义肢**——在身体格里可见

**双手与武器**：
**两手都空** ——无武器握持，无手抓握武器柄。右侧机械手垂在身侧或勾在腰带扣，左手垂在身侧。武器在身上（战刃挎在背后、飞刀挂在腰带、弓挎在背后）但不在手中。不是格斗站姿，不是亮武器站姿。

**身体与体格**：
不臃肿不肥胖（仅精瘦精悍的战士体格）。不是年轻干净脸的平民（35-45 岁饱经风霜的老兵）。

**美学**：
不是精密赛博义肢（仅原始粗糙、伤痕累累的青铜），不是中世纪欧洲，不是现代军装，不是明清宫廷戏服，不是武士铠甲，不是任何干净抛光的东西，不是闪亮的全新镀铬（仅风霜包浆），不是赛博朋克霓虹，不是明显的 CGI 3D 渲染，不是卡通，不是日漫风格，不是鲜艳高饱和的色板（仅低饱和的沙漠色），不是被头巾遮住的面部（在面部格里面部完全可见），左下"BODY FRONT (NO FACE)"格里不出现面部（面部严格裁切掉）。

**布局与布光**：
不是 1:1 正方形画幅（必须是竖向 9:16），不是等大的 4 格（上排小、下排大），不是彩色背景（仅纯色中性偏暖浅灰），不是柔和均匀的中性布光（必须仅为单侧轮廓逆光），不是正面光，不是补光，除了四个格标签和标题之外不出现任何文字。


=== END ===

【实操注意】

1. 跑出来重点检查（按风险优先级）：
   - **东亚单眼皮面部与参考相符**——容易跑成欧美双眼皮或现代 K-pop。必须单眼皮/低重睑+稍长狭眼+亚洲偏白净肤色
   - **干净剃须 无胡须 无胡茬**——容易跑成蓄须老炮（之前 v3 的设定残留）。必须刮干净
   - **左眼缺失+疤痕**——结构性特征，面部参考不能覆盖这个+必须在面部格里强制可见
   - **35-45 岁**——不是 20 岁年轻+不是 60 岁老人。饱经风霜的老兵但仍处于壮年
   - **两手都空**——容易跑成手握武器（之前 v3 的姿势设定）。两手都要明显空+武器都在身上不在手上
   - **右前臂机械义肢**——粗糙伤痕累累的青铜不是赛博精良
   - **9:16+4 格不等大**+左下格不能露脸
   - **同沙猎部落基因**（青铜包浆+部族纹+战利品）

2. 跑歪兜底加强：
   - 跑出欧美双眼皮 → "monolid single-eyelid East Asian eyes, narrow elongated almond shape, NO Western double-fold, matching the reference photo"
   - 跑出蓄须 → "ABSOLUTELY CLEAN-SHAVEN, no beard, no stubble, no mustache, smooth shaven skin on cheeks jaw and chin, mandatory in every face panel"
   - 跑出现代 K-pop 脸 → "weathered 35-45 year old desert veteran East Asian face, sun-aged fine lines around eyes, NOT modern K-pop, NOT smooth young actor face, real frontier veteran with combat history"
   - 跑出手握武器 → "BOTH HANDS COMPLETELY EMPTY, right mechanical hand resting at side or hooked into belt buckle area, left hand resting at side, NO weapon held, NO grip on any weapon hilt, weapons on body but not in hand"
   - 跑出 65 岁老人脸 → "35-45 years old veteran in his prime, NOT elderly, NOT gray-haired, weathered but still capable"
   - 左眼疤痕没了 → "LEFT EYE COMPLETELY MISSING with deep diagonal scar across empty socket, NO eye patch, raw scar tissue exposed, mandatory in every face panel even when face reference is being matched"

3. **关于使用方式建议**：
   - 如果用 GPT-Image-2 图生图：建议把参考图作为面部参考输入 + 用此 prompt 作为文本引导
   - 如果用纯文生图：此 prompt 独立能跑出与参考相符的面部（但可能需要 2-3 次迭代才能达到完美匹配）
```

**🀄 中文版**

```
一张单人全身角色概念设定图，采用3:4竖版人像比例，置于干净的米白色棚拍背景 #F0EDE7 之上，高级时装角色设计摄影美学，超写实服装摄影，透过真实相机镜头捕捉的真实材质质感，照片胶片静帧美学，不是3D渲染，不是CG，不是数字绘画，不是动漫插画，而是一件由真实人体模特穿着、在摄影棚内拍摄的真实手工实体服装，来自左上方的柔和漫射棚拍光配以来自右上方微妙的暖色轮廓光，背景上有微妙的投影，微妙的胶片颗粒。

主体是角色 Danmei @e4bce3ba2f39a302bd80cfdf4f8cae68 处于她觉醒后的战斗形态。她的脸、面部特征、眼形、瞳色、发色、发型以及整体面部身份必须与她的基准参考图绝对精确地一致——角色身份一致性至关重要，这必须毫无疑问地与参考图中是同一个人，只有她的服装发生了改变。

全身站姿，配以优雅的T台式对立平衡（contrapposto）姿态。她居中站立于画面中，重心落于右腿，左腿向外侧伸出并略微偏侧，胯部强烈地朝画面左侧旋转，形成富有戏剧性的S形曲线，双肩反向朝画面右侧旋转，姿态比典型的角色肖像更夸张——更接近时尚杂志封面那种带态度的站姿。她的头略向画面右侧倾斜，下巴下压，目光朝画面正前方侧视，眼神冷峻而傲慢，嘴角带着一丝若有若无的会心冷笑。相机角度略低，仰视着她。

关键比例锚点：Danmei 身高165厘米。她所持的武器也约为165厘米高，几乎与她自身身高完全相等。在构图中，武器与 Danmei 必须呈现出几乎相等的高度，武器从尖端到柄底的全长必须与 Danmei 从头到脚的全身高度相等。武器竖直立于她身侧，与她的站姿平行，无透视缩短，武器长度无透视压缩。她的右手握于刀刃与握柄交接处附近，武器的下端尖部撑在她右侧的地面上，整个武器主体笔直竖立于她身侧，武器顶端高度达到与她头顶齐平或略高。这是一比一的身高对照构图。
```

```
赛加是离摄影机最近的物体，位于前景左下。
刘回骑在赛加上，在同一前景体积内略靠后。
两艘沙艇位于中距离，画面中上区域。
主角的飞船是最远的物体，位于深远背景的右上。
这确立了追逐的层级：骑赛加的刘回正从后方迅速逼近，他前方的两艘沙艇也在追逐那艘作为猎物向远处奔逃的飞船。

赛加的移动方向将带它越过两艘沙艇、朝主角的飞船而去——通过冲锋的角度与相对的空间位置确立这一点。
```

```
=== GPT-Image-2 Prompt - 万物生第1集 沙猎部落单人沙艇 载具设计图 v1 ===

【格式】
- 16:9横版+最高分辨率
- 4格均等2x2 = SIDE + FRONT + TOP + 3/4 VIEW
- 同样的studio backdrop+single-side rim backlight环境（继承沙猎部落photoshoot DNA）

【沙艇定位】
**单人载具**——每个普通沙匪开自己的一艘，多艘从不同方向围猎+各自钩索拉拽="像捕鲸船一样把江流飞船拽停"的战术逻辑。一人独立完成驾驶+发射钩索+战斗。

【关键设计决策】
- **体积小**：约2.5米长（不是Viking longship+不是多人战舰）
- **视觉参考**：现代陆地帆板（land windsurfer）+ sand sled + 古代独木沙舟
- **单人操作位**：站立或半坐半站
- **混合动力**：小帆（主）+ 简化青铜机改驱动器（辅）
- **滑行机制**：沙橇底+辅助小轮
- **部族DNA**：war-torn salvage青铜机改+和合体外骨骼+人物装备同DNA

---

【prompt】


A vehicle design reference sheet. **Horizontal 16:9 aspect ratio. Maximum resolution output.** Pure solid neutral light-warm-grey studio backdrop. Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45 degrees behind the subject, no fill light, no front light, no other light sources—dramatic cinematic studio lookbook lighting. Same studio environment as the Sand Hunter character sheets—reads as part of the same photoshoot session.

【Layout】

**2x2 equal grid**:
- Top-left: SIDE VIEW (full broadside profile, the primary view of the vehicle)
- Top-right: FRONT VIEW (head-on, showing prow and frontal silhouette)
- Bottom-left: TOP-DOWN VIEW (god's-eye view from directly above showing footprint and rigger layout)
- Bottom-right: 3/4 PERSPECTIVE VIEW (three-quarter angle showing depth and dimensionality, hero shot)

Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels. Vehicle centered in each panel with even space margin.

【Subject — same vehicle across all 4 panels】

A Sand Hunter tribe **single-pilot sand skiff**—a small fast desert hunting vessel designed for one raider to operate alone. Pack hunting tactic: multiple skiffs (5-8 of these) swarm a target from different directions, each pilot launching grappling hooks to anchor the prey from multiple angles like harpoon-boats around a whale.

**Overall form (CRITICAL silhouette and scale)**:
A compact single-person desert vessel approximately **2.5 meters long, 0.8 meters wide, 0.6 meters tall** (not counting mast and sail). Think the silhouette of a **land-windsurfer board crossed with a small dugout canoe crossed with a sand sled**—a slender pointed hull with a single standing/half-seated position for one pilot. The hull tapers to a sharp prow and a slightly upswept stern. NOT a large multi-crew ship. NOT a Viking longship. NOT a Mad Max truck. Single-pilot scale only.

**Hull construction**:
Primary hull made of **weathered dark hardwood** carved/hewn into a single dugout-canoe-style shape (one solid piece with shaped interior cavity), reinforced with **bronze rivets and leather lashings** at stress points. War-torn salvage hand-crafted tribal carpentry, NOT factory-precision, NOT clean modern. Patches of mismatched repair wood show old battle damage. Tribal geometric patterns burned into the upper hull edges with branding-iron-style markings (matching the same runic-glyph aesthetic as the rest of the Sand Hunter tribe's bronze equipment).

**Bottom — sand-sled mechanism**:
The hull rides on **a single central long bronze-shod sand runner/skid** running the full length underneath (think: one large central ski-blade). The bronze runner shows heavy wear, sand-pitting, slight verdigris patina. Flanking the central skid, **2 small outrigger bronze-spoked auxiliary wheels** mounted on simple suspension struts on each side help with directional stability and tight turns.

**Salvaged ancient lost-tech drive — small bronze mechanism mid-hull**:
Mounted in a small recessed compartment in the rear-third of the hull is a **compact salvaged ancient bronze mechanical drive unit**—looted lost-tech, retrofitted with leather strapping and crude iron bolts. Smaller and simpler than a multi-crew ship's drive: just a single bronze gear-cluster with exposed clockwork, slight verdigris patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035—same DNA as Sand Hunter combat exoskeletons and prosthetics). Provides auxiliary thrust when wind fails.

**Mast and sail**:
A **single short slender mast** rising about 2.5 meters above the deck, hand-hewn wood, dark weathered. **Small triangular lateen-style sail** of weathered tan-and-rust desert canvas, hand-stitched, with tribal geometric patterns dyed in dried-blood-red across the sail face. Smaller and more agile than a longship's square sail—designed for single-pilot rigging and quick maneuvering. Rigging of weathered hemp ropes, small bronze pulleys, leather reinforcement. In the SIDE VIEW the sail is partially furled showing structure; in the 3/4 VIEW the sail is fully unfurled in a dramatic billow.

**Prow decoration — small tribal totem**:
At the upswept prow, a **small tribal totem affixed to the prow tip**: a single curved animal horn (suggestive of a small ram or sand-creature) bound with leather cords, plus 1-2 small bone fragments hanging beneath. NOT a large beast-skull figurehead (that's reserved for chieftain's flagship). Small-scale tribal marking appropriate for a common raider's solo skiff.

**Pilot position (single-person operation)**:
Located in the **forward-middle of the deck**: a **single standing/half-seated pilot position** with:
- A small wooden tiller/steering arm reaching back to the rear (foot-operated or hand-operated)
- A leather-padded standing rest or knee-rest (the pilot stands semi-braced against this during fast runs, can lean back into it)
- A vertical handhold post or small wooden grip attached to the deck for the off-hand
- **A bronze mounting plate just forward of the pilot position** where a portable hook launcher locks in (the pilot can fire the launcher one-handed while steering with the other hand and feet)
- Leather grip straps along the gunwales to keep the pilot braced during high-speed turns and combat impacts
- A small belt-pouch mount near the pilot's hip for spare grappling hooks/ammo

**Trophy decorations on hull**:
SPARSE — only 2-3 small bone fragments and one small fragment of defeated enemy equipment (broken belt buckle or weapon shard) tied with leather cords to the stern or upper hull strake. Markedly less than a chieftain's flagship would carry. This is a working raider's solo vessel, not a trophy display.

【Color Palette】
Hull: deep weathered umber wood with sun-bleached lighter patches. Bronze fittings/runners/drive: Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035 with verdigris patina. Sail and rigging: weathered tan canvas + dried-blood-red tribal markings. Horn totem: aged ivory-yellow bone. Leather lashings: deep umber. Overall palette: muted desert tones, NOT colorful, NOT saturated.

【4 Panels detail】

**Top-left panel — SIDE VIEW**: Pure broadside profile view. Vehicle facing camera-right (prow to the right). Full length visible from horn-totem prow to stern tiller. Single central bronze runner visible underneath the hull with the small outrigger wheels flanking. Mast vertical with sail partially furled showing sail material and rigging. Side hull tribal carvings, sparse trophy decorations, pilot position with mounting plate all visible. Side-rim backlight catches the upper hull edge and mast/sail.

**Top-right panel — FRONT VIEW**: Vehicle prow facing camera directly. Small horn totem at top center of frame. Hull tapers visibly to the prow point. Single central bronze runner visible underneath with the two outrigger wheels visible flanking on each side. Mast and partial sail visible rising from behind the prow.

**Bottom-left panel — TOP-DOWN VIEW**: God's-eye view from directly overhead looking down at the deck. Shows the slender single-person footprint: mast and sail rigging amidships, single pilot standing position with tiller behind, bronze mounting plate forward of pilot, small drive unit recessed at rear-third. The narrow elongated single-person form factor is clearly readable from above.

**Bottom-right panel — 3/4 PERSPECTIVE VIEW**: Three-quarter angle showing depth, approximately 30-45 degrees from front-side, slightly elevated viewpoint. Both side profile and prow visible. Sail FULLY UNFURLED in this view—dramatic billow showing the small triangular lateen sail size and tribal markings. Single central bronze runner catches the rim light underneath. The "hero shot" of the four views.

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual vehicle in all 4 panels—identical hull dimensions and proportions, identical horn totem at prow, identical sail size and markings, identical drive unit position, identical sparse trophy decoration arrangement, identical bronze runner and outrigger wheel configuration, identical pilot position layout. Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of the vehicle silhouette, leaving the left side in deep falloff shadow with subtle ambient bounce. (For TOP-DOWN VIEW the rim-light still comes from "camera right" within that panel's compass.)

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic vehicle design rendering—shot like a high-end practical prop vehicle photographed in studio for a film production design board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render.

**Reference aesthetics for single-pilot desert craft**:
- **Modern land windsurfer / sand sailing board** — for the single-pilot wind-driven desert craft functional logic and slender silhouette
- **Indigenous dugout canoe construction** — for the hand-hewn single-piece wooden hull aesthetic
- **Mad Max Fury Road war boys minor vehicles (the smaller ones, NOT the big rigs)** — for the war-torn salvage scrap-and-bolt construction style and tribal modification aesthetic
- **Studio Ghibli Nausicaä of the Valley of the Wind small tribal vehicles** — for hand-crafted weathered organic-mechanical fusion
- **Monster Hunter Wilds nomadic desert culture** — tribal weathered-leather and bone-fragment DNA (consistent with the Sand Hunter character sheets and other equipment)

Light Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain texture overlay.

【Quality】
Maximum resolution output. 8k ultra clean rendering. Sharp clean edges. Every wood grain on the hull, every bronze rivet, every gear tooth on the drive unit, every leather lashing knot, every tribal carving clearly defined. Real physical practical-prop cinematic photography aesthetic.

【Panel Labels】
Small clean monospace sans-serif black text below each panel:
- Top-left: "SIDE"
- Top-right: "FRONT"
- Bottom-left: "TOP"
- Bottom-right: "3/4 VIEW"
```

```
=== GPT-Image-2 提示词 - 万物生第1集 沙猎部落单人沙艇 载具设计图 v1 ===

【格式】
- 16:9横版+最高分辨率
- 4格均等2x2 = 侧视 + 正视 + 俯视 + 3/4视角
- 同样的影棚背景+单侧边缘逆光环境（继承沙猎部落拍摄DNA）

【沙艇定位】
**单人载具**——每个普通沙匪开自己的一艘，多艘从不同方向围猎+各自钩索拉拽="像捕鲸船一样把江流飞船拽停"的战术逻辑。一人独立完成驾驶+发射钩索+战斗。

【关键设计决策】
- **体积小**：约2.5米长（不是维京长船+不是多人战舰）
- **视觉参考**：现代陆地帆板（陆地风帆）+ 沙橇 + 古代独木沙舟
- **单人操作位**：站立或半坐半站
- **混合动力**：小帆（主）+ 简化青铜机改驱动器（辅）
- **滑行机制**：沙橇底+辅助小轮
- **部族DNA**：饱经战火的拾荒青铜机改+和合体外骨骼+人物装备同DNA

---

【提示词】


一张载具设计参考图。**16:9横版宽高比。最高分辨率输出。**纯净的中性浅暖灰影棚背景。单侧边缘逆光从镜头右侧、主体后方45度打来，无补光，无正面光，无其他光源——富有戏剧张力的电影感影棚画册布光。与沙猎部落人物设定图相同的影棚环境——读起来像是同一次拍摄场次的一部分。

【布局】

**2x2均等网格**：
- 左上：侧视（完整的全侧身剖面，是载具的主视图）
- 右上：正视（正对镜头，展示船首与正面轮廓）
- 左下：俯视（从正上方俯瞰的上帝视角，展示占地面积与舷外支架布局）
- 右下：3/4透视视角（四分之三角度，展示纵深与立体感，主打镜头）

四个分格之间用细细的中性浅灰分隔线。载具在每格中居中，四周留有均匀边距。

【主体 — 全部4格为同一艘载具】

一艘沙猎部落的**单人驾驶沙艇**——一艘小巧迅捷的沙漠狩猎舟，专为一名沙匪独立操作而设计。群猎战术：多艘沙艇（其中5-8艘）从不同方向蜂拥围攻一个目标，每名驾驶者发射抓钩，像围捕鲸鱼的捕鲸船那样从多个角度将猎物钩住固定。

**整体形态（极其关键的轮廓与尺度）**：
一艘紧凑的单人沙漠舟，长约**2.5米，宽约0.8米，高约0.6米**（不计桅杆与帆）。可联想为**陆地风帆板与小型独木舟与沙橇相杂交**的轮廓——细长的尖头船身，配一个供单人站立/半坐的操作位。船身收窄至尖锐船首，船尾略微上翘。不是大型多人战舰。不是维京长船。不是疯狂麦克斯式卡车。仅为单人驾驶尺度。

**船身构造**：
主船身由**风化的深色硬木**雕凿成单体独木舟造型（整块实木掏出成形的内腔），在受力点用**青铜铆钉与皮革绑缚**加固。饱经战火的拾荒手工部族木作，不是工厂级精度，不是干净的现代制品。拼凑的修补木料补丁透出旧日战损的痕迹。船身上缘烙有部族几何纹样，以烙铁烫印般的印记呈现（与沙猎部落其余青铜装备相同的符文-字符美学一致）。

**底部 — 沙橇机制**：
船身骑在**一条贯穿底部全长的中央长青铜包面沙撬/滑橇**之上（可想象成一片巨大的中央滑雪板刃）。青铜滑橇磨损严重，有被沙粒磨蚀的麻点、轻微的铜绿包浆。在中央滑橇两侧，**2个小型青铜辐条舷外辅助轮**安装在简易悬挂支柱上，每侧一个，用于辅助方向稳定与急转弯。

**拾荒而来的古代失落科技驱动器 — 船身中部的小型青铜机构**：
在船身后三分之一处一个小型凹陷舱内，安装着一台**紧凑的拾荒古代青铜机械驱动单元**——劫掠而来的失落科技，用皮带捆扎与粗糙铁螺栓改装而成。比多人战舰的驱动器更小更简单：只有单组青铜齿轮组配外露发条机构，轻微铜绿包浆（机甲绯红青铜 #7A6845 + 陈旧金 #846035——与沙猎部落战斗外骨骼及义肢相同DNA）。在风力不足时提供辅助推力。

**桅杆与帆**：
**一根短小细长的桅杆**，高出甲板约2.5米，手工凿制的木料，深色风化。**小三角拉丁式帆**，由风化的棕褐与锈红沙漠帆布制成，手工缝制，帆面横向染有干血红的部族几何纹样。比长船的方帆更小更灵活——专为单人操帆与快速机动而设计。索具为风化的麻绳、小青铜滑轮、皮革加固。在侧视中帆部分收起以展示其结构；在3/4视角中帆完全张满，戏剧性地鼓胀。

**船首装饰 — 小型部族图腾**：
在上翘的船首处，**一个固定于船首尖端的小型部族图腾**：一只弯曲的兽角（让人联想到小公羊或沙生生物），用皮绳绑缚，其下另垂挂1-2片小骨片。不是大型兽骨船首雕像（那是专属于酋长旗舰的）。是与普通沙匪单人小艇相称的小尺度部族标记。

**驾驶位（单人操作）**：
位于**甲板前中部**：**一个供单人站立/半坐的驾驶位**，配有：
- 一根向后延伸至船尾的小木舵柄/操舵臂（脚控或手控）
- 一处皮革衬垫的站立支撑或膝靠（驾驶者在高速航行中半倚着它撑住身体，可向后倚靠）
- 一根竖直的扶手柱或固定在甲板上的小木握把，供副手抓握
- **驾驶位正前方一块青铜安装板**，便携抓钩发射器锁定于此（驾驶者可一手发射、另一手与双脚操舵）
- 沿舷缘的皮革握带，在高速转弯与战斗冲击中帮助驾驶者稳住身体
- 驾驶者髋部附近一个小腰袋挂位，用于放置备用抓钩/弹药

**船身上的战利品装饰**：
稀疏——仅2-3片小骨片，外加一片被击败敌人的装备残块（破损的皮带扣或武器碎片），用皮绳系于船尾或船身上缘列板。明显少于酋长旗舰所携带的数量。这是一艘干活用的沙匪单人船，不是战利品陈列。

【色板】
船身：深沉风化的赭木色，带日晒褪色的浅色斑块。青铜配件/滑橇/驱动器：机甲绯红青铜 #7A6845 + 陈旧金 #846035，带铜绿包浆。帆与索具：风化棕褐帆布 + 干血红部族纹样。兽角图腾：陈旧的象牙黄骨色。皮革绑缚：深赭色。整体色板：低饱和沙漠色调，不鲜艳，不饱和。

【4格细节】

**左上格 — 侧视**：纯侧身剖面视图。载具朝向镜头右侧（船首朝右）。从兽角图腾船首到船尾舵柄全长可见。船身下方可见中央单条青铜滑橇，两侧有小型舷外轮辅翼。桅杆竖直，帆部分收起，展示帆料与索具。船身侧面部族雕纹、稀疏战利品装饰、带安装板的驾驶位皆可见。侧边逆光勾出船身上缘及桅杆/帆的边缘。

**右上格 — 正视**：载具船首正对镜头。小兽角图腾位于画面顶部中央。船身明显向船首尖端收窄。底部可见中央单条青铜滑橇，两侧各有一个舷外轮可见。桅杆与部分帆自船首后方升起可见。

**左下格 — 俯视**：从正上方俯瞰甲板的上帝视角。展示细长的单人占地：桅杆与帆索具居中，单人站立驾驶位与其后舵柄，驾驶位前方的青铜安装板，后三分之一处凹陷的小驱动单元。狭长的单人形制从上方清晰可读。

**右下格 — 3/4透视视角**：四分之三角度展示纵深，约从前侧方30-45度，视点略微抬高。侧身剖面与船首皆可见。此视图中帆**完全张满**——戏剧性地鼓胀，展示小三角拉丁帆的尺寸与部族纹样。底部中央单条青铜滑橇承接边缘光。是四个视图中的"主打镜头"。

【关键一致性】
全部4格为**同一艘**载具——船身尺寸与比例一致，船首兽角图腾一致，帆的尺寸与纹样一致，驱动单元位置一致，稀疏战利品装饰的排布一致，青铜滑橇与舷外轮配置一致，驾驶位布局一致。全部4格采用同样的影棚背景、同样的单侧边缘逆光布置。

【布光 - 关键】
**单侧边缘逆光从镜头右侧、主体后方45度打来。无补光。无正面光。无环境光。无其他光源。**强烈而富有戏剧张力的边缘光勾出载具轮廓的右侧，使左侧陷入深度衰减阴影中，仅有微妙的环境反弹光。（俯视图中边缘光仍来自该格自身方位中的"镜头右侧"。）

【风格】
超写实摄影级载具设计渲染——如同为电影美术设计板在影棚中拍摄一台高端实体道具载具那样拍摄。不是插画，不是明显的3D CGI渲染。

**单人沙漠舟的参考美学**：
- **现代陆地风帆/沙地帆板** —— 用于单人风力驱动沙漠舟的功能逻辑与细长轮廓
- **原住民独木舟构造** —— 用于手工凿制单体木船身的美学
- **疯狂麦克斯：狂暴之路战争男孩的次要载具（较小的那些，不是大型重卡）** —— 用于饱经战火的拾荒废料加螺栓构造风格与部族改装美学
- **吉卜力工作室《风之谷》中的小型部族载具** —— 用于手工制作的风化有机-机械融合
- **《怪物猎人：荒野》游牧沙漠文化** —— 部族风化皮革与骨片DNA（与沙猎部落人物设定图及其他装备保持一致）

叠加轻度柯达 Vision3 35mm 胶片颗粒质感。

【质量】
最高分辨率输出。8k超干净渲染。锐利干净的边缘。船身上每一道木纹、每一颗青铜铆钉、驱动单元上每一个齿、每一个皮革绑缚结、每一处部族雕纹都清晰可辨。真实物理实体道具的电影摄影美学。

【分格标签】
每格下方一行干净的等宽无衬线黑色文字：
- 左上："SIDE"
- 右上："FRONT"
- 左下："TOP"
- 右下："3/4 VIEW"

画布顶部中央标题（干净的小号黑色无衬线字体）："SAND HUNTER SOLO SKIFF — VEHICLE DESIGN SHEET"

【约束】

**尺度与乘员**：不是多人战舰，不是大型船只，不是长船尺寸（必须是小型单人2.5米长），不是2人，不是3人，不是配多名乘员（仅单人驾驶）。

**形制**：不是水上船（这是一台带帆的沙橇，不是水上载具），不是轮式手推车/越野小车（轮子是辅助稳定器，不是主要动力来源——滑橇才是主要），不是现代沙漠沙丘越野车，不是摩托车，不是气垫船，不是飞行载具。

**美学锚定**：不是精密赛博机械，不是干净的现代工程（仅限饱经战火的拾荒部族手工），不是蒸汽朋克维多利亚美学（这是古代失落科技，不是工业革命产物），不是闪亮的全新铬合金（仅限风化包浆），不是彩色喷漆（仅限低饱和沙漠色调），不是赛博朋克霓虹，不是明显的CGI 3D渲染，不是卡通，不是动漫，不是奇幻精灵造物的优雅。

**装饰克制**：不是大型兽骨船首雕像（那是酋长旗舰——仅用小兽角图腾），不是大量战利品装饰（稀疏，仅2-3处），不是华丽雕刻或珠宝（仅部族几何烙印）。

**布局**：不是1:1方形比例（必须是横版16:9），不是9:16竖版，不是彩色背景（仅纯中性浅暖灰），不是柔和均匀的中性布光（必须仅为单侧边缘逆光），不是正面光，不是补光，除四个分格标签与标题外不出现任何其他文字。


=== END ===

【实操注意】

1. 跑出来重点检查：
   - **单人载具**（最大风险）——容易跑成多人战舰。必须是2.5米长细长单人尺度，不是长船不是疯狂麦克斯大型重卡

   - **小兽角图腾 + 稀疏战利品**——区别于酋长旗舰会有的大兽骨与精致装饰
```

```
在她下方，沙丘戏剧性地向下倾斜，没入一片广阔的沙漠谷地。在谷底的远处，七个小小的身影以散开队形推进——沙漠劫掠者，面孔以破布缠裹，身着层叠的皮革与布甲，持弯刀，虽小但可辨认出是逼近而来的武装人类。
```

```
[左上格——标注 "FULL FRONT"]
一柄巨大的双手工业巨剑竖直陈列，剑身朝上、剑柄朝下，这件武器约有一名成年人的身高，其整体剪影与结构是《怪物猎人：世界》中飞雷点火大剑的忠实一比一复刻品——相同的、令人压迫的矩形板状剑身比例，相同的、位于剑身尖端的外扩梯形喷气引擎式排气阵列，相同的、带工业切削轮廓的宽矩形剑身主体，相同的剑身中段信息面板模块，相同的、沿剑身两侧延伸的弯曲青铜管路系统，相同的、剑身主体上的小椭圆排气孔，相同的、以配重球收尾的极简长握柄。整个结构设计是工业武器硬表面风格，每一条边缘都是带棱角的机加工切削轮廓，每一处接合都露出可见的铆钉和结构接缝。

材质语言是关键的转化——不再是原版的枪铁灰钢材与绯红漆面，这柄剑的每一处装甲表面都由密集排列的古董青铜六边形板片构成，这种六边形符号浮雕板片材质与中国古代青铜礼器上所见的相同，#7A6845 低饱和青铜绿为基色、#846035 更深的青铜色为凹陷处，每一块六边形板片都铸有凸起的古代篆书和道家符箓符号浮雕纹样，每块板片之间是细的发光青色 #4FC3D9 接缝线，更深的缝隙中有氧化斑块和铜绿，是真实金属风化而非数字磨损，表面划痕露出包浆层之下暗淡的底层青铜。飞雷点火设计中原本的绯红漆面点缀区域，在此被转译为带集中铜绿绿色氧化的更深青铜凹陷区，提供与原版绯红相同的色彩对比作用，只是改用青铜包浆语言。

顶部排气结构是从剑身主体向外扇形展开的外扩梯形阵列、由平行的水平格栅缝构成，六到八条格栅缝以标志性的飞雷点火喷气排气构型竖向堆叠，每条缝都以铸青铜镶框、带倒角边缘和铆钉细节，缝隙内部深邃黑暗，深处可见微弱的青色 #4FC3D9 辉光，暗示着休眠的能量腔室，排气表面没有任何铭文或文字，纯粹是带青铜材质语言的工业结构设计。

剑身中段信息面板模块保留了原版飞雷点火设计中精确的形状与位置——一块嵌入剑身中央的扁平矩形板，但原版那块带粉紫电路纹蚀刻的蓝灰色面板，在此被替换为一块更深的青铜板 #5A4A35、以分段青铜框边镶框并带铆钉，面板表面是光滑的青铜带微妙包浆，无铭文，无眼睛，无额外添加的符号——只是占据原版信息模块精确位置的、干净的深青铜板。

那套从排气阵列向下贯穿剑身、沿剑身两侧延伸的弯曲管路系统，由原版的黄铜液压管转译为分段古董青铜管路，与蟹形青铜器的眼柄分段构造相匹配，每段管节由带可见铆钉的青铜环接头连接，接头之间小的青色 #4FC3D9 发光接点漏出微弱的光，管路沿循原版飞雷点火管系精确的弯曲走向。

剑身主体两侧的小椭圆排气孔保留了它们原本的位置与形状，但如今以青铜铸造、边缘带包浆变暗，每个椭圆排气孔深处可见微弱的青色辉光。

剑身底部经由一段渐缩青铜节过渡到护手——一小片弯曲的新月形护手，深青铜带铜绿。

握柄保留了原版飞雷点火手柄的精确形态——一段表面带凸起螺旋脊的长圆柱握柄，但材质是偏红的铜 #A85530 抛光铜、螺旋纹路清晰可见，铜材呈现出真实的岁月包浆与更暖的红色调，这段铜握柄在整件武器更冷的青铜绿之中提供了温暖的色彩点缀，螺旋纹路与原版设计完全一致。

整件武器呈现出真实金属手工锻造的做旧——青铜包浆带天然铜绿，铜的暖色包浆，没有数字噪点纹样，没有程序化纹理，只有物理做旧金属那种不规则的天然之美。

同一柄巨剑以纯正侧面从右侧观看，剑身仍然竖直。侧视图揭示出武器的厚度轮廓——剑身是一块厚实的工业板状物，与原版飞雷点火的侧面轮廓相符，展现出顶部排气腔的深度，分段青铜管路此时清晰可见地沿剑身脊缘延伸，呈现为带青铜环接头的三维管路，青色 #4FC3D9 辉光从段间接点漏出。从这一侧看，剑身中段的青铜信息面板表现为从剑身主体微微凸起的部分。从这一角度，顶部排气结构揭示出平行格栅缝向剑身主体内部延伸的深度，宛如真实的喷气排气歧管，内部可见青色辉光。偏红铜的螺旋握柄连同底部可见的小铜柄尾球。同样的超写实材质渲染——古董青铜六边形板片工艺、铜握柄、真实金属风化。

[左下格——标注 "VENT DETAIL"]
剑身顶部排气结构的特写微距镜头，取景紧凑，使排气结构填满格子的大部分，下方留有一小部分上方青铜六边形板片以提供语境。这一镜头揭示出青铜喷气排气阵列的精细工业细节：每条平行格栅缝都是一个深邃干净的矩形腔体、以铸青铜镶框，缝缘带工业机加工倒角、沿结构框架有可见的小铆钉，排气孔周围的青铜表面呈现出真实的包浆质感，铜绿在凹陷接缝中积聚，划痕和氧化露出之下暗淡的底层青铜，排气结构上任何地方都没有铭文或字符，纯粹是工业机加工青铜表面。格栅缝深处可见青色辉光 #4FC3D9，呈现为柔和的体积雾、带微弱的内部结构、暗示能量腔室，青色辉光在各缝之间强度不一，暗示着休眠的脉动能量，无粒子，无纸片。排气框下方的青铜六边形板片可见，展现出它们标志性的古代符号浮雕铸造、板片之间带青色辉光接缝线。紧邻排气框的是分段青铜管路系统的上端，展现出青铜管接入排气基部一个外扩接头的样子。整个微距镜头强调这是一件用微距镜头拍摄的真实手工锻造青铜武器道具。

[右下格——标注 "GRIP DETAIL"]
武器握柄与柄尾区域的特写微距镜头，取景展现青铜剑身下部经青铜护手向下过渡到偏红铜螺旋握柄、并终结于铜柄尾球的过程。这一镜头揭示出材质的对比：剑身底部更冷的青铜绿六边形板片与握柄更暖的偏红铜 #A85530 相接，铜握柄上的螺旋纹路清晰可见、凸起的脊在摄影棚主光下泛光，铜表面呈现出真实的暖色包浆、螺旋凹槽中微妙变暗，真实金属氧化。剑身与握柄之间的新月形小青铜护手在其曲线中呈现出铜绿绿色包浆。在握柄最底端，带微小尖头的小圆铜柄尾球完全可见，是相匹配的暖色铜带岁月包浆。任何地方都没有铭文、没有符号、没有字符——纯粹是材质与结构细节。整个细节镜头强调两种金属的冷暖对比以及手工锻造的物理材质真实感。

整体呈现：干净的米白色摄影棚背景 #F0EDE7，来自左上方的柔和漫射灯光营造出柔和的阴影，整幅图像带微妙的胶片颗粒，中性的调色突出青铜与铜的色调，摄影级的景深、每个格子全幅锐利，没有花哨的 CGI 特效，这是一张设计参考图，将一件想象中的青铜与铜武器当作一件真实的物理博物馆道具、由产品摄影师拍摄那样处理，四个格子之间古董青铜六边形板片材质与暖色铜握柄材质的一致性至关重要。
```

```
【Layout】

**2x2 grid with unequal panel heights**:
- Top row: two smaller panels (each approximately 1/3 of total canvas height) — head/face close-ups
- Bottom row: two larger panels (each approximately 2/3 of total canvas height) — full body shots
- Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels
- All four panels share the same solid backdrop and same lighting setup

【4 Panels】

**Top-left panel — FRONT FACE CLOSE-UP**:
Tight close-up of the female raider's face, framed from approximately collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera directly. Side-rim backlight catches the right edge of the face/hair (camera's left side of frame), leaving the left side of the face in deeper shadow with subtle ambient bounce. Both eyes intact, alert focused gaze toward camera. **Sand goggles pushed up onto her forehead/hairline** (signature equipment visual—this is her defining feature, the pilot's gear at rest). Face fully visible—no headwrap covering nose or mouth.

**Top-right panel — SIDE FACE CLOSE-UP (PROFILE)**:
Tight close-up of her face in pure side profile, framed from collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera-right. Side-rim backlight catches the back of the head and silhouettes the profile against the backdrop. Sand goggles still pushed up on forehead visible from side angle. Black hair tied back into a high practical ponytail catches the rim light. Face fully visible.

**Bottom-left panel — FULL BODY FROM NECK DOWN, FRONT VIEW (NO FACE)**:
Full body shot from collarbone-line down to feet, facing camera directly. **The face/head is intentionally cropped out of frame at the top of this panel—only the neck, shoulders, torso, arms, hips, legs, and feet are visible.** Zero face involvement so this panel survives any future face replacement edit. Side-rim backlight catches the right shoulder and right side of body, defining lean athletic silhouette. **Mechanical communicator/whistle device on a leather cord around the neck visible**, throwing-dart belt across torso visible.

**Bottom-right panel — FULL BODY BACK VIEW (INCLUDING BACK OF HEAD)**:
Full body shot from head to feet, facing directly away from camera (back of subject toward camera). **Includes back of head with high practical ponytail of black hair visible falling down to upper-back length**, sand goggles pushed up on top of head visible from back angle. Short javelin/throwing spear strapped diagonally across upper back visible. Trophy decorations on back of belt visible (small/sparse). Side-rim backlight catches right shoulder silhouette.

【Subject — same character across all 4 panels】

A female desert raider, common-rank tribe member specializing in sand-skiff piloting for hunting parties. Youngest of the tactical unit.

**Character Core**:
Approximately 23-26 years old. **Lean wiry athletic combat physique—slender but strong, the build of a desert sailor used to constant balancing and quick movement on a moving sand-skiff. NOT bulky muscular, NOT model-fashion thin, NOT voluptuous, NOT teen-aged delicate. Built like a working pilot-raider.**

**ETHNICITY — CRITICAL**: Central Asian / Turkic / Sogdian Silk Road ethnic features—the historically accurate "杂胡" mixed-tribe peoples of the Sui-Tang frontier desert. Wide prominent cheekbones, deep-set almond-shaped eyes with monolid or low-fold (NOT round Western double-fold), black or very dark brown eyes, black hair, weathered tan-bronze skin from desert sun and wind exposure, strong jawline, straight or slightly broad nose (NOT thin pointed Western Caucasian), thick natural eyebrows.

**Hair**: black hair pulled tightly back into a high practical ponytail falling to upper-back length, kept tight and out of the face for piloting wind-exposed sand-skiffs. Possibly a few loose strands at temples from wind.

**Battle marks**: a small fresh scar across the bridge of nose, a healed thin scar on right cheek—the marks of a working raider who has crashed sand-skiffs and survived. Sun-and-wind weathered skin texture.

**Headwear (CRITICAL signature equipment)**: **bronze sand-pilot goggles** with thick leather strap and double round lenses (slightly tinted dark amber for sun protection)—pushed up onto her forehead/hairline at rest, NOT covering her eyes (this is the "goggles up, off duty" pose for character sheet purposes). Mechanical bronze frame, clearly war-torn salvage construction with visible rivets and a small hinge mechanism, slight verdigris patina.

**Communicator pendant**: hanging on a leather cord around her neck—a small bronze whistle/horn device with mechanical components (used by sand-skiff crews to signal each other across formation distances). Same Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035 patina as the rest of the tribe's equipment.

**Upper body**: weathered dark leather sleeveless cropped vest cut to allow free shoulder movement (functional pilot cut, NOT decorative belly-baring), exposing arms and partial midriff with battle scars and small tribal tattoo markings. Tightly laced front for secure fit during fast skiff maneuvers.

**Lower body**: form-fitting weathered dark leather breeches/trousers with reinforced inner thigh and seat panels (rider/pilot wear). Multiple belt straps with small pouches around waist (skiff control tools, signal flares, small mechanical components).

**Equipment belt across torso**: a leather bandolier crossing diagonally from right shoulder to left hip, holding **6-8 small bronze throwing darts/spike-darts** (her primary projectile weapons—small fast and numerous, designed to be thrown from a moving skiff). Some darts have small fletching.

**Trophy decorations**: SPARSE — a few small bone fragments, one small enemy belt buckle, a single trade bead, hanging from leather cords on the belt. Slightly more numerous than raider A but markedly less than the lieutenant or chieftain.

**Footwear**: knee-high desert leather pilot boots with cloth bindings around the calves, reinforced soles for grip on a moving deck.

【Weapons】
- **Primary projectile**: 6-8 small bronze throwing darts in the bandolier belt across torso
- **Secondary melee**: short javelin/throwing spear (about 3 feet long, bronze tip, can be thrown or used as short polearm) strapped diagonally across the upper back, visible in side and back panels
- **Sidearm**: small combat knife at right waist belt

【Color Palette】
Desert sand tan, deep burnt umber leather, aged bronze patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035), dried-blood-red tribal accent on cloth bindings, dark amber goggle lenses. ALL materials weathered war-torn dust-coated, NOT clean NOT polished.

【Pose - same across all 4 panels】
Default standing pose with feet shoulder-width apart, weight balanced lightly on the balls of the feet (the stance of someone always ready to react and balance). Hands resting near throwing darts at the bandolier or at sides. Subtle alert pilot energy—NOT aggressive action pose, NOT relaxed civilian, NOT seductive/sensual pose, NOT cute/coy pose.

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual character in all 4 panels—identical face structure (Central Asian/Turkic/Sogdian features), identical hair (high tight ponytail), identical sand goggles position (pushed up on forehead), identical clothing items, identical bandolier belt with identical dart count, identical communicator pendant, identical sparse trophy decorations. Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of subject silhouette in every panel, leaving the left side of subject in deep falloff shadow with subtle ambient bounce.

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic character design rendering—shot like a high-end practical costume photographed in a film production studio for a cinematic lookbook reference board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render.

**Reference aesthetics for Central Asian Silk Road ethnic accuracy**:
- **《长安十二时辰》(The Longest Day in Chang'an, 2019) 西域狼卫** — Sui-Tang Silk Road frontier desert peoples ethnographic anchor
- **Mongol: The Rise of Genghis Khan (2007)** — Central Asian battle-hardened warrior features (note: female ethnographic reference here)
- **Monster Hunter Wilds desert hunter aesthetic** — for tribal armor/weathered-leather DNA

Light Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain texture overlay.

【Quality】
Maximum resolution output. 8k ultra clean rendering. Sharp clean edges. Every leather strap, every dart, every rivet on the goggle frame clearly defined.

【Panel Labels】
Small clean monospace sans-serif black text below each panel:
- Top-left: "FRONT FACE"
- Top-right: "SIDE FACE"
- Bottom-left: "BODY FRONT (NO FACE)"
- Bottom-right: "BACK"

Title at top center of canvas (small clean black sans-serif): "SAND HUNTER COMMON RAIDER B · SKIMMER — DEFAULT FORM CHARACTER SHEET"

【Constraints】
**Ethnic accuracy (CRITICAL)**: NOT pure Caucasian European face, NOT blonde or red or light brown hair, NOT blue or green or grey or hazel eyes, NOT pale fair Northern European skin, NOT thin pointed delicate Western nose, NOT round Western double-fold eyes, NOT pure Han Chinese face, NOT modern K-pop or Chinese drama actress aesthetic, NOT cute kawaii anime face. MUST be Central Asian Turkic/Sogdian Silk Road mixed ethnicity.

**Body, age & framing**: NOT bulky muscular, NOT model-fashion thin, NOT voluptuous sensualized body, NOT teen-aged delicate, NOT young teen schoolgirl appearance (must be 23-26 working pilot-raider veteran of crashes), NOT seductive pose or framing, NOT cute/coy expression, NOT belly dancer aesthetic, NOT fantasy-game-female-warrior chainmail-bikini aesthetic, NOT chibi anime aesthetic.

**Equipment differentiation**: NOT a prosthetic limb replacement (no mechanical limbs, only wearable equipment), NOT heavy quantity of trophy decorations, goggles must be PUSHED UP ON FOREHEAD not over eyes (off-duty stance for character sheet), NOT chieftain-level brutalist trophy mass, NOT lieutenant-level refined woven beads, NOT same equipment as raider A (no lasso winch, no back-mounted hook launcher).

**Aesthetic**: NOT precision cybernetic, NOT modern, NOT clean (war-torn salvage bronze only), NOT medieval European, NOT Ming-Qing imperial, NOT samurai, NOT cyberpunk neon, NOT steampunk Victorian, NOT obvious CGI 3D render, NOT cartoon, NOT anime, NOT colorful saturated palette.

**Layout & lighting**: NOT 1:1 square aspect (must be vertical 9:16), NOT equal-sized 4 panels (top row smaller, bottom row larger), NOT face visible in bottom-left panel (face strictly cropped out at top of panel), NOT face covered by goggles in face close-up panels (goggles must be UP on forehead, eyes uncovered), NOT colorful background (solid neutral light-warm-grey only), NOT soft even neutral lighting (must be single-side rim backlight only), NOT front light, NOT fill light, NOT any text other than the four panel labels and the title.
```

```
=== GPT-Image-2 提示词 - 万物生第1集 沙猎喽啰B · 沙艇驾驶手 默认形态四视图人设图 v1 ===

【格式继承】
完全同头领/女副手/喽啰A格式：9:16竖版+4格不等大+正脸特写/侧脸特写/脖子以下/背面+single-side rim backlight。

【角色定位】
沙猎部落普通喽啰之一——**沙艇驾驶手 Skimmer**。功能：驾驶沙艇围困目标。在战术单位里负责"机动围困"环节，配合A钩索手锁定+C弓箭手远程支援。

【性别/年龄说明】
B设为女性（25岁，最年轻）——给沙猎部落"女性参与战斗"的世界观（中亚游牧文化女战士的历史先例）+和女副手（30-40岁veteran）形成"年轻新一代女性"的代际层次。她不是少女向甜美形象，是working raider+只是性别不同。

---

【prompt】


一张角色设计参考图。**竖版 9:16 画面比例。最高分辨率输出。** 纯实色中性偏暖浅灰影棚背景。单侧侧边逆光，从相机右侧、被摄主体身后 45 度射入，无补光，无正面光，无其他光源——富戏剧感的电影影棚 lookbook 打光。

【布局】

**2x2 网格，面板高度不等**：
- 上排：两个较小的面板（各约为整幅画布高度的 1/3）——头/脸特写
- 下排：两个较大的面板（各约为整幅画布高度的 2/3）——全身镜头
- 四个面板之间用细的中性浅灰分隔线
- 四个面板共用同一实色背景与同一打光设置

【4 个面板】

**左上面板 —— 正脸特写（FRONT FACE CLOSE-UP）**：
女劫掠者脸部的紧凑特写，取景从约锁骨线向上到头顶。主体正对相机。侧边逆光勾住脸/发的右缘（即画面的相机左侧），令脸的左侧落入更深的阴影中并带细微的环境光反弹。双眼完好，警觉而专注地凝视相机。**风沙护目镜推上至她的前额/发际线**（标志性装备视觉——这是她的定义特征，飞行员装备处于休息状态）。脸部完全可见——没有头巾遮住鼻子或嘴。

**右上面板 —— 侧脸特写（SIDE FACE CLOSE-UP，正侧剖面）**：
她脸部的紧凑特写，呈纯正侧剖面，取景从锁骨线向上到头顶。主体面朝相机右侧。侧边逆光勾住后脑并使侧脸剖影衬于背景之上。从侧角可见仍推在前额上的风沙护目镜。黑发束成一条高扎的实用马尾，承接逆光。脸部完全可见。

**左下面板 —— 脖子以下全身，正视图（无脸，FULL BODY FROM NECK DOWN, FRONT VIEW, NO FACE）**：
全身镜头，从锁骨线向下到脚，正对相机。**脸/头在此面板顶部被有意裁出画框——只可见脖子、肩膀、躯干、手臂、髋部、腿和脚。** 零脸部出现，以便此面板能在未来任何换脸编辑中保留可用。侧边逆光勾住右肩与身体右侧，勾勒出精瘦运动型的剪影。**脖子上一根皮绳挂着机械通讯器/哨子装置可见**，横跨躯干的飞镖腰带可见。

**右下面板 —— 全身背视图（含后脑，FULL BODY BACK VIEW, INCLUDING BACK OF HEAD）**：
全身镜头，从头到脚，完全背对相机（主体背部朝向相机）。**含后脑，可见一条高扎的实用马尾黑发垂至上背长度**，从背角可见推在头顶的风沙护目镜。可见一支短标枪/投掷矛斜挎于上背。腰带背面可见战利品装饰（小/稀疏）。侧边逆光勾住右肩剪影。

【主体 —— 四个面板中为同一角色】

一名沙漠女劫掠者，普通阶级的部落成员，专精为狩猎队驾驶沙艇。战术单位中最年轻者。

**角色核心**：
约 23-26 岁。**精瘦结实的运动型作战体格——纤细但有力，是惯于在移动沙艇上不断保持平衡、迅速移动的沙漠水手体型。不是壮硕肌肉型，不是模特时尚的瘦，不是丰满，不是青少年般纤弱。体格如一名工作中的飞行员-劫掠者。**

**族群——关键**：中亚 / 突厥 / 粟特丝绸之路族群特征——隋唐边塞沙漠中历史上准确的"杂胡"混部族群。宽而突出的颧骨，深陷的杏仁形眼睛，单眼皮或低眼褶（不是西方圆眼双眼皮），黑色或极深棕色的眼睛，黑发，受沙漠日晒风吹而风化的古铜偏褐肤色，强健的下颌线，挺直或略宽的鼻子（不是西方高加索人那种又细又尖的鼻子），浓密的天然眉毛。

**头发**：黑发紧紧向后束成一条高扎的实用马尾，垂至上背长度，为在风吹的沙艇上驾驶而保持紧束、不挡脸。两鬓可能有几缕被风吹散的发丝。

**战斗痕迹**：鼻梁上一道小的新伤疤，右脸颊上一道已愈合的细疤——一名撞过沙艇并幸存下来的工作中劫掠者的印记。受日晒风吹风化的皮肤质感。

**头戴（关键的标志性装备）**：**青铜沙艇驾驶护目镜**，配粗皮带与双圆镜片（略带深琥珀色调以防晒）——休息时推上至她的前额/发际线，不遮住她的眼睛（为人设图目的而采用"护目镜上推、下勤"的姿态）。机械青铜框，明显是饱经战火的回收拼装结构，可见铆钉与一个小铰链机构，略带铜绿包浆。

**通讯器吊坠**：用一根皮绳挂在她脖子上——一个带机械组件的小青铜哨子/号角装置（沙艇队用以在编队距离间互相打信号）。与部落其余装备相同的 Mecha Crimson Bronze 机改赤铜 #7A6845 + Aged Gold 旧金 #846035 包浆。

**上身**：风化的深色皮质无袖短款背心，剪裁以便肩部自由活动（功能性的驾驶剪裁，不是装饰性的露脐），露出手臂与部分腹部，带战斗疤痕和小型部落纹身标记。前襟紧密系带以在快速操艇时保持牢固贴合。

**下身**：贴身的风化深色皮质马裤/长裤，内大腿与坐部有加强护板（骑手/驾驶员穿着）。腰间多条带小袋的腰带（操艇工具、信号弹、小型机械组件）。

**横跨躯干的装备带**：一条皮质子弹带从右肩斜跨到左髋，挂着 **6-8 支小青铜投掷飞镖/尖刺镖**（她的主要投射武器——又小又快又多，设计用于从移动的沙艇上投掷）。部分飞镖带小尾翼。

**战利品装饰**：稀疏——几片小骨碎、一个小的敌方腰带扣、一颗贸易珠子，用皮绳挂在腰带上。比劫掠者A略多一些，但明显少于副官或首领。

**鞋履**：及膝沙漠皮质驾驶靴，小腿处缠布带，鞋底加强以便在移动甲板上抓地。

【武器】
- **主投射武器**：横跨躯干的子弹带上 6-8 支小青铜投掷飞镖
- **次要近战**：短标枪/投掷矛（约 3 英尺长，青铜矛尖，可投掷或作短柄武器使用），斜挎于上背，在侧面与背面面板中可见
- **副武器**：右腰带处一把小型战斗刀

【色板】
沙漠沙土褐、深焦褐皮革、旧青铜包浆（Mecha Crimson Bronze 机改赤铜 #7A6845 + Aged Gold 旧金 #846035）、布带上的干血红部落点缀、深琥珀护目镜镜片。所有材质均风化、饱经战火、覆满尘土，不洁净、不抛光。

【姿态 - 四个面板中相同】
默认站姿，双脚与肩同宽，重心轻轻落于前脚掌（一个时刻准备反应与保持平衡之人的站姿）。双手停在子弹带的飞镖附近或垂于身侧。带微妙的警觉驾驶员气场——不是攻击性的动作姿态，不是放松的平民，不是诱惑/性感姿态，不是可爱/俏皮姿态。

【关键一致性】
四个面板中为同一个体角色——相同的脸部结构（中亚/突厥/粟特特征），相同的头发（高扎紧马尾），相同的风沙护目镜位置（推上至前额），相同的服饰物件，相同的子弹带、相同的飞镖数量，相同的通讯器吊坠，相同的稀疏战利品装饰。四个面板共用同一影棚背景、同一单侧侧边逆光设置。

【打光 - 关键】
**单侧侧边逆光，从相机右侧、被摄主体身后 45 度射入。无补光。无正面光。无环境光。无其他光源。** 每个面板中，强烈的戏剧性边缘光勾住主体剪影的右侧，令主体左侧落入深处的衰减阴影并带细微的环境光反弹。

【风格】
超写实摄影风格的角色设计渲染——拍摄手法如同在电影制作影棚里为电影 lookbook 参考板拍摄一套高端的实拍戏服。不是插画，不是明显的 3D CGI 渲染。

**中亚丝绸之路族群准确性的参考美学**：
- **《长安十二时辰》（The Longest Day in Chang'an, 2019）西域狼卫** —— 隋唐丝绸之路边塞沙漠族群的人种志锚定
- **《Mongol: The Rise of Genghis Khan》（蒙古王，2007）** —— 中亚久经沙场的战士特征（注：此处为女性人种志参考）
- **《Monster Hunter Wilds》（怪物猎人：荒野）沙漠猎人美学** —— 用于部落铠甲/风化皮革的 DNA

叠加轻度 Kodak Vision3 35mm 胶片颗粒质感。

【质量】
最高分辨率输出。8k 极干净渲染。锐利干净的边缘。每一根皮带、每一支飞镖、护目镜框上每一颗铆钉都清晰可辨。

【面板标签】
每个面板下方为小而干净的等宽无衬线黑色文字：
- 左上："FRONT FACE"
- 右上："SIDE FACE"
- 左下："BODY FRONT (NO FACE)"
- 右下："BACK"

画布顶部居中标题（小而干净的黑色无衬线字）："SAND HUNTER COMMON RAIDER B · SKIMMER — DEFAULT FORM CHARACTER SHEET"

【约束】
**族群准确性（关键）**：不是纯高加索欧洲脸，不是金发或红发或浅棕发，不是蓝色或绿色或灰色或淡褐色眼睛，不是苍白白皙的北欧肤色，不是又细又尖的纤巧西方鼻，不是西方圆眼双眼皮，不是纯汉族脸，不是现代 K-pop 或中国剧女演员美学，不是可爱卡哇伊动漫脸。必须是中亚突厥/粟特丝绸之路混血族群。

**体型、年龄与取景**：不是壮硕肌肉型，不是模特时尚的瘦，不是性感化的丰满身材，不是青少年般纤弱，不是青少年女学生形象（必须是 23-26 岁、经历过多次撞艇的工作中飞行员-劫掠者），不是诱惑姿态或取景，不是可爱/俏皮表情，不是肚皮舞者美学，不是奇幻游戏女战士的锁甲比基尼美学，不是 Q 版动漫美学。

**装备区分**：不是义肢替换（无机械肢，仅可穿戴装备），不是大量战利品装饰，护目镜必须推上至前额而非遮在眼睛上（人设图的下勤站姿），不是首领级的野蛮主义战利品堆叠，不是副官级的精致编织珠串，不是与劫掠者A相同的装备（无套索绞盘，无背挂钩索发射器）。

**美学**：不是精密赛博机械，不是现代，不是洁净（仅限饱经战火的回收青铜），不是中世纪欧洲，不是明清帝制，不是武士，不是赛博朋克霓虹，不是蒸汽朋克维多利亚，不是明显的 CGI 3D 渲染，不是卡通，不是动漫，不是色彩饱和的调色板。

**布局与打光**：不是 1:1 正方比例（必须是竖版 9:16），不是等大的 4 面板（上排较小，下排较大），左下面板不露脸（脸严格在面板顶部裁出），脸部特写面板中脸不被护目镜遮住（护目镜必须上推至前额，眼睛不被遮挡），不是彩色背景（仅限纯中性偏暖浅灰），不是柔和均匀的中性光（必须仅为单侧边缘逆光），不是正面光，不是补光，不要除四个面板标签与标题以外的任何文字。

=== END ===

【实操注意】
1. 跑出来重点检查：
   - **族群准确**——Central Asian/Turkic/Sogdian。欧美白人脸或纯汉人脸都失败
   - **9:16+4格不等大**+最高分辨率
   - **左下panel不能露脸**
   - **风沙护目镜PUSHED UP在额头不是覆盖在眼睛上**——这是face close-up panel的关键（眼睛要露出来给五官参考替换的灵活性）
   - **高马尾**——和女副手的长辫区分+和A钩索手的短发区分
   - **bandolier多飞镖**——signature equipment+和A钩索手装备区分
   - **不是teen少女不是belly dancer**——working pilot raider
   - **战利品sparse but slightly more than A**——3-4个+和A的2-3形成微差
```

## ② 故事板 / 分镜草图（铅笔稿·九宫格 3x3·竖排）　（共 10 条）

### 1（18003 字）

```
A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, in the style of professional film production storyboards. Total image aspect ratio strictly 21:9.

Layout: 3 columns x 2 rows grid on aged paper background. Each of the 6 cells contains one element. Top row (left to right): Panel 1, Panel 2, Panel 3. Bottom row (left to right): Panel 4, Panel 5, Shooting Plan diagram. Each of Panels 1-5 is strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. The Shooting Plan cell in the bottom-right is a top-down bird's eye view of the desert battlefield with camera positions and 180-degree axis line marked.

Beneath each storyboard panel inner frame is a hand-lettered caption strip showing panel number, lens specification, shot type, and brief SFX/action notes in serif block letters slightly uneven.

The drawing technique is loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective and clear cinematic composition. Sketches are deliberately gestural but every spatial relationship and prop detail is clearly readable. Selective red colored pencil arrows and emphasis marks indicate movement direction, force vectors, and key dramatic beats.

LIGHTING NOTE FOR ALL PANELS: late afternoon desert golden hour, low sun key light from upper-left 30 degrees. Dense cross-hatching dominates most surfaces representing the shadow base of the warm desert atmosphere. Selective bright untouched paper zones represent the directional sun-key highlights on metal armor surfaces and rim-lit silhouettes. Strong tonal contrast throughout — deep blacks in shadow zones, bright untouched paper for highlights, NOT evenly lit.

Style references: Wes Anderson production storyboards, traditional Hollywood film storyboard aesthetic, animator's pre-visualization sketches, Mad Max Fury Road action sequence boards, Spider-Man train-stop sequence storyboards.

Drawing rules:
- Pencil graphite on warm off-white aged paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use
- Black ink for panel borders and hand-lettered captions
- Red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera position triangles, force vectors, and 180-degree axis line
- Cross-hatching for shading; bright highlight zones use untouched paper
- Hand-lettered captions in serif-style block letters, slightly uneven

DIRECTIONAL AXIS RULE: the protagonist ship is moving deep into the battlefield (forward vector toward one consistent direction). Liu Hui stands as the anchor at the rope's near end, pulled toward the ship's direction by rope tension. Two remaining sand skimmers are dragged behind the ship between Liu Hui and the ship — connected to the ship's mechanical legs by their own ropes. The female hunter's third skimmer has been knocked off the chain in the prior segment — it now drifts off-axis on one side, separated from the main battlefield. The action axis runs from Liu Hui through the ship; cameras stay on one consistent side of this axis throughout the sequence to maintain spatial continuity.

Title at top center of entire image, hand-lettered in confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9B — THE SHIP COMES DOWN"

Below title, hand-lettered subtitle in smaller letters: "MULTI-SHOT HARD CUT / 3 SHOTS / 5 STORYBOARD PANELS + SHOOTING PLAN / SCALE CONTRAST CLIMAX"

PANEL 1 (top-left cell) — SHOT 1: EXTREME CLOSE-UP OF THE HAND GRIPPING THE ROPE:
Scene composition: extreme close-up framing dominated by Liu Hui's massive bronze mechanical hand gripping the rope at the center of frame. The hand occupies roughly 60% of the frame area — every detail of the armor plating, weathered patina, etched ancient gold symbols, and the tension in the knuckles is rendered with confident pencil shading. The rope passes through the hand horizontally across the frame, stretched taut and straight — visible fibers in the rope show micro-trembling under extreme tension (rendered as short fine pencil lines along the rope's length). The lower-left portion of frame shows the bottom half of Liu Hui's face — chin and lower jaw to the bridge of the nose visible — jaw muscles clenched, teeth gritted, lips pressed tight. The face is partially in shadow with cross-hatching, the highlighted edge of the jaw rim-lit by the golden hour sun from upper-left. Background behind the hand and face: completely defocused with light pencil hatching suggesting motion blur of the desert flowing past — the entire frame conveys that everything is still in motion despite the close-up framing.

Camera annotation: red pencil emphasis marks on the trembling rope fibers, labeled "rope fibers micro-tremble." Red pencil indicator inside the panel showing camera handheld micro-shake.

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — EXTREME CLOSE-UP HAND ON ROPE / 85mm prime / handheld with micro-shake, no large camera movement / SFX: rope tensioning humming building rapidly, low contained throat groan from Liu Hui, jaw-clenching mechanical creak"

**HARD CUT.**

PANEL 2 (top-middle cell) — SHOT 2: SIDE 90° WIDE — THE SCALE CONTRAST CLIMAX:
Scene composition: this is the dramatic anchor of the entire storyboard — the camera is at a wide side 90° angle to the action axis. Liu Hui occupies the lower-left quadrant of the frame in his anchored-resistance pose, captured in full side profile: both arms extended forward gripping the rope, both heels dragging in the sand creating two visible deep parallel furrows that extend from beyond the lower frame edge into the middle distance, body leaning backward at a slight angle resisting the rope's pull, head lowered, jaw clenched, the cyan core at chest and shoulders glowing strongly. Liu Hui is rendered at small-to-medium scale relative to the frame — his height fills approximately the lower 1/4 to 1/5 of frame height.

The protagonist ship occupies the right two-thirds of the frame in the middle-to-deep distance — drawn with its complete rectangular bronze-mechanized side profile visible, the wooden deck on top, and all 9 mechanical legs underneath in clear pencil detail. The ship's overall height in frame is approximately 4 to 5 times the height of Liu Hui — this scale contrast is the central dramatic anchor of the entire storyboard. Three of the ship's 9 mechanical legs (the ones strained by the rope tension) are visibly pulled back at extreme angles toward the lower-left of frame, with armor segments visibly deformed under stress. The remaining 6 legs are still galloping forward (toward the upper-right of frame, deeper into the distance) but in a faltering broken rhythm — some legs caught mid-stride awkwardly, others planted at off-balance angles. The ship's body is tilted slightly backward overall, showing the deceleration moment. The ship's stern sand-dust plume is reduced — thinner and more broken than in earlier segments.

Between Liu Hui and the ship in the middle distance: two smaller sand skimmers, each connected by their own rope to one of the ship's legs, being dragged in the chain — both visibly struggling in the deceleration. Far right edge of frame, off-axis from the main chain: the female hunter's third skimmer drifting off to the side at a clearly different angle from the main action, separated from the chain — drawn smaller and isolated. The rope from Liu Hui's hands extends in a single taut diagonal line across the entire frame from lower-left to upper-right, connecting to one of the ship's strained mechanical legs.

Background sky: dense cross-hatching for golden hour atmosphere, the entire scene rim-lit from the upper-left golden sun creating strong silhouetting on the ship.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow showing camera position at side 90° angle to the action axis, labeled "side wide / 90° / scale anchor." Red pencil force-vector arrows along the rope showing tension force between Liu Hui and the ship. A red pencil scale-reference bracket on the left edge of frame indicating Liu Hui's height vs the ship's height — clearly showing the 1:4 to 1:5 ratio.

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — SIDE 90° WIDE — SCALE CONTRAST / 24mm wide / lateral tracking + handheld / SFX: ship's 9-leg crashes becoming irregular and slowing, mechanical leg-strain metallic groans, rope humming at climax, background desert wind"

**HARD CUT.**

PANEL 3 (top-right cell) — SHOT 3 OPENING: THE SHIP STARTS TO LOSE BALANCE:
Scene composition: camera at approximately 3/4 angle behind Liu Hui, looking past him into the deeper space where the ship is. Liu Hui occupies the foreground left area as a partial silhouette (back, shoulders, the bronze spiral horns rising into the upper frame, the cyan core glowing softly through the silhouette from the front side) — he is still in the anchored pose but the rope tension is at its peak, his body bracing. The protagonist ship dominates the middle and right area of frame — it is in the moment of beginning to lose balance: the ship's body is now tilted forward at a noticeable angle (front of ship dipping toward the sand), three of the mechanical legs (the rope-strained ones) are visibly buckling at the knee joints with the buckling just beginning, the other 6 legs are caught in mid-collapse motion — some legs unable to step properly, some planted at clearly off-balance angles. The ship is in motion but the forward motion is collapsing into downward motion. The two remaining sand skimmers in the middle distance are visibly thrown off-balance by the ship's sudden deceleration — being whipped sideways by the rope chain. The female hunter's skimmer drifts further off-axis at the edge of frame.

Background sky: dense cross-hatching for golden hour, atmospheric haze in the deep distance.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow showing camera position 3/4 behind Liu Hui, labeled "3/4 back / collapse opening." Red pencil emphasis marks on the buckling knee joints of the three strained mechanical legs.

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 3 PART 1 / PANEL 3 — 3/4 BACK — SHIP BEGINS COLLAPSE / 28mm wide / handheld with subtle seismic vibration beginning / SFX: ship's leg-galloping rhythm breaking down, knee-joint metal groan, rope tension at maximum"

PANEL 4 (bottom-left cell) — SHOT 3 MIDDLE: BOW PLOUGHS INTO SAND:
Scene composition: same 3/4 back camera position as Panel 3, this is the next critical moment in the same continuous shot. Liu Hui's silhouette still occupies the foreground left area (back, shoulders, bronze spiral horns, cyan core glow) — the rope in his hands is going slack from peak tension, hanging in a curving slack line because the ship has lost its forward momentum. The protagonist ship dominates the middle and right area of frame: the bow has now ploughed forward into the sand at a tilted-forward angle of roughly 25 degrees, the front of the ship buried into the desert surface, three of the mechanical legs have fully buckled at the knee joints with armor segments splayed outward in awkward kneeling angles, the other 6 legs caught mid-collapse — some unable to compensate, some with feet not yet landed. The bow's impact point erupts in an enormous massive sand-and-dust plume that explodes upward and outward — the plume is drawn as dense cross-hatched cloud filling the bottom third of frame and partially obscuring the ship's lower body, with sand particles radiating outward in radial bursts as short pencil dot streaks. The two remaining sand skimmers are visible at the edges of the chaos — one tipped sideways, one being dragged into the dust cloud. The female hunter's skimmer is at the far right edge of frame, further off-axis now, having continued drifting during the collapse.

Background sky: dense cross-hatching for golden hour, the upper portion shows the massive dust plume rising into the sky above the impact point.

Camera annotation: red pencil emphasis marks at the bow-impact point with radial burst lines indicating the explosion. Red pencil arrow on the slack rope in Liu Hui's hands showing the tension release. Red pencil indicator showing camera seismic shake at peak.

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 3 PART 2 / PANEL 4 — 3/4 BACK — BOW PLOUGH IMPACT / 28mm wide / handheld with seismic shake at peak / SFX: deafening THOOM of bow impact, massive sustained sand-explosion roar, mechanical leg-collapse buckling clanks, seismic ground shake"

PANEL 5 (bottom-middle cell) — SHOT 3 END: LIU HUI STANDS, SHIP HAS FALLEN:
Scene composition: same 3/4 back camera position as Panel 3 and 4, this is the end of the same continuous shot. Liu Hui's silhouette in the foreground left area now stands firm and still — back, shoulders, bronze spiral horns rising into upper frame, the cyan core has settled from peak power surge down to a calm baseline glow. The rope in his hands has gone fully slack and is partially trailing on the ground. Liu Hui's body is upright in the standing-firm pose, slightly elevated chin as he watches the result. The protagonist ship in the middle-to-right area of frame has come to rest in its final collapsed position: the bow deeply buried in the sand, the body tilted forward at roughly 25 degrees, three of the mechanical legs buckled in kneeling positions, the other 6 legs splayed in mid-collapse poses at awkward angles, the ship is now fully stationary. The massive sand-dust plume from the impact is still rising into the upper portion of frame but starting to settle and thin — particles slowly drifting downward at the edges of the plume. The two remaining sand skimmers have come to rest in their disrupted positions. The female hunter's skimmer is far off-axis at the edge of frame.

Background sky: dense cross-hatching for golden hour, the dust plume gradually fading into the upper atmosphere.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow indicating camera has settled from seismic shake to stable handheld. Red pencil indicator on Liu Hui's settled posture, labeled "standing firm / watches the result."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 3 PART 3 / PANEL 5 — 3/4 BACK — FINAL STATE / 28mm wide / handheld settling to stable / SFX: dust plume slowly settling, Liu Hui's single heavy exhale, distant residual creaking of broken ship metal"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right cell) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW:
Drawn as a clean top-down schematic diagram on the aged paper. Layout uses the entire cell width effectively. Center-upper of the diagram: the protagonist ship drawn as a rectangular schematic silhouette with nine small leg-marks underneath, marked with a red pencil directional arrow showing its forward vector pointing toward the upper edge of the diagram. Between the ship and the foreground area below: two small triangular schematic outlines representing the two remaining sand skimmers, each connected by a thin line (rope) to one of the ship's leg-marks. Foreground-lower-left of the diagram: Liu Hui drawn as a small figure-outline with the rope extending from his hands as a thin straight line up to one of the ship's leg-marks (the rope passes alongside the two intermediate skimmers — clearly showing the rope is a single continuous line from Liu Hui to the ship). Far-right side of the diagram (off the main action chain): the female hunter's third skimmer drawn drifting off-axis at an angle, with a small dashed line showing its drift trajectory away from the chain — labeled "OFF-CHAIN / DRIFTING."

Three red pencil triangles around the action area marking the camera positions for each of the 3 shots: Shot 1 (extreme close-up) positioned very close to Liu Hui's hand, Shot 2 (side wide) positioned at side 90° to the action axis, Shot 3 (3/4 back covering Panels 3+4+5) positioned 3/4 behind Liu Hui — each red triangle is labeled with the shot number. A dashed red pencil straight line running across the diagram from Liu Hui through the ship — this is the 180-degree axis line. Labels in hand-lettered serif block letters: "180° AXIS LINE", "S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS", "LIU HUI ANCHOR", "PROTAGONIST SHIP HEADING", "2 SKIMMERS IN CHAIN", "FEMALE HUNTER'S SKIMMER OFF-CHAIN".

Caption strip below the shooting plan cell: "SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED / 3 hard-cut shot positions / S2 scale contrast is the dramatic anchor / S3 covers Panels 3+4+5 as one continuous take from one camera"

PRODUCTION NOTES at bottom edge of overall image (hand-lettered serif block letters along the bottom margin of the aged paper, outside the panel grid): "WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 9B — THE SHIP COMES DOWN / multi-shot hard cut sequence — NOT a continuous orbit one-take / continuity: prior segment 9A closes on Liu Hui having seized the rope and beginning to resist / this segment opens on the extreme close-up of the hand and rope tension moment / next segment 10 opens with the aftermath of the fallen ship"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting style, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red colored pencil for arrows and annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO AI-style smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour desert high-contrast lighting in all panels — dense cross-hatch shadows + selective bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each storyboard panel's inner frame strictly 16:9 aspect ratio
- Total image strictly 21:9 aspect ratio
- All hand-lettered text in confident serif block letters with slight unevenness — readable but not machine-printed
- Aged off-white paper texture throughout with subtle edge wear
- Maintain consistent fusion-form chieftain character appearance across all panels — same character with bronze mechanical armor, shoulder spiral horns visible in P2/P3/P4/P5, cyan chest core visible across all panels, partial face only in P1
- The scale contrast in PANEL 2 — Liu Hui's height vs ship's height at approximately 1:4 to 1:5 — must be visually unambiguous and is the dramatic anchor of the entire storyboard
- Panels 3, 4, 5 must show clearly that the camera position is unchanged (same 3/4 back angle) — they are three temporal moments within the same continuous Shot 3, not three different camera setups
```

**🀄 中文版**

```
一张手绘铅笔素描故事板，画在泛旧的米白色纸张上，采用专业电影制作故事板的风格。整图画面比例严格为 21:9。

布局：在泛旧纸张背景上呈 3 列 x 2 行的网格。6 个单元格各包含一个元素。上排（从左到右）：面板 1、面板 2、面板 3。下排（从左到右）：面板 4、面板 5、拍摄方案示意图。面板 1 至 5 每一格都严格为 16:9 横向风景格式，带粗重的手绘黑墨边框。右下角的拍摄方案单元格是沙漠战场的俯视鸟瞰图，标注了机位与 180 度轴线。

每个故事板面板内框下方都有一条手写说明字条，以略微参差的衬线方块字标出面板编号、镜头规格、景别以及简短的音效/动作注记。

绘画技法为松散的石墨铅笔，配以自信的建筑透视和清晰的电影感构图。素描刻意带有手势感，但每一处空间关系与道具细节都清晰可读。选择性使用的红色彩铅箭头与强调标记指示运动方向、力的矢量以及关键的戏剧节拍。

所有面板的光照说明：午后晚段沙漠黄金时刻，低角度太阳主光从左上方 30 度射入。密集的交叉排线主导大多数表面，表现温暖沙漠氛围中的阴影基底。选择性保留的明亮空白纸面区域表现金属铠甲表面上具方向性的太阳主光高光以及边缘逆光勾勒的剪影。整体保持强烈的影调对比——阴影区是深黑，高光处用明亮的空白纸面，并非均匀打光。

风格参考：Wes Anderson（韦斯·安德森）制作故事板、传统好莱坞电影故事板美学、动画师的预演素描、《Mad Max: Fury Road》（疯狂的麦克斯：狂暴之路）动作段落分镜板、《Spider-Man》（蜘蛛侠）列车停靠段落故事板。

绘制规则：
- 石墨铅笔，画在带轻微边缘磨损与淡淡污渍的暖米白色泛旧纸张背景上，暗示繁重的制作使用痕迹
- 黑墨用于面板边框与手写说明字幕
- 红色彩铅仅用于箭头、强调标记、机位三角、力的矢量以及 180 度轴线
- 交叉排线用于阴影；明亮高光区使用空白纸面
- 手写说明采用衬线风格方块字，略微参差

方向轴线规则：主角飞船正深入战场（前进矢量朝向一个固定方向）。刘回作为锚点站在绳索的近端，被绳索张力拉向飞船的方向。其余两艘沙艇被拖在飞船后方，位于刘回与飞船之间——它们各自用自己的绳索连接到飞船的机械腿上。女猎手的第三艘沙艇在上一段中已被甩离链条——它此刻漂离轴线，偏向一侧，与主战场分离。动作轴线从刘回贯穿到飞船；整段过程中机位始终保持在这条轴线的同一侧，以维持空间连贯性。

整图正上方居中处，以自信的方块衬线手写体标题："WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9B — THE SHIP COMES DOWN"

标题下方，以较小字体的手写副标题："MULTI-SHOT HARD CUT / 3 SHOTS / 5 STORYBOARD PANELS + SHOOTING PLAN / SCALE CONTRAST CLIMAX"

面板 1（左上单元格）—— 镜头 1：握住绳索的手部极特写：
画面构图：极特写取景，由刘回画面中央那只巨大的青铜机械手握住绳索的画面主导。这只手占据约 60% 的画面面积——铠甲护板的每一处细节、风化的包浆、刻蚀的古代金色符号以及指关节中的张力，都用自信的铅笔阴影渲染出来。绳索水平穿过手部横贯画面，绷得笔直紧绷——可见绳索中的纤维在极度张力下微微颤动（沿绳索长度方向以短细铅笔线表现）。画面左下部分露出刘回脸部的下半截——从下巴和下颌到鼻梁可见——下颌肌肉紧绷，咬牙切齿，双唇紧抿。脸部部分处于阴影中并施以交叉排线，下颌的高光边缘被左上方黄金时刻的太阳逆光勾勒。手部与脸部背后的背景：完全失焦，以淡淡的铅笔排线暗示沙漠从旁掠过的运动模糊——整幅画面传达出，尽管是特写取景，一切仍在运动之中。

机位注记：在颤动的绳索纤维上以红色彩铅作强调标记，标注 "rope fibers micro-tremble"。面板内有红色彩铅指示符，表示相机手持微抖。

面板下方说明字条："SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — EXTREME CLOSE-UP HAND ON ROPE / 85mm prime / handheld with micro-shake, no large camera movement / SFX: rope tensioning humming building rapidly, low contained throat groan from Liu Hui, jaw-clenching mechanical creak"

硬切。

面板 2（中上单元格）—— 镜头 2：侧 90° 广角——尺度对比的高潮：
画面构图：这是整张故事板的戏剧锚点——相机位于与动作轴线成侧向 90° 的广角位置。刘回处于画面左下象限，呈他锚定抵抗的姿态，以完整的侧面剖影呈现：双臂前伸抓住绳索，双脚脚跟在沙中拖出两道清晰可见的深平行沟壑，从下画框边缘之外一直延伸到中景，身体略向后倾抵抗绳索的拉力，头部低垂，下颌紧咬，胸部与肩部的青色核心强烈发光。刘回相对画面以中小尺度渲染——他的身高约填满画面高度的下 1/4 到 1/5。

主角飞船占据画面右侧三分之二，位于中景至深景——绘出其完整的矩形青铜机械化侧面剖影，顶部的木质甲板，以及下方全部 9 条机械腿，皆以清晰的铅笔细节呈现。飞船在画面中的整体高度约为刘回身高的 4 到 5 倍——这一尺度对比是整张故事板的中心戏剧锚点。飞船 9 条机械腿中有 3 条（被绳索张力拉扯的那几条）明显被向画面左下方以极端角度拉回，铠甲段在应力下明显变形。其余 6 条腿仍在向前奔驰（朝画面右上方、更深的远处），但节奏踉跄破碎——有的腿尴尬地卡在迈步中途，有的以失衡的角度落地。飞船船体整体略向后倾，呈现减速的瞬间。飞船船尾的沙尘羽流减弱——比早前段落更稀薄、更破碎。

刘回与飞船之间的中景：两艘较小的沙艇，各自用自己的绳索连到飞船的一条腿上，被拖在链条中——两者都在减速中明显挣扎。画面最右边缘、偏离主链条之外：女猎手的第三艘沙艇以明显不同于主动作的角度漂向一侧，与链条分离——画得更小且孤立。从刘回手中伸出的绳索以一条紧绷的对角线横贯整个画面，从左下到右上，连到飞船一条受力的机械腿上。

背景天空：黄金时刻氛围用密集交叉排线，整个场景被左上方的黄金太阳逆光勾勒，在飞船上形成强烈的剪影效果。

机位注记：红色彩铅箭头标出相机位于与动作轴线成侧 90° 的位置，标注 "side wide / 90° / scale anchor"。沿绳索方向有红色彩铅力矢量箭头，表示刘回与飞船之间的张力。画面左边缘有一个红色彩铅尺度参照括号，标示刘回身高与飞船身高的对比——清晰显示 1:4 到 1:5 的比例。

面板下方说明字条："SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — SIDE 90° WIDE — SCALE CONTRAST / 24mm wide / lateral tracking + handheld / SFX: ship's 9-leg crashes becoming irregular and slowing, mechanical leg-strain metallic groans, rope humming at climax, background desert wind"

硬切。

面板 3（右上单元格）—— 镜头 3 开端：飞船开始失去平衡：
画面构图：相机位于刘回身后约 3/4 的角度，越过他望向飞船所在的更深空间。刘回占据前景左侧区域，呈部分剪影（背部、肩膀、升入上画框的青铜螺旋角、从正面一侧透过剪影柔和发光的青色核心）——他仍处于锚定姿态，但绳索张力达到顶点，身体在支撑。主角飞船主导画面的中部与右侧区域——它正处于开始失去平衡的瞬间：飞船船体此刻以明显的角度向前倾（船头向沙地下沉），3 条机械腿（被绳索拉扯的那几条）在膝关节处明显开始屈曲，屈曲刚刚开始，其余 6 条腿陷于半坍塌的动态中——有的腿无法正常迈步，有的以明显失衡的角度落地。飞船在运动，但前进的运动正坍缩为向下的运动。中景中其余两艘沙艇被飞船的骤然减速明显甩得失去平衡——被绳索链条横向甩动。女猎手的沙艇在画框边缘进一步漂离轴线。

背景天空：黄金时刻用密集交叉排线，深远处有大气霾。

机位注记：红色彩铅箭头标出相机位于刘回身后 3/4 的位置，标注 "3/4 back / collapse opening"。在那 3 条受力机械腿屈曲的膝关节上以红色彩铅作强调标记。

面板下方说明字条："SHOT 3 PART 1 / PANEL 3 — 3/4 BACK — SHIP BEGINS COLLAPSE / 28mm wide / handheld with subtle seismic vibration beginning / SFX: ship's leg-galloping rhythm breaking down, knee-joint metal groan, rope tension at maximum"

面板 4（左下单元格）—— 镜头 3 中段：船头犁入沙中：
画面构图：与面板 3 相同的 3/4 身后机位，这是同一连续镜头中的下一个关键瞬间。刘回的剪影仍占据前景左侧区域（背部、肩膀、青铜螺旋角、青色核心辉光）——他手中的绳索从峰值张力中松弛下来，因飞船已失去前冲动量而呈下垂的弧线悬着。主角飞船主导画面的中部与右侧区域：船头此刻以约 25 度的前倾角犁入沙中，船的前部埋进沙漠表面，3 条机械腿已在膝关节处完全屈曲，铠甲段以尴尬的跪姿角度向外摊开，其余 6 条腿陷于半坍塌中——有的无法补偿平衡，有的脚尚未落地。船头的撞击点喷出一道巨大的沙尘羽流，向上向外爆开——羽流绘为密集交叉排线的尘云，填满画面下三分之一并部分遮挡飞船下半身，沙粒以放射状爆裂向外辐射，呈短铅笔点状条痕。其余两艘沙艇在混乱边缘可见——一艘侧翻，一艘被拖入尘云。女猎手的沙艇在画面最右边缘，此刻进一步偏离轴线，在坍塌过程中持续漂移。

背景天空：黄金时刻用密集交叉排线，上部显示巨大的尘羽在撞击点上方升入天空。

机位注记：在船头撞击点处以红色彩铅作强调标记，配放射状爆裂线表示爆炸。刘回手中松弛的绳索上以红色彩铅箭头表示张力释放。红色彩铅指示符表示相机在峰值处的地震式抖动。

面板下方说明字条："SHOT 3 PART 2 / PANEL 4 — 3/4 BACK — BOW PLOUGH IMPACT / 28mm wide / handheld with seismic shake at peak / SFX: deafening THOOM of bow impact, massive sustained sand-explosion roar, mechanical leg-collapse buckling clanks, seismic ground shake"

面板 5（中下单元格）—— 镜头 3 结尾：刘回站立，飞船已倒下：
画面构图：与面板 3 和 4 相同的 3/4 身后机位，这是同一连续镜头的结尾。前景左侧区域的刘回剪影此刻稳稳站立、静止不动——背部、肩膀、升入上画框的青铜螺旋角，青色核心已从峰值能量涌动平息至平静的基准辉光。他手中的绳索已完全松弛，部分拖在地上。刘回身体直立呈稳稳站立的姿态，下巴略微抬起注视结果。画面中部至右侧的主角飞船已在最终坍塌位置停驻：船头深埋沙中，船体以约 25 度前倾，3 条机械腿屈曲成跪姿，其余 6 条腿以尴尬角度摊开成半坍塌姿态，飞船此刻已完全静止。撞击产生的巨大沙尘羽流仍升入画面上部，但开始平息变薄——羽流边缘的颗粒缓慢向下飘落。其余两艘沙艇已在被打乱的位置上停驻。女猎手的沙艇在画框边缘远远偏离轴线。

背景天空：黄金时刻用密集交叉排线，尘羽逐渐消融入上层大气。

机位注记：红色彩铅箭头标示相机已从地震式抖动平稳为稳定的手持。红色彩铅指示符标在刘回平稳的姿态上，标注 "standing firm / watches the result"。

面板下方说明字条："SHOT 3 PART 3 / PANEL 5 — 3/4 BACK — FINAL STATE / 28mm wide / handheld settling to stable / SFX: dust plume slowly settling, Liu Hui's single heavy exhale, distant residual creaking of broken ship metal"

拍摄方案（右下单元格）—— 俯视鸟瞰总览：
画在泛旧纸张上，绘为一张干净的俯视示意图。布局有效利用整个单元格宽度。示意图中上部：主角飞船绘为矩形示意剪影，下方有九个小腿标记，并以红色彩铅方向箭头标出其前进矢量指向示意图的上边缘。飞船与下方前景区域之间：两个小三角形示意轮廓代表其余两艘沙艇，各以一条细线（绳索）连到飞船的一个腿标记上。示意图前景左下方：刘回绘为一个小人形轮廓，绳索从他手中作为一条细直线伸出，向上连到飞船的一个腿标记（绳索从两艘中间沙艇旁经过——清晰表明绳索是从刘回到飞船的一条连续单线）。示意图最右侧（脱离主动作链条）：女猎手的第三艘沙艇绘为以一定角度漂离轴线，并以一条小虚线表示其偏离链条的漂移轨迹——标注 "OFF-CHAIN / DRIFTING"。

动作区域周围有三个红色彩铅三角，标出 3 个镜头各自的机位：镜头 1（极特写）紧贴刘回的手部，镜头 2（侧广角）位于与动作轴线成侧 90° 的位置，镜头 3（3/4 身后，涵盖面板 3+4+5）位于刘回身后 3/4 处——每个红三角都标有镜头编号。一条红色彩铅虚直线横贯示意图，从刘回穿过飞船——这是 180 度轴线。手写衬线方块字标注："180° AXIS LINE"、"S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS"、"LIU HUI ANCHOR"、"PROTAGONIST SHIP HEADING"、"2 SKIMMERS IN CHAIN"、"FEMALE HUNTER'S SKIMMER OFF-CHAIN"。

拍摄方案单元格下方说明字条："SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED / 3 hard-cut shot positions / S2 scale contrast is the dramatic anchor / S3 covers Panels 3+4+5 as one continuous take from one camera"

整图底边的制作注记（以手写衬线方块字沿泛旧纸张底部边距排列，位于面板网格之外）："WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 9B — THE SHIP COMES DOWN / multi-shot hard cut sequence — NOT a continuous orbit one-take / continuity: prior segment 9A closes on Liu Hui having seized the rope and beginning to resist / this segment opens on the extreme close-up of the hand and rope tension moment / next segment 10 opens with the aftermath of the fallen ship"

硬性约束：
- 不要数字绘画风格，不要 3D 渲染，除红色彩铅用于箭头与注记外不要任何色彩叠加
- 不要摄影元素，不要 AI 风格的平滑渐变
- 所有面板都用强烈的黄金时刻沙漠高对比光照——密集交叉排线阴影 + 选择性保留的明亮空白纸面高光，并非均匀打光
- 每个故事板面板的内框严格为 16:9 画面比例
- 整图严格为 21:9 画面比例
- 所有手写文字采用自信、略带参差的衬线方块字——可读但非机印
- 全图采用泛旧米白纸张质感，带细微边缘磨损
- 在所有面板中保持一致的融合形态首领角色外观——同一角色，配青铜机械铠甲，在 P2/P3/P4/P5 中可见肩部螺旋角，所有面板中可见青色胸部核心，仅在 P1 中露出部分脸部
- 面板 2 中的尺度对比——刘回身高与飞船身高约为 1:4 到 1:5——必须在视觉上毫不含糊，是整张故事板的戏剧锚点
- 面板 3、4、5 必须清楚显示机位未变（同一 3/4 身后角度）——它们是同一连续镜头 3 中的三个时间瞬间，而非三个不同的机位设置
```

### 2（9666 字）

```
=== GPT-Image-2 Prompt - 万物生第1集 副手登场段 抽象故事板 1x5竖排版 v2 ===

【用途】副手登场段的15秒分镜图+5个panel故事板+1x5竖排布局+每panel保持16:9横构图+确保每格构图与最终视频画面比例一致

【prompt】

A 5-panel film storyboard laid out as a 5-row 1-column vertical grid. Overall canvas aspect ratio is 16:45 vertical (extremely tall portrait orientation). Each individual panel within the grid is a 16:9 horizontal landscape rectangle. The 5 panels are stacked vertically from top to bottom. Each panel is a rectangular pencil-and-marker rough sketch with a white margin border and a small information strip underneath each panel.

Hand-drawn animation school storyboard aesthetic with pencil line-work, grey marker shading, occasional red-pencil arrow annotations indicating camera movement or character motion arc, off-white paper texture background. Deliberately rough and abstract sketch quality - NOT a finished illustration - all human figures rendered as minimal stick-figure silhouettes or simple body shapes with no facial features no clothing details no accessories visible, all objects represented as basic geometric shapes (boxes for crates, triangles for sails, simple curved lines for ship hull). The purpose is to communicate composition and spatial relationships only, not visual details.

[PANEL 1 顶部 - WIDE ESTABLISHING AERIAL]
Label underneath: "PANEL 1 · WIDE AERIAL · stratosphere · static · 2s · SFX: bird scream"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: extreme wide aerial shot showing high-altitude clouds in upper portion of frame and vast desert landscape in lower portion. The desert horizon line sits at the bottom third of the frame. A tiny silhouette of a winged mechanical bird-figure appears as a small dark shape descending from upper-center of frame, drawn as a simple rough pencil silhouette of spread-wing bird shape no larger than a fingernail in the panel. A red-pencil arrow points downward from the bird-figure indicating direction of dive. The desert below shows a few faint horizontal lines suggesting dunes and at the very bottom-right of the desert area a tiny barely-visible rectangular shape with a few small dot-shapes around it represents the protagonist ship and pursuing sand-skiffs as distant tiny silhouettes. Cloud layer is rendered as soft grey marker smudges in upper portion of frame.

[PANEL 2 第二格 - OTS DIVE TRACKING]
Label underneath: "PANEL 2 · OTS DIVE / 50mm / 3s / SFX: wind tear, war cry"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: over-the-shoulder shot from behind a diving figure. In the immediate foreground occupying left and right edges of the frame, the rough silhouette outlines of a figure's shoulders and partial wing edges visible as dark silhouette frames at the screen edges (the over-the-shoulder element). The figure is shown from behind with wings partially spread visible at frame edges, drawn as simple pencil silhouettes. The middle and lower portion of the frame shows the desert landscape rapidly approaching the camera from a steep diving angle - dunes drawn as simple curved horizontal lines with strong perspective lines pointing toward a vanishing point in the lower portion of frame. In the deep middle-distance below, a small rectangular shape with leg-lines underneath represents the protagonist's ship as a small target the figure is diving toward. A red-pencil arrow shows the dive trajectory descending diagonally from upper-left to lower-right.

[PANEL 3 第三格 - REVERSE SHOT FROM BELOW]
Label underneath: "PANEL 3 · REVERSE LOW ANGLE / 35mm / 4s / wings deploy"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: low-angle reverse shot looking up at the diving figure. The frame shows the figure descending from upper portion toward the camera, drawn as a stick-figure with arms and legs in dive pose, with wings partially deployed extending outward from the back. The wings are drawn as simple curved-line shapes radiating outward from the figure's back. Above the figure in the upper portion of frame, the cloud layer and sky background drawn as soft marker shading. The figure occupies the central upper portion of frame, growing larger in the panel composition compared to panel 2. A red-pencil arrow indicates wings spreading outward from the figure's back.

[PANEL 4 第四格 - REVERSE TO PROTAGONIST DIVING INTO CRATE]
Label underneath: "PANEL 4 · MEDIUM SHOT PROTAGONIST CRATE-DIVE / 50mm / 3s / SFX: chest banging, distant scream"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: medium shot of the protagonist on the ship deck. The protagonist is drawn as a simple stick-figure with NO upper body visible at all - the protagonist's entire upper half (head, torso, arms) has plunged headfirst INTO a large rectangular crate-shape in the center of the frame. Only the protagonist's lower half is visible sticking out from the open crate top: the legs and butt are sticking up into the air at a comical angle, legs kicking awkwardly, feet flailing - like a cartoon character who has dived too deep into something. The crate occupies the center-foreground of the frame, drawn as a simple rectangular box-shape with the lid open and the protagonist's lower body comically protruding from the opening. Several small geometric shapes (squares, circles, triangles) are scattered around the crate representing items the protagonist has already pulled out and tossed aside. The deck is drawn as horizontal lines representing wooden planks. In the deep background visible at the upper portion of frame, faint suggestions of ship railings and mast verticals as simple straight pencil lines. Small motion-line clusters around the protagonist's flailing feet and the scattered objects suggest hurried slapstick comedic rummaging.

[PANEL 5 底部 - LANDING POSE STERN]
Label underneath: "PANEL 5 · MEDIUM WIDE LANDING / 35mm / 3s / SFX: impact, wing fold"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: medium-wide shot on the ship's stern area. The ship's stern building structure is drawn as simple rectangular and triangular geometric shapes - a vertical mast in the right portion of frame with a triangular sail at top, and the stern building structure as a stepped rectangular shape in the middle-distance. The figure (副手) has just landed on the deck in the center foreground of the frame, drawn as a stick-figure in a one-knee-down landing pose with one hand touching ground and the other arm bent. Wings are shown half-folded behind the figure as simple curved lines arcing back from the shoulders. A small dust-cloud burst is drawn around the figure's landing point as soft marker smudges. The figure's head is angled forward looking off-screen toward the screen-left direction (toward the bow of the ship where the protagonist is). A few small motion lines show wings still folding.

[OVERALL ART DIRECTION]
Classic animation school storyboard aesthetic. Pencil line-work with visible sketch lines, occasional construction lines and rough corrections. Grey marker shading for tonal areas (sky, shadows, smoke). Red-pencil arrow annotations on panels 1, 2, 3, and 5 to indicate camera movement and character motion arcs. Off-white paper texture background. NOT a finished illustration. NOT photorealistic. NOT detailed character art. NOT colored. The entire storyboard is intentionally monochromatic (pencil grey + red arrow accents) to emphasize that this is a planning document for composition only, not a visual reference for final rendering.

Title at top center reads "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG5 - LIEUTENANT BIRD-DIVE ENTRANCE STORYBOARD" in small clean black sans-serif text.
```
