**🀄 中文版**

```
=== GPT-Image-2 提示词 - 万物生第1集 副手登场段 抽象故事板 1x5竖排版 v2 ===

【用途】副手登场段的15秒分镜图+5个panel故事板+1x5竖排布局+每panel保持16:9横构图+确保每格构图与最终视频画面比例一致

【提示词】

一张 5 格电影故事板，排成 5 行 1 列的竖向网格。整体画布比例为 16:45 竖向（极度高的竖幅取向）。网格内每一个单独的格子都是一个 16:9 横向矩形。5 个格子自上而下竖向堆叠。每个格子都是一张带白色页边的铅笔加马克笔粗略草图矩形，每格下方有一条小小的信息条。

手绘动画学院故事板美学，铅笔线条勾勒、灰色马克笔上调、偶有红铅笔箭头批注以指示运镜或角色运动弧线、米白色纸面质感背景。刻意保持粗糙抽象的草图质感——不是完成稿插画——所有人形都画成极简的火柴人剪影或简单的身体形状，没有面部特征、没有服装细节、没有可见配饰，所有物体都用基本几何形状表示（箱子用方块、帆用三角形、船体用简单弧线）。其目的仅在于传达构图与空间关系，而非视觉细节。

[PANEL 1 顶部 - 大远景空中建立镜头]
下方标签："PANEL 1 · WIDE AERIAL · stratosphere · static · 2s · SFX: bird scream"
格子比例严格为 16:9 横向。
构图：极大远景空中俯拍，画面上部是高空云层，下部是辽阔的沙漠地貌。沙漠地平线位于画面下三分之一处。一个带翼机械鸟形物的微小剪影呈现为一个小小的深色形状，从画面上方中央俯冲下降，画成一个简单粗略的铅笔剪影、展翅的鸟形，在格子里不超过一个指甲盖大小。一支红铅笔箭头从鸟形物向下指，标示俯冲方向。下方沙漠显示几条淡淡的水平线以暗示沙丘，沙漠区域的最右下角有一个微小到几乎看不见的矩形，周围有几个小点形状，代表主角的船和追击的沙艇，作为远处的微小剪影。云层在画面上部画成柔和的灰色马克笔涂抹。

[PANEL 2 第二格 - 过肩俯冲跟拍]
下方标签："PANEL 2 · OTS DIVE / 50mm / 3s / SFX: wind tear, war cry"
格子比例严格为 16:9 横向。
构图：从一个俯冲人物身后拍的过肩镜头。在最贴近镜头的前景、占据画面左右边缘处，一个人物的肩膀与部分翅膀边缘的粗略剪影轮廓作为深色剪影框出现在画面边缘（这就是过肩元素）。人物从背后呈现，翅膀部分张开、可见于画面边缘，画成简单的铅笔剪影。画面的中部与下部展示沙漠地貌从一个陡峭的俯冲角度迅速朝镜头逼近——沙丘画成简单的水平弧线，配以强烈的透视线指向画面下部的一个灭点。在纵深中远景的下方，一个底部带腿线的小矩形代表主角的船，作为人物正在俯冲对准的小目标。一支红铅笔箭头展示俯冲轨迹，从左上向右下斜向下降。

[PANEL 3 第三格 - 仰角反打镜头]
下方标签："PANEL 3 · REVERSE LOW ANGLE / 35mm / 4s / wings deploy"
格子比例严格为 16:9 横向。
构图：仰角反打镜头，向上看着俯冲的人物。画面展示人物从上部朝镜头下降，画成一个火柴人，手臂和腿呈俯冲姿态，翅膀从背部部分展开、向外延伸。翅膀画成从人物背部向外辐射的简单弧线形状。在人物上方、画面上部，云层与天空背景画成柔和的马克笔上调。人物占据画面的中央上部，相比第 2 格在格子构图中显得更大。一支红铅笔箭头标示翅膀从人物背部向外张开。

[PANEL 4 第四格 - 反打到主角一头扎进货箱]
下方标签："PANEL 4 · MEDIUM SHOT PROTAGONIST CRATE-DIVE / 50mm / 3s / SFX: chest banging, distant scream"
格子比例严格为 16:9 横向。
构图：主角在船甲板上的中景。主角画成一个简单火柴人，上半身完全不可见——主角的整个上半身（头、躯干、手臂）已经一头扎进画面中央一个大型矩形货箱形状里。只有主角的下半身露在敞开的箱顶之外可见：双腿和屁股以滑稽的角度翘在空中，双腿别扭地乱踢，双脚乱蹬——就像一个卡通角色扎进什么东西扎得太深了。货箱占据画面的中央前景，画成一个简单的矩形箱体，箱盖打开，主角的下半身滑稽地从开口处探出。货箱周围散落着若干小几何形状（方块、圆形、三角形），代表主角已经翻出来扔到一旁的物品。甲板画成水平线条，代表木板。在画面上部可见的纵深背景里，淡淡暗示着船的栏杆和桅杆竖线，画成简单的直铅笔线。主角乱蹬的双脚周围和散落物体周围有小簇运动线，暗示着匆忙的、闹剧式喜剧的翻找。

[PANEL 5 底部 - 着陆姿势·船尾]
下方标签："PANEL 5 · MEDIUM WIDE LANDING / 35mm / 3s / SFX: impact, wing fold"
格子比例严格为 16:9 横向。
构图：船尾区域的中-远景。船尾的建筑结构画成简单的矩形和三角形几何形状——画面右部一根竖直桅杆顶端带一面三角帆，中远景里船尾建筑结构画成一个阶梯状矩形。那个人物（副手）刚刚降落在画面中央前景的甲板上，画成一个火柴人，呈单膝跪地的着陆姿势，一只手触地、另一只手臂弯曲。翅膀在人物身后画成半折叠状，呈从肩膀向后弧出的简单弧线。人物着陆点周围画着一小团爆开的尘云，呈柔和的马克笔涂抹。人物的头部朝前、向画面左侧方向望去（朝向船头，主角所在的方向）。几条小运动线表明翅膀仍在折叠中。

[整体艺术指导]
经典动画学院故事板美学。铅笔线条勾勒，可见草图线、偶有结构辅助线和粗略修正。灰色马克笔上调表现影调区域（天空、阴影、烟雾）。第 1、2、3、5 格上有红铅笔箭头批注，以指示运镜与角色运动弧线。米白色纸面质感背景。不是完成稿插画。不是照片级真实。不是精细角色美术。不上色。整张故事板刻意保持单色（铅笔灰 + 红箭头点缀），以强调这是一份仅供构图用的策划文档，而非最终渲染的视觉参考。

顶部居中标题写着 "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG5 - LIEUTENANT BIRD-DIVE ENTRANCE STORYBOARD"，用小号干净的黑色无衬线文字。

[关键约束]
布局铁律: 整体画布是16:45 vertical aspect ratio竖向超长比例+5 panels stacked vertically from top to bottom in a 5-row 1-column grid+绝对不要横向排列+绝对不要2x3网格+绝对不要任何其他布局。

每格比例铁律: EACH individual panel inside the grid MUST be a 16:9 horizontal landscape rectangle+每一个panel都是宽多于高的横向矩形+绝对不要竖向panel+绝对不要正方形panel+5个panel高度相等+5个panel宽度相等+5个panel都严格保持16:9 horizontal landscape ratio.

风格铁律: 纯手绘铅笔素描风格+pencil-and-marker rough sketch+animation school storyboard aesthetic+visible sketch lines+rough construction corrections+灰色marker shading+red-pencil camera arrow annotations+off-white paper background texture+intentionally rough and abstract+NOT finished illustration+NOT photorealistic+NOT colored+NOT detailed character rendering+NOT clean line art+NOT digital perfectly clean lines+all lines must look hand-drawn with natural variations.

简笔画铁律: ALL human figures must be rendered as MINIMAL STICK-FIGURE SILHOUETTES or basic body shapes only+NO facial features+NO clothing details+NO accessories+NO equipment details+NO weapon details+NO hair details+NO color on figures+absolutely NO character design rendering+the figures are placeholder shapes indicating position and pose only+not character illustrations.

几何形状铁律: ALL objects must be rendered as BASIC GEOMETRIC SHAPES only+ship as simple rectangles+sails as triangles+crates as squares+masts as straight lines+wings as simple curved lines+no detail rendering of any object+all objects are placeholder shapes indicating spatial position only.

NOT horizontal layout+NOT 1-row 5-column grid+NOT 2x3 grid+NOT square panels+NOT vertical 9:16 panels inside the grid+NOT photorealistic+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT detailed concept art+NOT finished character illustration+NOT colored characters+NOT realistic lighting+NOT cinematic photography+NOT detailed costume design+NOT detailed weapon design+NOT shaded character details+NOT clean digital line art+NOT manga-style detailed art+NOT comic book detailed art+NOT brand-style mascot illustration+无字幕水印+无装饰元素除铅笔箭头与shot label外。

A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, in the style of professional film production storyboards. Total image aspect ratio strictly 21:9.

Layout: 2 columns x 2 rows grid on aged paper background. Top-left and top-right and bottom-left cells are storyboard panels each strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. Bottom-right cell is a shooting plan diagram (top-down bird's eye view of the desert with camera positions and 180-degree axis line marked).

Beneath each storyboard panel inner frame is a hand-lettered caption strip showing panel number, lens specification, shot type, and brief SFX/action notes in serif block letters slightly uneven.

The drawing technique is loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective and clear cinematic composition. Sketches are deliberately gestural but every spatial relationship and prop detail is clearly readable. Selective red colored pencil arrows and emphasis marks indicate movement direction, force vectors, and key dramatic beats.

LIGHTING NOTE FOR ALL PANELS: late afternoon desert golden hour, low sun key light from upper-left 30 degrees. Dense cross-hatching dominates most surfaces representing the shadow base of the warm desert atmosphere. Selective bright untouched paper zones represent the directional sun-key highlights on metal armor surfaces and rim-lit silhouettes. Strong tonal contrast throughout — deep blacks in shadow zones, bright untouched paper for highlights, NOT evenly lit.

Style references: Wes Anderson production storyboards, traditional Hollywood film storyboard aesthetic, animator's pre-visualization sketches, Mad Max Fury Road action sequence boards.

Drawing rules:
- Pencil graphite on warm off-white aged paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use
- Black ink for panel borders and hand-lettered captions
- Red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera position triangles, and 180-degree axis line
- Cross-hatching for shading; bright highlight zones use untouched paper
- Hand-lettered captions in serif-style block letters, slightly uneven

DIRECTIONAL AXIS RULE: the protagonist ship is moving deep into frame (forward vector toward upper frame edge). The buried Liu Hui erupts from the desert sand ahead of and to the side of the ship's forward path, between the ship's current position and its destination. The three sand skimmers are dragged behind the ship trailing in the rear. Cameras stay on one consistent side of this action axis throughout the sequence to maintain spatial continuity.

Title at top center of entire image, hand-lettered in confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9A — CHIEFTAIN ENTRANCE & POWER RUN"

Below title, hand-lettered subtitle in smaller letters: "12 SECONDS / 3 PANELS / SHOOTING PLAN"

PANEL 1 (top-left cell) — POV FROM SAND HUNTERS LOOKING AT DISTANT SAND MOUND:
Scene composition: this is a point-of-view shot from inside one of the sand skimmers looking out across the desert toward the horizon. Lower foreground edge of frame: the sketched silhouette of a sand hunter's shoulder and the rear-end of the taut rope still connected off-frame to the protagonist ship's leg — silhouette of skimmer rim faintly visible bottom corners. Mid-ground and background: vast empty desert dunes stretching to the horizon line at roughly upper third of panel. Center of frame, in middle distance: a localized sand mound is rising up from the otherwise flat desert surface — sand mound is drawn with crisp ink lines for the rising silhouette, surrounded by spreading radial crack lines on the desert surface fanning outward from the mound's base, and small puffs of sand dust drawn as cross-hatched clouds at the mound's perimeter. Above the mound, faint sketched vibration lines indicate the ground is trembling. Sky: late afternoon golden hour, dense cross-hatching for the warm-toned upper atmosphere, with selective bright zones near the horizon for sun glow.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow inside the panel pointing from the hunter silhouette outward toward the mound, labeled "POV / hunter's eyeline."

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 1 — POV SHOT FROM SKIMMER / 50mm telephoto / hand-held with breathing micro-shake / SFX: distant ground rumble, sand cracking, low sub-bass swell"

PANEL 2 (top-right cell) — LOW ANGLE HERO LANDING — CHIEFTAIN ERUPTS FROM SAND:
Scene composition: extreme low angle ground-level camera looking up at a fusion-form mechanical chieftain figure who has just erupted from the desert floor. The figure occupies the central two-thirds of the frame, towering up out of frame top — dense pencil-sketched massive bronze mechanical armor covering torso, arms, legs; two tall spiraling tower-like horns rising from the shoulders piercing into the upper sky; a small human face visible at the top of the chest armor (peering out from the armor collar). Glowing cyan core circle is visible at the chest center — drawn as untouched paper with radiating bright lines. The figure's landing pose: hero-landing classic — both knees slightly bent, both armored fists planted into the sand ground, head lowered, body coiled like a compressed spring just before standing. At the base around the figure: massive sand particles and debris explode outward and upward in radial bursts — drawn as scattered ink dots and short streaks fanning radially around the impact point. The desert sand floor is cratered with deep cracks radiating outward from beneath the figure. Background sky: dense cross-hatching for shadow underside of the sky as seen from below, with bright untouched paper zones at the upper edges representing the sun behind the figure creating a strong backlight rim around the figure's silhouette.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow rising from ground level pointing upward toward the chieftain figure, labeled "extreme low angle / hero landing." Additional red pencil emphasis marks: short impact-burst lines around the kneeling pose, and small "POW" emphasis marks (no English text) drawn as radial impact rays at the contact points between fists and ground.

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 2 — LOW ANGLE HERO LANDING / 24mm wide / hand-held with sharp impact-frame stabilize / SFX: ground-shattering THOOM, sand-explosion roar, cyan-core power-up surge"

PANEL 3 (bottom-left cell) — ORBIT TRACKING POWER RUN — CHIEFTAIN CHARGES FORWARD:
Scene composition: this panel depicts a single moment in an orbit tracking shot — camera is positioned approximately 3/4 angle relative to the running chieftain figure, capturing him from front-quarter view as he charges forward in a power run. The chieftain figure occupies the center-right portion of the frame, mid-stride with one massive armored leg planted hard into the sand creating a visible deep crater impact, the other leg lifted high in mid-step. His upper body is upright and stable, NOT bobbing — the heavy armor stays balanced while his lower body powers through the ground. His face is set in stern focused expression (single eye-patched, the visible eye narrowed in determination), NOT shouting. The two shoulder spiral horns trail slightly behind from running momentum. The cyan chest core glows. Behind the chieftain figure, partial background visible: the sand desert with skimmer silhouettes and the protagonist ship trailing in the far distance ahead of him — the chieftain is catching up to them rapidly. At the chieftain's planted foot: massive sand plume erupting upward in cross-hatched dust cloud, with deep ground-crater impact lines radiating outward. Sweeping orbit-motion blur lines drawn around the figure to indicate the camera arc.

Camera annotation: a large red pencil curved arrow drawn arcing around the chieftain figure indicating the orbit camera path, labeled "orbit / half-rotation / front to back." Red pencil straight arrow pointing forward from the chieftain in the direction of his charge toward the off-frame ship.

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 3 — ORBIT TRACKING POWER RUN / 35mm prime / orbit at 3/4 angle, half-rotation / SFX: thunderous footfalls, sand-impact booms, armor mechanism rhythmic clanks, growing low-frequency engine-like power throb"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right cell) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW OF THE DESERT BATTLEFIELD:
Drawn as a clean top-down schematic diagram on the aged paper. Center of cell: a sketched simplified outline of the desert terrain as seen from above. Upper portion of cell: the protagonist ship drawn as a rectangular schematic silhouette with nine small leg-marks underneath, moving toward the upper edge of the diagram (with a red pencil directional arrow showing the forward vector). Behind the ship trailing in lower-middle area of cell: three small triangular schematic outlines representing the three sand skimmers, each connected by a thin line (rope) to one of the ship's leg-marks. Lower-middle area of cell, slightly off to one side from directly behind the ship: a small starburst symbol marking the sand mound where Liu Hui erupts from underground (with a label "POINT OF ERUPTION"). Around this starburst, three small red pencil triangles marking the camera positions for each of the three panels (PANEL 1 camera positioned inside one skimmer pointing at the starburst; PANEL 2 camera positioned at ground level just outside the starburst pointing up; PANEL 3 camera positioned several meters away from the starburst with a curved orbit-arc red line indicating the half-rotation arc). A dashed red pencil straight line running across the diagram from the ship's forward vector through the eruption starburst — this is the 180-degree axis line. Labels in hand-lettered serif block letters: "180° AXIS LINE", "P1 / P2 / P3 CAMERA POSITIONS", "POINT OF ERUPTION", "PROTAGONIST SHIP HEADING", "SKIMMERS DRAGGED".

Caption strip below the shooting plan cell: "SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED / cameras stay on one side of action / spatial continuity maintained across all 3 panels"

PRODUCTION NOTES at bottom edge of overall image (hand-lettered serif block letters along the bottom margin of the aged paper, outside the panel grid): "WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 9A — CHIEFTAIN ENTRANCE / continuity: prior segment closes with sand hunter head-turn at off-screen 'WO LAI!' shout / this segment opens with their POV revealing the source / 12 second total runtime / 3 shots cut internally as one continuous Seedance generation / next segment 9B begins with chieftain arriving at female sand hunter's skimmer to take the rope"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting style, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red colored pencil for arrows and annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO AI-style smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour desert high-contrast lighting in all panels — dense cross-hatch shadows + selective bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each storyboard panel's inner frame strictly 16:9 aspect ratio
- Total image strictly 21:9 aspect ratio
- All hand-lettered text in confident serif block letters with slight unevenness — readable but not machine-printed
- Aged off-white paper texture throughout with subtle edge wear
- Maintain consistent fusion-form chieftain character appearance across PANEL 2 and PANEL 3 — both show same character with bronze mechanical armor, shoulder spiral horns, cyan chest core, eye patch on right eye
```

**🀄 中文版**

```
一幅绘制在做旧米白纸张上的手绘铅笔素描故事板，采用专业影视制作故事板的风格。整幅图像宽高比严格为 21:9。

版式：做旧纸张背景上的 2 列 × 2 行网格。左上、右上和左下格是故事板分镜，每格严格为 16:9 横向横幅格式，带粗重的手绘黑色墨水画框边线。右下格是一张拍摄方案示意图（沙漠的俯视鸟瞰图，标出机位和 180 度轴线）。

每个故事板分镜内框下方都有一条手写说明文字条，以略微参差的衬线方块字标注分镜编号、镜头规格、景别以及简短的音效/动作备注。

绘画技法为松弛的石墨铅笔，配以自信的建筑透视和清晰的电影化构图。素描刻意带有手势感的随性笔触，但每一处空间关系和道具细节都清晰可辨。有选择地使用红色彩铅箭头与强调标记，指示运动方向、力矢量以及关键的戏剧节拍。

所有分镜的光照说明：午后晚些时分的沙漠黄金时刻，低位太阳主光来自左上方 30 度。密集的交叉排线主导大部分表面，表现温暖沙漠氛围的阴影基底。有选择地保留明亮的留白纸张区域，表现金属盔甲表面的定向阳光主光高光与轮廓逆光剪影。整体色调对比强烈——阴影区为深黑，高光处为明亮留白，绝非均匀打光。

风格参考：韦斯·安德森（Wes Anderson）制作故事板、传统好莱坞影视故事板美学、动画师的预演素描、《疯狂的麦克斯：狂暴之路》（Mad Max Fury Road）动作场面分镜板。

绘画规则：
- 在温暖的米白做旧纸张背景上用石墨铅笔作画，边缘略有磨损、隐约带有污渍，暗示历经繁重的制作使用
- 黑色墨水用于分镜边框和手写说明
- 红色彩铅仅用于箭头、强调标记、机位三角形以及 180 度轴线
- 用交叉排线表现阴影；明亮高光区域使用留白纸张
- 手写说明采用衬线风格方块字，略微参差

方向轴规则：主角飞船正向画面纵深方向移动（前进矢量朝向画面上缘）。深埋地下的刘回从沙漠沙地中、在飞船前进路径的前方偏侧处破土而出，位于飞船当前位置与其目的地之间。三艘沙地滑沙艇被飞船拖拽在后方尾随。在整个序列中，机位始终保持在这条动作轴线的同一侧，以维持空间连贯性。

整幅图像顶部居中标题，以自信的方块衬线手写："WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9A — CHIEFTAIN ENTRANCE & POWER RUN"

标题下方，以较小字体手写副标题："12 SECONDS / 3 PANELS / SHOOTING PLAN"

分镜 1（左上格）——沙猎手望向远处沙丘的主观视角：
画面构图：这是从某艘沙地滑沙艇内部向外、越过沙漠望向地平线的主观视角镜头。画面下方前景边缘：一名沙猎手肩膀的素描剪影，以及那根绷紧绳索的尾端，绳索仍在画外连接到主角飞船的腿——滑沙艇边缘的剪影在底部两角隐约可见。中景与背景：广袤空旷的沙漠沙丘绵延至地平线，地平线大致在分镜的上三分之一处。画面中央、中景距离上：一座局部隆起的沙丘正从原本平坦的沙漠表面拔地而起——沙丘以利落的墨线勾勒出隆起的剪影，四周是从丘底向外扇形散开的放射状裂纹，丘缘有以交叉排线绘成的尘云状小股扬沙。沙丘上方，淡淡的素描振动线条表示地面正在颤动。天空：午后晚些时分的黄金时刻，以密集交叉排线表现暖调高空，地平线附近有选择性的明亮区域表现日照辉光。

机位标注：分镜内一支红铅箭头从猎手剪影向外指向沙丘，标注 "POV / hunter's eyeline"。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 1 — POV SHOT FROM SKIMMER / 50mm telephoto / hand-held with breathing micro-shake / SFX: distant ground rumble, sand cracking, low sub-bass swell"

分镜 2（右上格）——低角度英雄落地——首领破沙而出：
画面构图：极低角度的地面机位仰拍一个刚刚从沙漠地面破土而出的融合形态机械首领身形。该身形占据画面中部三分之二，高耸出画面顶部——以密集铅笔素描绘出庞大的青铜机械装甲覆盖躯干、双臂、双腿；两根高耸的塔状螺旋犄角从双肩升起，刺入上方天际；胸甲顶部可见一张小小的人脸（从装甲领圈中探出）。发着光的青色核心圆环在胸口中央可见——以留白纸张绘出，带向外辐射的明亮线条。该身形的落地姿态：经典英雄落地——双膝微屈，双装甲拳头扎进沙地，头部低垂，身体如压缩弹簧般蓄势，正待起身。身形周围的地基处：庞大的沙粒与碎屑向外向上以放射状爆裂迸发——画成散落的墨点和短促条痕，绕撞击点放射状散开。沙漠沙地面被砸出凹坑，深裂缝从身形下方向外辐射。背景天空：以密集交叉排线表现自下而上看到的天空阴影底面，上缘有明亮留白区域，表现身形背后的太阳，在身形剪影周围形成强烈的逆光轮廓。

机位标注：一支红铅箭头从地面高度向上指向首领身形，标注 "extreme low angle / hero landing"。另有红铅强调标记：跪姿周围的短促撞击爆裂线，以及在拳头与地面接触点处画成放射状撞击光线的 "POW" 小强调标记（无英文文字）。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 2 — LOW ANGLE HERO LANDING / 24mm wide / hand-held with sharp impact-frame stabilize / SFX: ground-shattering THOOM, sand-explosion roar, cyan-core power-up surge"

分镜 3（左下格）——环绕跟拍发力奔跑——首领向前冲锋：
画面构图：本分镜描绘一个环绕跟拍镜头中的单一瞬间——机位相对于奔跑的首领身形约处于四分之三角度，以前四分之一视角捕捉他全力奔跑向前冲锋。首领身形占据画面中右部，迈步至半途，一条庞大的装甲腿狠狠扎进沙中砸出明显的深坑撞击，另一条腿在迈步中高高抬起。他的上半身挺直而稳定，绝不上下晃动——沉重的装甲保持平衡，下半身则发力穿过地面。他的脸神情严峻专注（独眼带罩，可见的那只眼在决意中眯起），并未呐喊。两根肩部螺旋犄角因奔跑的惯性略微向后曳。胸口青色核心发着光。首领身形之后，可见部分背景：沙漠以及滑沙艇剪影，主角飞船在远处他前方拖拽前行——首领正迅速追上他们。在首领扎稳的脚下：庞大的沙尘羽流以交叉排线尘云向上喷涌，带深深的地面凹坑撞击线向外辐射。身形周围画有横扫的环绕运动模糊线，指示机位的弧形轨迹。

机位标注：一支大红铅曲线箭头绕首领身形弧形画出，指示环绕机位路径，标注 "orbit / half-rotation / front to back"。一支红铅直线箭头从首领向前、朝画外飞船的方向指出，表示他的冲锋方向。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 3 — ORBIT TRACKING POWER RUN / 35mm prime / orbit at 3/4 angle, half-rotation / SFX: thunderous footfalls, sand-impact booms, armor mechanism rhythmic clanks, growing low-frequency engine-like power throb"

拍摄方案（右下格）——沙漠战场的俯视鸟瞰总览：
在做旧纸张上画成一张干净的俯视示意图。格子中央：从上方俯视的沙漠地形的简化轮廓素描。格子上部：主角飞船画成一道长方形示意剪影，下方带九个小腿标，朝示意图上缘移动（配一支红铅方向箭头表示前进矢量）。飞船后方、在格子的中下区域尾随：三个三角形小示意轮廓代表三艘沙地滑沙艇，每个都由一条细线（绳索）连到飞船的某个腿标。格子的中下区域、略偏离飞船正后方一侧：一个小星爆符号，标出刘回从地下破土而出的沙丘（带标签 "POINT OF ERUPTION"）。围绕这个星爆，三个红铅小三角形标出三个分镜各自的机位（分镜 1 机位置于某艘滑沙艇内，指向星爆；分镜 2 机位置于地面高度、紧邻星爆外侧，向上仰拍；分镜 3 机位置于距星爆数米之外，带一道红色环绕弧线指示半圈环绕弧）。一条红铅虚线笔直贯穿示意图，从飞船的前进矢量穿过破土星爆——这便是 180 度轴线。手写衬线方块字标签："180° AXIS LINE"、"P1 / P2 / P3 CAMERA POSITIONS"、"POINT OF ERUPTION"、"PROTAGONIST SHIP HEADING"、"SKIMMERS DRAGGED"。

拍摄方案格下方说明条："SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED / cameras stay on one side of action / spatial continuity maintained across all 3 panels"

整幅图像底缘的制作笔记（沿做旧纸张底边、网格之外，以手写衬线方块字书写）："WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 9A — CHIEFTAIN ENTRANCE / continuity: prior segment closes with sand hunter head-turn at off-screen 'WO LAI!' shout / this segment opens with their POV revealing the source / 12 second total runtime / 3 shots cut internally as one continuous Seedance generation / next segment 9B begins with chieftain arriving at female sand hunter's skimmer to take the rope"

硬性约束：
- 不要数字绘画风格，不要 3D 渲染，除红色彩铅用于箭头和标注外不要任何彩色叠加
- 不要摄影元素，不要 AI 风格的平滑渐变
- 所有分镜都采用强烈的黄金时刻沙漠高对比光照——密集交叉排线阴影 + 有选择的明亮留白高光，绝非均匀打光
- 每个故事板分镜的内框严格为 16:9 宽高比
- 整幅图像严格为 21:9 宽高比
- 所有手写文字采用自信的衬线方块字，略带参差——可读但非机器打印
- 通篇做旧米白纸张质感，带细微边缘磨损
- 在分镜 2 和分镜 3 之间保持融合形态首领角色外观一致——两者展现同一角色，带青铜机械装甲、肩部螺旋犄角、青色胸口核心、右眼眼罩

=== 万物生 - 关羽机甲多角度细节图 ===
=== 工具：GPT-Image-2 image2image ===
=== Reference：关羽机甲主图（25米巨型机甲完整版） ===
=== 用途：项目核心视觉资产+穿越段手部素材+长期项目素材库 ===
=== 输出：1张16:9合成图，含6个视角分镜 ===

【Change】
Transform the reference image (the full-body Guan Yu mecha hero shot) into a multi-angle character reference sheet showing 6 distinct views of the same mecha character. The output is a single 16:9 widescreen canvas divided into 6 panels arranged in a 3x2 grid layout. Each panel shows a different view of the mecha against a clean neutral warm-toned studio background.

The 6 panels are:

PANEL 1 (top-left, full-body front view): The complete Guan Yu mecha shown in full body straight-on front view, standing upright with neutral pose, both arms hanging naturally at sides, the curved blade weapon held in the right hand resting tip-down on the ground beside the right foot. Head tilted slightly downward in characteristic temple-guardian gaze. Show the entire mecha from head to feet, occupying the full vertical space of this panel.

PANEL 2 (top-middle, full-body back view): The complete Guan Yu mecha shown in full body straight-on back view, standing upright with neutral pose, both arms hanging at sides. The two large articulated heat exhaust panels extending from the back like a flowing cape are clearly visible and prominent. Show the back-side ornamentation density - dragons, clouds, ancient script engravings on the back armor plates. Show the entire mecha from head to feet.

PANEL 3 (top-right, head close-up front view): A tight close-up of the mecha's head and upper shoulders ONLY, showing the face plate in detail. Clearly show: the smooth featureless mechanical face plate with the single horizontal optical sensor slit emitting dim red glow; the tall pointed conical sensor mast on top reading as helmet spike; the two horizontal cooling fins on the head sides reading as helmet ear flaps; the vertical bundle of long thin metal cables hanging from the lower mask plate reading as a stylized mechanical beard; the engraved patterns radiating from the central spike. The head is at center frame, filling most of this panel's vertical space.

PANEL 4 (bottom-left, right hand close-up extended forward): An extreme close-up of the mecha's RIGHT mechanical hand, the hand is extended forward toward the camera in a NATURAL OPEN STATE - all five mechanical fingers fully extended and spread naturally, palm slightly visible facing the camera, NOT a fist, NOT a grip, NOT closed, the hand is in a relaxed open position as if reaching forward or offering. Show clearly: the long articulated mechanical fingers, the palm articulation, the visible hydraulic pistons and cable bundles at the wrist and finger joints, the tarnished bronze and aged gold accent details at the joint sockets, the antique crimson red and dark jade green matte paint with weathering and chipping. The hand fills the panel and is the clear focal point. The wrist and a small portion of forearm are visible at the edge of the panel for scale context. NO weapon held in this hand in this panel.

PANEL 5 (bottom-middle, weapon close-up): A close-up of the curved blade polearm weapon (mechanical Guan Dao / Green Dragon Crescent Blade), shown floating against the neutral background or held by a partial mechanical hand at the bottom edge of the panel. Show the weapon clearly: the long pole shaft with engraved Chinese dragon patterns; the curved crescent blade with industrial mechanical detail; ancient script etched into the blade surface; tarnished bronze and aged gold accents along the pole. The weapon is angled diagonally across this panel for dynamic composition.

PANEL 6 (bottom-right, foot and lower leg close-up): A close-up showing one of the mecha's lower legs and foot, from approximately mid-thigh down to the ground. Show clearly: the sectioned mechanical skirt of overlapping armor lamellae plates partially visible at top of panel; the sculpted thigh armor with engraved cloud patterns; the mechanical knee joint with visible hydraulics; the calf armor with mechanical detailing; the large mechanical foot. Show the deeply lowered planted stable stance the leg is in. Emphasize the engraved ornamentation density on the leg armor.

LAYOUT AND COMPOSITION RULES:
- The 6 panels are arranged in a clean 3x2 grid (3 columns, 2 rows)
- Each panel is approximately equal in size, separated by thin clean white/cream borders
- Above each panel, a small label in clean monospace black sans-serif text identifies the view: "FRONT", "BACK", "HEAD DETAIL", "RIGHT HAND OPEN", "WEAPON", "LEG DETAIL"
- Title text at the top center of the entire canvas: "GUAN YU MECHA - CHARACTER REFERENCE SHEET" in clean sans-serif black text
- Background of all panels is a uniform neutral warm-toned studio backdrop, slightly desaturated, subtle warm-tan vignette, NO environmental scenery, NO sacred ground elements (NO banners, NO steles, NO altar - this is a clean character reference, not a scene)
- Lighting on all 6 panels is consistent: soft directional warm key light from upper-left at approximately 30 degrees, gentle fill from right, rim lighting picking up edges of mecha forms

【Preserve】
THE GUAN YU MECHA IDENTITY MUST REMAIN 100% IDENTICAL to the reference image across all 6 panels:

- Exact same overall silhouette: extremely tall and slender humanoid (translates to whatever framing in each panel)
- Exact same proportions: small head, narrow shoulders, slim torso, dramatically narrow waist, long arms, oversized mechanical hands, very long thin legs
- Exact same face plate design: smooth featureless mechanical mask with single horizontal optical sensor slit emitting dim red glow, NO human features
- Exact same head ornamentation: tall pointed conical sensor mast on top, two horizontal cooling fins on sides, vertical bundle of long thin metal cables hanging from lower mask plate as stylized mechanical beard
- Exact same body construction: bio-mechanical aesthetic, segmented chest plates resembling exposed ribs and sternum, sculpted shoulder pieces with sharp angular edges, mechanical arms with exposed cable bundles, articulated waist with visible mechanical spine column, sculpted thigh and calf armor with clear mechanical jointing
- Exact same chest engraving: massive central dragon engraving on chest plate
- Exact same skirt: sectioned mechanical skirt of overlapping armor lamellae plates around narrow waist
- Exact same back panels: two large articulated heat exhaust panels extending from back like flowing cape
- Exact same ornamentation density: dense intricate ornamentation across all body surfaces, dragons clouds flames and ancient script engraved on every armor plate, like a Tibetan ritual artifact or Ming dynasty imperial object
- Exact same weapon design: long-handled curved blade polearm with engraved dragon patterns and ancient script
- Exact same paint and color palette: thick weathered military matte paint, dominant deep antique crimson red on chest pauldrons and helmet, secondary deep faded jade green on lower body and skirt sections, accent details in tarnished bronze and aged gold at engravings rivets and joint sockets, all paint matte non-reflective with no shine, decades of weathering causes paint chipping revealing dull bare grey metal underneath at edges
- Exact same color grade: warm sun-bleached tonality, vivid antique red and rich jade green clearly readable, tonal contrast compressed and gentle, heavy 35mm film grain texture
- Exact same Wong Kar-wai aesthetic: warm rich and saturated colors NOT muted grey, like a frame from In the Mood for Love or Ashes of Time
- Exact same overall impression: ancient war god temple guardian deity at absolute rest, weight and stillness and dread, sacred and inevitable

EVERY visual DNA element from the reference must be preserved across all 6 panels.

【Constraints】
NOT a battle scene, NOT environmental setting (no sacred ground, no banners, no steles, no altar - clean studio reference sheet ONLY).
NOT illustrated or cartoonish style - maintain the photorealistic film-grain aesthetic from reference.
NOT 3D rendering look, NOT anime style, NOT video game character sheet style.
NOT a different mecha design - the mecha identity must be 100% recognizable as the same character from reference.
NOT changing the proportions, colors, ornamentation, or design language between panels.
NOT muted grey palette - the warm saturated antique red and jade green must remain vivid as in reference.
NOT panels showing different mechas - all 6 panels show the SAME single character from different angles.
NOT empty or sparse panels - each panel must be clearly composed with the mecha element prominently visible.
NOT motion blur, NOT action poses except where specified (Panel 4 hand extended is NOT action, it is a clean reference pose).
NOT a fist or closed hand in Panel 4 - the right hand MUST be in fully open natural extended state.
NOT environmental scenery in any panel background - clean uniform neutral warm studio backdrop only.

Aspect ratio: 16:9 landscape cinematic widescreen.

**🀄 中文版**

```
=== 万物生 - 关羽机甲多角度细节图 ===
=== 工具：GPT-Image-2 图生图 ===
=== 参考图：关羽机甲主图（25米巨型机甲完整版） ===
=== 用途：项目核心视觉资产+穿越段手部素材+长期项目素材库 ===
=== 输出：1张16:9合成图，含6个视角分镜 ===

【改动】
将参考图（关羽机甲全身主打镜头）转化为一张多角度人物参考图，展示同一机甲角色的6个不同视图。输出为单张16:9宽屏画布，划分为按3x2网格排布的6个分格。每个分格在干净的中性暖调影棚背景前展示该机甲的一个不同视图。

6个分格为：

分格1（左上，全身正视）：完整的关羽机甲以全身正对视角呈现，直立站姿、姿态中性，双臂自然垂于体侧，弯刃武器持于右手、刀尖朝下立于右脚旁地面。头部以其标志性的庙宇守护者凝视微微下垂。展示从头到脚的整个机甲，占满该格的全部纵向空间。

分格2（顶部中央，全身背视）：完整的关羽机甲以全身正对的背视角呈现，直立站姿、姿态中性，双臂垂于体侧。两片如飘扬披风般从背部伸展的大型铰接式散热排气板清晰可见且醒目。展示背面的装饰密度——背甲板上的龙、云、古文字镌刻。展示从头到脚的整个机甲。

分格3（右上，头部正面特写）：仅对机甲头部与上肩进行紧凑特写，细致展示面甲。清晰展示：光滑无表情的机械面甲，配单条横向光学传感器缝、散发暗红微光；顶部高耸的尖锥形传感器桅杆，读作头盔尖刺；头部两侧的两片横向冷却鳍，读作头盔护耳；自下颌面甲板垂下的一束细长金属缆，读作风格化的机械胡须；自中央尖刺向外辐射的镌刻纹样。头部位于画面中央，占据该格大部分纵向空间。

分格4（左下，右手前伸特写）：对机甲**右**机械手的极近特写，手向前朝镜头伸出，处于**自然张开状态**——五根机械手指全部伸展、自然张开，掌心略微可见、朝向镜头，不是握拳，不是抓握，不是闭合，手处于放松张开的姿态，仿佛正在前伸或递出。清晰展示：长长的铰接机械手指、掌部铰接、腕部与指关节处可见的液压活塞与缆束、关节窝处暗淡青铜与陈旧金的点缀细节、带风化与剥落的古董绯红与暗玉绿哑光漆。手填满该格，是明确的视觉焦点。腕部与一小段前臂在格边缘可见以提供尺度参照。此格中该手不持任何武器。

分格5（底部中央，武器特写）：弯刃长柄武器（机械版关刀/青龙偃月刀）的特写，悬浮于中性背景前，或由格底边缘一只局部机械手持握。清晰展示武器：刻有中国龙纹的长杆柄；带工业机械细节的弯月刀刃；刀面蚀刻的古文字；沿杆柄的暗淡青铜与陈旧金点缀。武器斜跨该格呈对角排布，构图富有动感。

分格6（右下，足部与小腿特写）：展示机甲一条小腿与足部的特写，从约大腿中段向下直至地面。清晰展示：格顶部分可见的、由叠覆鳞甲板构成的分段机械裙甲；刻有云纹的雕塑感大腿护甲；带可见液压的机械膝关节；带机械细节的小腿护甲；硕大的机械足。展示该腿所处的深沉下压、稳固扎根的站姿。强调腿甲上镌刻装饰的密度。

布局与构图规则：
- 6个分格按干净的3x2网格（3列2行）排布
- 各格尺寸大致相等，以细净的白/奶油色边线分隔
- 每格上方有一行干净的等宽无衬线黑色小字标识视图："FRONT"、"BACK"、"HEAD DETAIL"、"RIGHT HAND OPEN"、"WEAPON"、"LEG DETAIL"
- 整个画布顶部中央的标题文字："GUAN YU MECHA - CHARACTER REFERENCE SHEET"，干净无衬线黑色文字
- 所有分格背景为统一的中性暖调影棚背景，略微降饱和，带微妙的暖棕褐渐晕，无环境景物，无圣地元素（无旗幡，无石碑，无祭坛——这是干净的人物参考图，不是场景）
- 所有6格布光一致：来自左上约30度的柔和定向暖调主光，来自右侧的轻柔补光，勾出机甲各形体边缘的边缘光

【保留】
关羽机甲的身份特征在全部6格中必须保持与参考图100%一致：

- 完全相同的整体轮廓：极度高大而修长的人形（在每格中转译为相应的取景）
- 完全相同的比例：小头、窄肩、瘦躯干、极度纤细的腰、长臂、超大机械手、极长极细的腿
- 完全相同的面甲设计：光滑无表情的机械面具，配单条横向光学传感器缝、散发暗红微光，无人类五官
- 完全相同的头部装饰：顶部高耸的尖锥形传感器桅杆，两侧两片横向冷却鳍，自下颌面甲板垂下的一束细长金属缆作为风格化机械胡须
- 完全相同的躯体构造：生物机械美学，状如外露肋骨与胸骨的分段胸甲板，带锐利棱角的雕塑感肩件，带外露缆束的机械臂，带可见机械脊柱列的铰接腰部，带清晰机械关节的雕塑感大腿与小腿护甲
- 完全相同的胸部镌刻：胸甲板上巨大的中央龙形镌刻
- 完全相同的裙甲：环绕纤细腰部、由叠覆鳞甲板构成的分段机械裙甲
- 完全相同的背板：两片如飘扬披风般从背部伸展的大型铰接式散热排气板
- 完全相同的装饰密度：遍布全身各表面的密集精细装饰，龙、云、火焰与古文字镌刻于每一块护甲板，宛如藏式法器或明代皇家器物
- 完全相同的武器设计：刻有龙纹与古文字的长柄弯刃长杆武器
- 完全相同的漆面与色板：厚重风化的军用哑光漆，主色为胸甲、肩甲与头盔上的深沉古董绯红，次色为下身与裙甲段的深沉褪色玉绿，点缀细节为镌刻、铆钉与关节窝处的暗淡青铜与陈旧金，所有漆面均哑光无反光，数十年风化导致漆面剥落、在边缘露出底下暗淡的裸灰金属
- 完全相同的调色：暖调日晒褪色色性，鲜明的古董红与浓郁玉绿清晰可读，影调对比压缩而柔和，厚重的35mm胶片颗粒质感
- 完全相同的王家卫美学：暖调浓郁饱和的色彩，不是低饱和灰调，宛如《花样年华》或《东邪西毒》中的一帧
- 完全相同的整体观感：绝对静止状态下的古代战神庙宇守护神祇，分量感、静寂感与威慑感，神圣而不可逆

参考图中**每一处**视觉DNA元素都必须在全部6格中保留。

【约束】
不是战斗场景，不是环境设定（无圣地，无旗幡，无石碑，无祭坛——仅干净影棚参考图）。
不是插画或卡通风格——保持参考图中的照片级胶片颗粒美学。
不是3D渲染观感，不是动漫风格，不是电子游戏人物设定图风格。
不是另一种机甲设计——机甲身份必须100%可辨识为参考图中的同一角色。
不在各格之间改变比例、色彩、装饰或设计语言。
不是低饱和灰调色板——暖调饱和的古董红与玉绿必须如参考图般保持鲜明。
不是展示不同机甲的各格——全部6格展示**同一**角色的不同角度。
不是空荡或稀疏的分格——每格必须构图清晰、机甲元素醒目可见。
不是运动模糊，不是动作姿态（除指定处外）（分格4手部前伸不是动作，而是干净的参考姿态）。
分格4中不是握拳或闭合的手——右手**必须**处于完全张开的自然伸展状态。
任何分格背景中都不是环境景物——仅干净统一的中性暖调影棚背景。

宽高比：16:9横版电影感宽屏。
```

### 5（14838 字）

```
A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, professional film production style. Image aspect ratio strictly 16:9.

Layout: 2x2 grid, 4 cells, each cell strictly 16:9 landscape with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. Top row: Panel 1, Panel 2. Bottom row: Panel 3, Shooting Plan diagram. Beneath each cell a hand-lettered caption strip in serif block letters.

DRAWING TECHNIQUE: loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective. Cross-hatching for shading. Selective red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera positions, motion vectors, annotations. Aged off-white paper with slight edge wear. Hand-lettered captions in slightly uneven serif block letters.

GLOBAL LIGHTING: late afternoon golden hour, low sun key light from one consistent direction. Strong rim-lighting on character silhouettes, deep shadow zones opposite. Dense cross-hatching for shadows, bright untouched paper for highlights. Strong tonal contrast — NOT evenly lit.

GLOBAL COMPOSITION RULE: avoid centered symmetric framing. All 3 panels use off-axis, asymmetric, rule-of-thirds, or diagonal compositions. NO panel has the main subject dead-center.

Style references: Marvel Studios action board, Hollywood film storyboards, Mad Max Fury Road character beat boards, Looney Tunes whip pan transition boards.

Title at top center, hand-lettered confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10D — INVITATION FOUND + LIU HUI'S ORDER + WHIP PAN TO STUNNED PAIR"

Below title, smaller hand-lettered subtitle: "3 SHOTS / 2 HARD CUTS + 1 WHIP PAN / SHIP DECK / LIEUTENANT FINDS INVITATION → LIU HUI INSPECTS → ORDERS BIRD-DRAGON DISMANTLED → WHIP PAN REVEALS COMEDIC FREEZE"

PANEL 1 (top-left) — SHOT 1: SLIGHTLY LOW-OVERHEAD ANGLE / LIEUTENANT KNEELS BESIDE UNCONSCIOUS JIANG LIU, PULLS INVITATION FROM HIS GARMENT:
Slightly low overhead angle (camera positioned ~20-30 degrees above ground level, looking slightly down at the deck floor area). Tight medium shot focused on Lieutenant's kneeling body. Off-axis composition: Lieutenant occupies center-left to right portion of frame, Jiang Liu only partially visible at the LEFT edge of frame showing localized body parts (torso, part of upper arm, edge of his cream-colored billowing sleeve) — Jiang Liu's face and head NOT in frame.

JIANG LIU LOCAL VISIBLE: lying FLAT ON HIS BACK on the deck planks (face-up supine position), wine-red leather vest over cream loose-sleeved billowing shirt, wine-red trousers — only partial torso and arm visible at the left edge of frame, not the head.

LIEUTENANT (kneeling beside Jiang Liu, occupying center-to-right of frame): middle-aged woman with dark hair in high braided ponytail wrapped with tribal cloth headband decorated with beads and small charms, scar across face, sun-tanned copper skin, worn deep-brown leather sleeveless top, cream neck scarf, tribal blood-red ribbon and crimson cloth accents on arms and waist, bronze mechanical prosthetic right leg from hip to foot. Twin curved blades on hip belt (not drawn). Kneeling on one knee beside Jiang Liu.

ACTION CAPTURED: Lieutenant's hand has just pulled an invitation letter object out from Jiang Liu's garment, mid-action. She holds the invitation in her hand, about to raise her arm up. Her other hand may be steadying on Jiang Liu's chest area.

INVITATION LETTER: drawn as a small rectangular ornate object in Lieutenant's hand — its exact visual design will be specified by the asset reference at video production stage, but for storyboard purposes draw as a small flat rectangular letter-shaped item, roughly the size of a playing card, with subtle ornamental edges suggesting an imperial document.

In the final beat of this panel: Lieutenant's hand is raising up to extend the invitation toward the UPPER-RIGHT direction of the off-frame space (where Liu Hui stands off-frame). Lieutenant's eye-line follows her raised hand looking up toward the upper-right off-frame direction.

Background: deck planks #6B4830 receding in slight perspective. Dust motes in the air. Some scattered debris (loose fabric scrap, broken piece). Ship's structural elements (rope coil, mast base, deck cleats) visible in background depths.

Lighting: golden hour direct light from one direction rim-lighting Lieutenant's shoulder and the raised hand holding the invitation. Cross-hatched shadow on the opposite side.

Red pencil annotations: triangle in lower-left indicating slight low-overhead camera position. Arrow on Lieutenant's hand showing the pull-and-raise motion labeled "pulls invitation from garment → raises toward off-frame Liu Hui." Eye-line dashed arrow from Lieutenant up to upper-right off-frame labeled "Lieutenant looks up at Liu Hui."

Caption: "SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — SLIGHT LOW-OVERHEAD ANGLE / LIEUTENANT FINDS INVITATION / 50mm prime / camera mostly static with handheld micro-shake / SFX: fabric rustle as letter is pulled, Lieutenant: '老大,找到了,皇帝的邀请函。' / ACTION: Lieutenant pulls invitation from Jiang Liu's garment, raises arm to extend it toward off-frame Liu Hui, eye-line follows hand"

PANEL 2 (top-right) — SHOT 2: SHALLOW DOF CLOSE-UP / LIU HUI EXAMINES INVITATION + TURNS HEAD TO YELL:
Close-up framing, upper body only (waist-up to head). Shallow depth of field — Liu Hui sharp in focus, background heavily blurred bokeh. Off-axis composition: Liu Hui occupies the right 2/3 of frame, body angled at 3/4 view, his face slightly turned toward the invitation in his hand (which he holds at chest level).

LIU HUI in DEFAULT FORM upper body: bare upper torso with tribal tattoos on shoulders and chest, cream tribal scarf wrapped loosely around neck trailing over one shoulder, tribal blood-red scarf accents, short spiky black hair with forehead braid cord, eye-patch over right eye with scar through brow, bronze mechanical prosthetic right arm visible from shoulder down.

ACTION CAPTURED: Liu Hui holds the invitation letter at chest level with both hands (flesh left hand + prosthetic right hand jointly), his single visible eye narrowed in concentrated examination, his head slightly tilted down to look at the invitation. Expression: focused, scrutinizing.

BACKGROUND BLUR: heavily out-of-focus bokeh. From the bottom of frame to the top, the background reads as follows (all in shallow DOF blur):
- Bottom edge of frame: out-of-focus ship's inner railing or deck edge structure (rendered as soft pencil strokes with reduced contrast)
- Middle band: out-of-focus desert horizon line beyond the ship (warm pale tan band)
- Upper band: out-of-focus golden hour sky in #C8581F sunset orange (rendered with soft cross-hatching)

The deep depth of background allows the viewer to read "Liu Hui is standing on a ship deck with outside desert view beyond" without needing sharp background detail.

TRANSITION BEAT IN THIS PANEL: in the final beat, Liu Hui suddenly snaps his head to the LEFT (toward another off-frame direction where the bird-dragon + crab chase is happening). Expression shifts from focused-scrutinizing to irritated-impatient. He's about to yell.

Lighting: golden hour direct light rim-lighting Liu Hui's shoulder, edge of face, and the invitation letter. Cross-hatched shadow on the side of his face away from the light.

Red pencil annotations: triangle indicating close-up camera position with shallow DOF marker labeled "shallow DOF / bg blur." Arrow showing Liu Hui's head snap-turn labeled "head snaps left → irritated." Annotation near mouth labeled "yells: 够了!把那只青铜兽拆了!"

Caption: "SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — SHALLOW DOF CLOSE-UP / LIU HUI EXAMINES + ORDERS / 85mm prime shallow DOF / camera static with handheld micro-shake / SFX: invitation rustle as it changes hands, then off-frame distant bird-dragon pecking + crab whining continues throughout, Liu Hui: '够了!把那只青铜兽拆了!' / ACTION: receives invitation, examines closely with narrowed eye, then suddenly snaps head left and yells the order"

PANEL 3 (bottom-left) — SHOT 3: POST-WHIP-PAN REVEAL / BIRD-DRAGON + CRAB BALL FREEZE STARING AT CAMERA:
Medium-wide shot of the ship deck open area. Off-axis composition: bird-dragon and crab ball positioned in the left 2/3 of frame, the rest of the deck space + ship's wheel (bronze ship wheel) visible in the right portion as deep background context.

This panel is the END of a fast WHIP PAN from Panel 2's framing — the camera has just whipped from Liu Hui's close-up position over to this new deck area framing in roughly 0.5 seconds. The whip pan transition is indicated in the captions and shooting plan.

BIRD-DRAGON (positioned in middle-left of frame): the independent mechanical bird-dragon, war-mount sized mechanical raptor form, full bronze mech armor, mechanical raptor head helmet, single #4FC3D9 cyan-glowing eye on forehead, mechanical beak with #8E6F3D polished bronze highlight, partly extended wings showing #6B4A2E ochre crimson feathers with #2E1F15 dark brown feather tips. Posed in mid-chase posture: body leaning forward, neck extended, beak forward, claws planted on deck with slight forward weight. Body language reads as "about to peck/chase." BUT — head and eye are turned toward the CAMERA, eye locked on camera, completely frozen mid-action.

CRAB BALL (positioned slightly to the right of bird-dragon on the deck, lower in frame): the bronze crab ball in its closed sphere form, roughly head-sized sphere, #7A6845 bronze olive shell with #846035 ancient gold etched glyph facets and #4FC3D9 cyan joint seam glow. Strictly maintained in ball form — no legs, no eyes visible. Positioned on the deck where it was just rolling. Frozen completely still, no rolling motion. Although crab ball has no visible face, its overall posture/orientation reads as "staring at camera" by slight tilt toward the camera direction.

BOTH bird-dragon and crab ball are completely FROZEN — caught mid-action by the sudden whip pan — they both look toward the camera in absurd comedic stillness. This is the comedy peak beat — chase-and-prey freeze together in Tom-and-Jerry style.

Background: deep deck space visible behind the pair, with the BRONZE SHIP WHEEL visible in the deep right portion of frame as a clear environmental landmark. Some other deck details (rope coils, deck cleats, perhaps part of mast base) visible. Background recedes in slight perspective.

Lighting: golden hour direct light rim-lighting bird-dragon's wing feathers and crab ball's curve. Cross-hatched shadow on opposite sides. The bronze ship wheel in deep background catches some light highlights.

Red pencil annotations: triangle indicating medium-wide camera position post-whip-pan. Whip-pan motion indicator at the panel's left edge labeled "WHIP PAN ENDS HERE from Liu Hui's POV direction." Frozen-still emphasis lines around bird-dragon and crab ball labeled "FREEZE BEAT / both stare at camera." Arrow pointing at the bronze ship wheel in deep background labeled "Bronze Ship Wheel (continuity anchor)."

Caption: "SHOT 3 / PANEL 3 — POST-WHIP-PAN MEDIUM-WIDE / COMEDIC FREEZE / 35mm wide / whip pan from Panel 2 in ~0.5 seconds settles into static framing with handheld micro-shake / SFX: whip pan whoosh + sudden silence as both freeze, no more pecking, no more crab whining — comedic dead silence / ACTION: post-whip-pan reveal of bird-dragon mid-chase + crab ball mid-roll, both freeze and stare at camera, holds for beat"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW:
Top-down schematic diagram of the ship deck local area on the aged paper.

Layout shows the deck floor space. Schematic outlines from top to bottom:
- Upper portion: ship's outer railing edge + beyond it a label "DESERT VIEW BEYOND"
- Middle portion (deck open area): on one side, a small figure-outline of LIEUTENANT kneeling beside a horizontal outline of JIANG LIU lying flat on his back; on the OTHER side a small figure-outline of LIU HUI standing upright
- Bottom-middle portion (other deck area): two small schematic outlines marked "BIRD-DRAGON" and "CRAB BALL" positioned near a small circular outline labeled "BRONZE SHIP WHEEL"

Three red pencil triangles marking camera positions for the 3 shots:
- Shot 1 (slight low-overhead on Lieutenant + Jiang Liu) — positioned looking down at Lieutenant's kneeling area
- Shot 2 (shallow DOF close-up on Liu Hui) — positioned facing Liu Hui's standing position with arrow indicating shallow DOF cone
- Shot 3 (post-whip-pan medium-wide on bird-dragon + crab) — positioned facing the bird-dragon + crab area

Red pencil curved arrow connecting Shot 2's camera position to Shot 3's camera position, labeled "WHIP PAN ~0.5s — Liu Hui's POV swings from invitation examination to the chase area."

Red pencil dashed eye-line from Liu Hui's figure-outline to the bird-dragon + crab area labeled "Liu Hui's POV direction → triggers whip pan."

Hand-lettered labels: "S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS," "JIANG LIU FLAT ON BACK," "LIEUTENANT KNEELS," "LIU HUI STANDS," "WHIP PAN PATH," "BRONZE SHIP WHEEL."

Caption: "SHOOTING PLAN — 3 shots on ship deck / S1+S2 are hard-cut on Lieutenant→Liu Hui exchange / S2→S3 is whip pan triggered by Liu Hui's head-turn yell / S3 lands on comedic freeze with bird-dragon + crab ball"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red pencil for annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour high-contrast — dense cross-hatch shadows + bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each panel strictly 16:9, total image strictly 16:9 with 2x2 grid
- Aged off-white paper texture with subtle edge wear
- NONE of 3 panels use centered symmetric composition
- Jiang Liu in PANEL 1: lying FLAT ON HIS BACK (face-up supine position), NOT side-lying, only partial body visible at left edge of frame (head and face NOT in frame)
- Lieutenant in PANEL 1: high braided ponytail with beads, scar across face, bronze mechanical prosthetic right leg
- Liu Hui in PANEL 2: short spiky black hair with forehead braid cord, eye-patch over right eye, bronze mechanical prosthetic right arm, bare upper torso with tribal tattoos, cream tribal scarf
- Liu Hui in PANEL 2 background must be shallow DOF / heavily blurred bokeh — bottom band reads as out-of-focus ship railing, middle band as desert horizon, upper band as sunset sky #C8581F
- Bird-dragon in PANEL 3: mechanical raptor form, ochre crimson wing feathers, dark brown feather tips, cyan eye, polished bronze beak — FROZEN mid-chase posture but head turned toward camera
- Crab ball in PANEL 3: strictly bronze sphere form (NO legs, NO eyes visible), with ancient gold facets and cyan joint seam glow — FROZEN, no rolling motion
- Bronze Ship Wheel must be visible in PANEL 3 deep background as environmental landmark
- The WHIP PAN motion from PANEL 2 to PANEL 3 is indicated via red pencil annotations in both panels and the shooting plan

**🀄 中文版**

```
一张绘制在做旧米白色纸张上的手绘铅笔素描故事板，专业电影制作风格。图片宽高比严格为 16:9。

版式：2x2 网格，共 4 格，每格严格为 16:9 横向，带粗重的手绘黑色墨水画框边框。上排：分镜 1、分镜 2。下排：分镜 3、拍摄方案示意图。每格下方有一条手写衬线大写字母的说明文字条。

绘制技法：松散的石墨铅笔，配合自信的建筑透视。用交叉排线表现明暗。仅在箭头、强调标记、机位、运动矢量、注释处选择性地使用红色彩铅。做旧米白色纸张，边缘略有磨损。手写说明文字采用略带不齐的衬线大写字母。

整体光照：傍晚黄金时刻，低角度太阳作为主光，来自一个统一的方向。角色剪影上有强烈的轮廓光，背光一侧为深暗的阴影区。阴影处用密集交叉排线，高光处保留明亮的留白纸面。色调对比强烈——并非均匀打光。

整体构图规则：避免居中对称取景。全部 3 个分镜都采用偏轴、不对称、三分法或对角线构图。没有任何一格让主体处于正中央。

风格参考：漫威影业动作分镜板、好莱坞电影故事板、《疯狂的麦克斯：狂暴之路》角色节拍板、《乐一通》甩镜转场分镜板。

顶部居中标题，自信的手写衬线大写字母："WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10D — INVITATION FOUND + LIU HUI'S ORDER + WHIP PAN TO STUNNED PAIR"

标题下方，较小的手写副标题："3 SHOTS / 2 HARD CUTS + 1 WHIP PAN / SHIP DECK / LIEUTENANT FINDS INVITATION → LIU HUI INSPECTS → ORDERS BIRD-DRAGON DISMANTLED → WHIP PAN REVEALS COMEDIC FREEZE"

分镜 1（左上）——镜头 1：略带俯角的低位顶视 / 副手跪在昏迷的姜流身旁，从他衣物里抽出邀请函：
略带俯角的低位顶视（机位约位于地面以上 20-30 度，略微俯视甲板地面区域）。聚焦于副手跪姿身体的中近景。偏轴构图：副手占据画面从中偏左到右的部分，姜流仅在画面最左缘局部可见，露出身体的局部（躯干、上臂的一部分、他奶油色飘扬衣袖的边缘）——姜流的脸和头不入画。

姜流局部可见：平躺仰卧在甲板木板上（面朝上的仰卧姿势），酒红色皮革马甲外搭奶油色宽松飘扬衬衫，酒红色长裤——只有部分躯干和手臂在画面最左缘可见，头部不入画。

副手（跪在姜流身旁，占据画面从中到右的部分）：中年女性，深色头发扎成高高的编辫马尾，缠着饰有珠子和小挂饰的部落布质头带，脸上有一道疤，古铜色晒黑肤色，磨旧的深棕色皮革无袖上衣，奶油色颈巾，手臂和腰部有部落血红色缎带与深红色布料点缀，从髋部到脚为青铜机械义肢右腿。髋部腰带上有一对弯刀（不画出）。单膝跪在姜流身旁。

捕捉的动作：副手的手刚从姜流的衣物里抽出一封邀请函，动作进行中。她手里握着邀请函，正要将手臂抬起。她的另一只手可能正撑在姜流的胸口区域稳住身体。

邀请函：在副手手中画成一个小巧的长方形华丽物件——其确切的视觉设计将在视频制作阶段由资产参考图指定，但就故事板而言，画成一个扁平的长方形信件状小物件，大小约相当于一张扑克牌，边缘有细微的装饰花纹，暗示这是一份皇室文书。

本格的最后一拍：副手的手正抬起，将邀请函朝画面外空间的右上方向递出（刘辉站在画外那个方向）。副手的视线跟随她抬起的手，向右上方画外方向望去。

背景：甲板木板 #6B4830，沿轻微透视向远处延伸。空气中有尘埃微粒。一些散落的杂物（松散的布料碎片、断裂的零件）。船的结构件（盘绕的绳索、桅杆底座、甲板系缆桩）在背景纵深处可见。

光照：黄金时刻直射光从一个方向，给副手的肩膀和握着邀请函的抬起的手打上轮廓光。背光一侧为交叉排线阴影。

红色铅笔注释：左下方一个三角形，标示略带俯角的低位顶视机位。副手手上一个箭头，标示抽取并抬起的动作，标注"pulls invitation from garment → raises toward off-frame Liu Hui"。从副手向右上画外的视线虚线箭头，标注"Lieutenant looks up at Liu Hui"。

说明文字："SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — SLIGHT LOW-OVERHEAD ANGLE / LIEUTENANT FINDS INVITATION / 50mm prime / camera mostly static with handheld micro-shake / SFX: fabric rustle as letter is pulled, Lieutenant: '老大,找到了,皇帝的邀请函。' / ACTION: Lieutenant pulls invitation from Jiang Liu's garment, raises arm to extend it toward off-frame Liu Hui, eye-line follows hand"

分镜 2（右上）——镜头 2：浅景深特写 / 刘辉端详邀请函 + 转头吼叫：
特写取景，仅上半身（腰部以上到头部）。浅景深——刘辉清晰对焦，背景大幅虚化为散景。偏轴构图：刘辉占据画面右侧 2/3，身体呈 3/4 侧角，他的脸略微转向手中（齐胸高度握着）的邀请函。

刘辉默认形态的上半身：裸露的上半身躯干，肩膀和胸口有部落纹身，奶油色部落颈巾松松地绕在脖子上、垂在一侧肩头，部落血红色围巾点缀，黑色短硬发配额前编绳，右眼戴眼罩、眉骨有疤穿过，从肩膀往下可见青铜机械义肢右臂。

捕捉的动作：刘辉用双手（左侧血肉之手 + 右侧机械义肢之手共同）齐胸握着邀请函，他唯一可见的那只眼睛眯起，专注地审视，头略微低下看着邀请函。表情：专注、审视。

背景虚化：大幅失焦的散景。从画面底部到顶部，背景读起来依次如下（全部处于浅景深虚化中）：
- 画面底缘：失焦的船内栏杆或甲板边缘结构（用对比度降低的柔和铅笔笔触表现）
- 中段：失焦的、船外远处的沙漠地平线（暖色淡棕色带）
- 上段：失焦的黄金时刻天空，落日橙色 #C8581F（用柔和交叉排线表现）

背景的深远纵深让观者能读出"刘辉正站在船甲板上，外面是远处的沙漠景观"，无需清晰的背景细节。

本格中的转场拍：在最后一拍，刘辉突然把头猛地转向左侧（朝另一个画外方向，那里正在上演鸟龙 + 螃蟹的追逐）。表情从专注审视转为恼怒不耐。他正要吼叫。

光照：黄金时刻直射光给刘辉的肩膀、脸的边缘和邀请函打上轮廓光。脸的背光一侧为交叉排线阴影。

红色铅笔注释：一个三角形标示特写机位，配浅景深标记，标注"shallow DOF / bg blur"。一个箭头表示刘辉猛地转头，标注"head snaps left → irritated"。嘴部附近一处注释，标注"yells: 够了!把那只青铜兽拆了!"。

说明文字："SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — SHALLOW DOF CLOSE-UP / LIU HUI EXAMINES + ORDERS / 85mm prime shallow DOF / camera static with handheld micro-shake / SFX: invitation rustle as it changes hands, then off-frame distant bird-dragon pecking + crab whining continues throughout, Liu Hui: '够了!把那只青铜兽拆了!' / ACTION: receives invitation, examines closely with narrowed eye, then suddenly snaps head left and yells the order"

分镜 3（左下）——镜头 3：甩镜后揭示 / 鸟龙 + 螃蟹球定格凝视镜头：
船甲板开阔区域的中宽景。偏轴构图：鸟龙和螃蟹球位于画面左侧 2/3，甲板空间其余部分 + 船舵（青铜船舵）在画面右侧作为纵深背景可见。

本格是从分镜 2 取景快速甩镜的终点——镜头刚刚从刘辉的特写位置以约 0.5 秒的速度甩到这片新的甲板区域取景。甩镜转场在说明文字和拍摄方案中标示。

鸟龙（位于画面中偏左）：独立行动的机械鸟龙，战骑体型的机械猛禽形态，全身青铜机甲护甲，机械猛禽头盔，额头有一只青色发光的眼睛 #4FC3D9，机械喙带打磨光亮的青铜高光 #8E6F3D，半展开的翅膀露出赭红色羽毛 #6B4A2E、羽尖深褐色 #2E1F15。摆出追逐途中的姿态：身体前倾，脖颈伸出，喙朝前，爪子扎在甲板上、重心略向前。肢体语言读起来像"正要啄/追逐"。但——头和眼睛都转向镜头，眼睛锁定镜头，动作进行到一半完全定格。

螃蟹球（位于鸟龙右侧偏一点的甲板上，在画面下方）：青铜螃蟹球处于闭合的球形态，约头部大小的球体，青铜橄榄色外壳 #7A6845，带古代暗金蚀刻纹饰刻面 #846035 和青色关节缝隙辉光 #4FC3D9。严格保持球形态——不露腿、不露眼。位于它刚刚滚到的甲板上。完全静止定格，没有滚动。虽然螃蟹球没有可见的脸，但其整体姿态/朝向通过略微朝镜头方向倾斜，读起来像"凝视镜头"。

鸟龙和螃蟹球两者都完全定格——被突如其来的甩镜在动作中抓个正着——它们都朝镜头看去，呈现荒诞的喜剧式静止。这是喜剧高潮拍——追逐者与猎物一同定格，汤姆与杰瑞式风格。

背景：这一对身后可见甲板的深远空间，青铜船舵在画面右侧纵深处清晰可见，作为明确的环境地标。还有一些其他甲板细节（盘绕的绳索、甲板系缆桩，或许还有部分桅杆底座）可见。背景沿轻微透视向远处延伸。

光照：黄金时刻直射光给鸟龙的翅膀羽毛和螃蟹球的弧面打上轮廓光。背光一侧为交叉排线阴影。纵深背景里的青铜船舵接到一些高光。

红色铅笔注释：一个三角形标示甩镜后的中宽机位。本格左缘一个甩镜运动标识，标注"WHIP PAN ENDS HERE from Liu Hui's POV direction"。鸟龙和螃蟹球周围的定格静止强调线，标注"FREEZE BEAT / both stare at camera"。一个箭头指向纵深背景里的青铜船舵，标注"Bronze Ship Wheel (continuity anchor)"。

说明文字："SHOT 3 / PANEL 3 — POST-WHIP-PAN MEDIUM-WIDE / COMEDIC FREEZE / 35mm wide / whip pan from Panel 2 in ~0.5 seconds settles into static framing with handheld micro-shake / SFX: whip pan whoosh + sudden silence as both freeze, no more pecking, no more crab whining — comedic dead silence / ACTION: post-whip-pan reveal of bird-dragon mid-chase + crab ball mid-roll, both freeze and stare at camera, holds for beat"

拍摄方案（右下）——俯视鸟瞰总览：
做旧纸面上船甲板局部区域的俯视示意图。

版式呈现甲板地面空间。从上到下的示意轮廓：
- 上部：船的外栏杆边缘 + 其外侧一个标注"DESERT VIEW BEYOND"
- 中部（甲板开阔区域）：一侧是副手跪姿的小人形轮廓，旁边是平躺仰卧的姜流的水平轮廓；另一侧是刘辉直立站姿的小人形轮廓
- 中下部（另一片甲板区域）：两个小示意轮廓，分别标注"BIRD-DRAGON"和"CRAB BALL"，位于一个标注"BRONZE SHIP WHEEL"的小圆形轮廓附近

三个红色铅笔三角形标示 3 个镜头的机位：
- 镜头 1（对副手 + 姜流的略带俯角低位顶视）——位于俯视副手跪姿区域的位置
- 镜头 2（对刘辉的浅景深特写）——位于正对刘辉站位的位置，配箭头标示浅景深视锥
- 镜头 3（对鸟龙 + 螃蟹的甩镜后中宽景）——位于正对鸟龙 + 螃蟹区域的位置

一条红色铅笔弧形箭头连接镜头 2 的机位到镜头 3 的机位，标注"WHIP PAN ~0.5s — Liu Hui's POV swings from invitation examination to the chase area"。

一条红色铅笔虚线视线从刘辉的人形轮廓引向鸟龙 + 螃蟹区域，标注"Liu Hui's POV direction → triggers whip pan"。

手写标注："S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS"、"JIANG LIU FLAT ON BACK"、"LIEUTENANT KNEELS"、"LIU HUI STANDS"、"WHIP PAN PATH"、"BRONZE SHIP WHEEL"。

说明文字："SHOOTING PLAN — 3 shots on ship deck / S1+S2 are hard-cut on Lieutenant→Liu Hui exchange / S2→S3 is whip pan triggered by Liu Hui's head-turn yell / S3 lands on comedic freeze with bird-dragon + crab ball"

硬性约束：
- 不要数字绘画，不要 3D 渲染，除红色铅笔用于注释外不要任何色彩叠加
- 不要摄影元素，不要平滑渐变
- 强烈的黄金时刻高对比——密集交叉排线阴影 + 明亮留白纸面高光，并非均匀打光
- 每格严格 16:9，整幅图像严格 16:9，采用 2x2 网格
- 做旧米白色纸张质感，边缘略有磨损
- 3 个分镜没有一个采用居中对称构图
- 分镜 1 里的姜流：平躺仰卧（面朝上的仰卧姿势），不是侧卧，只有部分身体在画面最左缘可见（头和脸不入画）
- 分镜 1 里的副手：带珠子的高编辫马尾，脸上有一道疤，青铜机械义肢右腿
- 分镜 2 里的刘辉：黑色短硬发配额前编绳，右眼眼罩，青铜机械义肢右臂，裸露上半身配部落纹身，奶油色部落颈巾
- 分镜 2 里刘辉的背景必须是浅景深 / 大幅虚化散景——底带读作失焦的船栏杆，中带为沙漠地平线，上带为落日天空 #C8581F
- 分镜 3 里的鸟龙：机械猛禽形态，赭红色翅膀羽毛，深褐色羽尖，青色眼睛，打磨光亮的青铜喙——追逐途中定格，但头转向镜头
- 分镜 3 里的螃蟹球：严格的青铜球形态（不露腿、不露眼），带古代暗金刻面和青色关节缝隙辉光——定格，没有滚动
- 青铜船舵必须在分镜 3 的纵深背景中作为环境地标可见
- 从分镜 2 到分镜 3 的甩镜运动通过两格及拍摄方案中的红色铅笔注释标示
```

### 6（15738 字）

```
A 3x3 grid of 9 cinematic black-and-white film stills, total canvas aspect ratio 16:9. Each of the 9 panels is itself a 16:9 cinematic frame, separated by clean thin black borders (2-3px) with small white margins between panels. Small panel numbers "01" through "09" rendered in clean sans-serif font in the top-left corner of each panel. Read order: left-to-right, top-to-bottom.

CRITICAL RENDERING DIRECTIVE: These are NOT line art, NOT manga panels, NOT sketches, NOT concept illustrations. These are PHOTOREALISTIC CINEMATIC STILL PHOTOGRAPHS rendered in pure black-and-white grayscale only - as if shot on a black-and-white digital cinema camera. Every panel must look like a frame extracted from a live-action martial arts film. Full continuous grayscale tonal range from pure black to pure white with all midtones. Real photographic depth of field, lens characteristics, motion blur where appropriate, atmospheric haze, sub-surface scattering on skin, accurate material reflectance on metal/leather/fabric/sand, fine film grain texture overlay. Color information is the ONLY thing missing - everything else must read as a real photograph from a real film production.

Photographic specifications: shot on ARRI Alexa Mini LF equivalent, anamorphic widescreen lenses, T2.8 aperture giving cinematic shallow depth of field where appropriate, Kodak Double-X 5222 black-and-white film stock aesthetic with subtle grain, natural directional daylight as primary lighting source (consistent direction across all 9 panels - sun from upper-left), no stylized hatching or graphic effects whatsoever.

MAIN CHARACTER (must be identical across all 9 panels - lock the design):
Lean young Asian female warrior. Short black bob haircut with straight blunt bangs covering forehead, fine hair strands visible. Hair ornament: a small red braided tassel hanging from the left side of her hair near a hairpin. Pale skin with realistic photographic texture. Sharp dark eyes.

Costume:
- White ragged cropped shirt with frayed torn hem, midriff exposed, fabric showing real cotton weave texture
- Long worn black leather coat (NOT a cloak) reaching ankle-length, with dark red interior lining visible at the lapels and when the coat flares in motion. Coat has heavy weathered texture, faded red Chinese calligraphy character printed on the back, metal chain decorations across the shoulder and back, multiple leather pouches and small buckles attached
- Tattered dark fabric short skirt with frayed lower edge, layered with deep red fabric beneath, metal chain belt at waist with hanging wooden charm tags inscribed with faded Chinese characters
- Black ripped fishnet stockings (the legs are bare otherwise - no greaves, no armor plating on the legs)
- Black thigh straps wrapping the upper thigh
- Long black leather gloves extending past the elbows
- Black leather high-heel ankle boots with thick stacked heels (do not invent additional details about boot construction - render them as realistic dark leather heeled boots)

Weapon: an oversized heavy sword roughly equal to her own height. The sword has an industrial-mechanical design - exposed metal pipes and tubing running along the spine, brass rivets, weathered dark metal surface with visible wear marks, the guard area is bulky with mechanical components, faded red painted text markings on the blade flat. The blade itself is broad and heavy-looking. This is a single signature weapon - keep its design absolutely consistent across all panels.

Overall aesthetic of the character: Eastern mechanical-punk, lived-in and weathered, real costume on a real actress - photoreal not stylized.

OPPONENTS (consistent design across all panels where they appear): 7 desert raiders, all male, lean and weathered. Faces fully wrapped in tattered dust-cloth with only narrow eye slits visible. Layered armor of stitched leather plates over coarse cloth wrappings, hanging bone amulets and small metal trinkets, weathered leather belts. Each wields a curved single-edged saber with crude wrapped grip and simple metal guard. Their builds are sinewy. Render them as real actors in real costumes, not as graphic designs.

ENVIRONMENT: vast windswept desert. Layered dune ridges receding to a distant horizon line. Scattered weathered rocks half-buried in sand. Distant rock formations as silhouettes on the horizon. Vast sky with thin high-altitude cirrus clouds. Strong directional sunlight from upper-left creating long hard-edged shadows consistent across all 9 panels. Wind blowing sand particles consistently from right to left across all panels. Real photographic atmospheric haze increasing with distance.

GRAYSCALE RENDERING REQUIREMENTS:
- Skin tones: midtone grays with subtle highlight gradients, realistic sub-surface light scattering, no graphic outlines
- Black leather coat / boots / gloves: deep blacks with realistic highlight rolloff where light catches the surface, visible leather grain texture in close-ups
- White cropped shirt: bright midtone to highlight range, realistic cotton fabric texture
- Heavy sword: dark metallic grays with sharp specular highlights along the blade edges, brass fittings rendered slightly lighter
- Red elements (coat lining, calligraphy on coat back, fabric beneath skirt): rendered as medium-dark grays but with enough tonal separation from black to remain readable
- Sand: bright midtone with fine granular texture in foreground, smooth gradient surfaces in midground, atmospheric haze in background
- Sky: lighter gray gradient with cloud detail rendered as soft tonal variations
- Shadows: deep but with detail retention - not crushed pure black
- Highlights: bright but with detail retention - not blown out pure white
- Motion blur on fast-moving elements (sword swings, sand, fabric in motion): realistic photographic motion blur, not stylized speed lines
- Fine film grain overlay across the entire image for cinematic texture

PANEL 01 [Extreme wide shot / High aerial angle]:
The warrior stands on the crest of a high dune, viewed from behind and slightly above, her full body silhouetted against the bright sky. Heavy sword stabbed vertically into the sand to her right, her left hand resting loosely on the pommel. The dark red interior lining of her coat is visible as the wind catches and lifts the left side of the coat outward. Below her, the dune slopes down dramatically into a wide valley. In the far distance at the valley floor, 7 tiny figures advance in spread formation - small but clearly readable as armed human silhouettes. The sky occupies the upper two-thirds of the frame with thin streaked cirrus clouds. Her long hard shadow stretches across the dune ridge. Airborne sand particles streak across the frame. Deep focus - everything sharp from foreground to horizon.

PANEL 02 [Medium shot / Eye level, very slight low angle]:
Tight medium shot of the lead opponent from chest up. His cloth-wrapped face fills the frame with only his narrow sharp eyes visible through the wrap. His curved saber rests across his right shoulder. His left hand is raised mid-gesture, fingers spread, signaling his men to flank. Strong backlight from the upper-left rim-lights the edges of his head wrap and shoulders with a bright highlight, his front in shadow but with detail retention. Behind him in soft-focused background, 4 other figures visible in spread formation as out-of-focus silhouettes. Dust streaks blow across the frame from right to left. Shallow depth of field isolates the leader.

PANEL 03 [Extreme low angle / Vertical upward composition]:
A very low camera angle, ground level. In the lower portion of the frame: an extreme close-up of the warrior's boots planted firmly in the sand, individual sand grains compressed and displaced visibly around the boot edges. The boots are dark and heeled (render based on the established character design - do not invent fabricated details). Rising vertically from beside the boots through the entire frame: the heavy sword, blade flat facing camera, every rivet and mechanical pipe along its length visible in sharp focus, surface wear and engraved markings catching a sharp specular highlight from the upper-left sun. At the top edge of the frame: the partial underside of the warrior's chin and lower face, mostly out of frame. Background: out-of-focus dune slope with atmospheric haze.

PANEL 04 [Wide shot / Behind-character three-quarter angle / Dynamic motion]:
The warrior viewed from a behind-right three-quarter angle, captured mid-charge, her body rotated through a powerful horizontal sword swing. The heavy sword is extended out to her left in a wide arc, the blade frozen at the moment of full extension with realistic photographic motion blur trailing the swing. The sword's path has thrown up a curving wall of sand particles - rendered as a dense cloud of individual airborne grains catching the light. Three opponents in the path of the swing are knocked backward by the displaced air and sand wall - their bodies tumbling away from the warrior, sabers knocked from their hands tumbling through the air alongside loose cloth fragments from their tattered wrappings. Her coat fully extended behind her with the red lining flared open, real fabric motion. Other opponents visible in the background reacting and backing away. Low horizon line, sky compressed to upper sliver. The visual focus is on the sand wall and the force of the swing, not on body damage - no wounds, no blood, no torn flesh, just opponents being knocked back by force.

PANEL 05 [Low angle / Strict profile tracking shot / Sharp diagonal composition]:
The warrior in strict profile from her left side, her body angled into a powerful diagonal sword swing. The heavy sword is swung from upper-right to lower-left of the frame, the blade caught at the exact moment of meeting a foreground opponent's raised saber that he holds up defensively across his chest. Sparks burst from the metal-on-metal contact point as individual radiating photographic particles. The opponent's saber is bending under the force of the blow, his body recoiling backward from the impact, his feet sliding through the sand. In the background in sharp focus: a second opponent rushing in with raised saber, his cloth face wraps fluttering with his motion. The warrior's face partially visible: cold expression, controlled, mouth slightly set, hair caught by motion. Low camera angle. The focus is metal-on-metal impact and force - no body damage, no wounds.

PANEL 06 [Extreme close-up / Eyes only / Horizontal letterbox composition within the 16:9 panel]:
An extreme close-up of just the warrior's eyes occupying the full width of the panel. Black bob hair strands fall across the upper forehead, individual strands rendered with photographic detail. Both eyes rendered in extreme detail: realistic iris texture and pattern, pupils, lower lash line, micro-tension at the outer eye corner, subtle skin texture under the eyes. The skin tone is rendered as soft midtone grays. In the right pupil, a tiny but clearly legible reflection: the silhouette of an opponent in mid-motion with raised saber, rendered with enough sharpness to be readable. Expression: absolute cold focus, no anger, no fear, no exertion - just precision. The background is soft-focused into abstract darkness, isolating the eyes completely.

PANEL 07 [Medium shot / Three-quarter front angle / Symmetrical pressure composition]:
The warrior in a deep kneeling stance on her right knee, left leg braced forward, the heavy sword held horizontally across her chest as a guard in both hands. Two curved sabers come down onto the flat of her blade from upper-left and upper-right simultaneously - she is blocking both strikes. Both contact points burst with photographic spark particles radiating outward. The two attacking opponents visible at the left and right edges of the frame, their bodies leaning into the strike with visible muscular effort, faces showing strain through their cloth wraps. Two more opponents compressing in from the extreme left and right edges of the frame with sabers raised, preparing to strike. The warrior's expression: focused, jaw set, controlled. Her coat pooled around her on the sand, real fabric weight and fold detail. The sand around her shows pressure displacement under her stance. Focus on the moment of defense, the sparks of metal blocking metal.

PANEL 08 [Extreme low angle / Ground-level upward / Explosive vertical composition]:
Camera positioned at sand level looking sharply upward. The warrior bursting upward from her kneeling position into a standing position, the heavy sword swung in a powerful upward semicircular arc, the blade tracing a path across the upper half of the frame with realistic motion blur. Two opponents who had been pressing down on her are now thrown upward and backward by the force of her rising counter-strike, their bodies tumbling backward through the air with arms and legs splayed in tumbling motion, their sabers knocked loose from their grips spinning away. Her coat fully extended behind her at the apex of the motion, the red lining flared wide. Her face visible from below: teeth slightly bared in effort, hair lifted by the upward movement, expression showing controlled intensity. Background: dramatic sky with cloud detail filling the upper frame. Sand particles thrown up around her feet in a cloud. The focus is the explosive force of her rising movement - opponents are tumbling, not damaged. No body injuries visible.

PANEL 09 [Extreme wide shot / High aerial angle / Mirror of Panel 01]:
EXACT same camera position, angle, and lens as Panel 01. The warrior now stands at the exact center of the frame on the dune crest, the heavy sword stabbed vertically into the sand to her right, her left hand on the pommel - identical posture to Panel 01 but mirrored in position within the frame. Her coat now hangs still, minimal wind motion. Surrounding her in a loose radius on the sand: 7 fallen opponents lying motionless on the dune in varied resting poses - they have been defeated and are lying still on the sand. Each fallen figure is rendered in clear photographic detail but with no visible injuries, no blood, no damage - they simply lie still on the sand as if asleep or unconscious, their loose clothing settled around them, their sabers fallen beside them. Long sunset shadows stretched dramatically across the dunes - the shadow direction has shifted to suggest end-of-day light (lower sun angle, longer shadows than Panel 01). The sky now compressed in the upper portion with denser cloud cover suggesting a lower sun. Atmospheric haze rendered with photographic depth. The visual rhyme with Panel 01 must be exact: same warrior posture, same sword position, same dune crest, same camera. The DIFFERENCE: she is now centered, the figures who were approaching her in Panel 01 are now still and fallen around her feet, and the light has changed.

CRITICAL FINAL DIRECTIVE: Every single panel must look like a real photograph from a real live-action martial arts film production. Pure black-and-white photographic grayscale only. Fine film grain texture across all panels. Consistent lighting direction across all panels. Consistent character design across all panels. The action is choreographed martial arts - emphasis on force, motion, and impact, NOT on injury or damage. No blood. No wounds. No torn flesh. No graphic injury detail. The opponents are knocked back, blocked, thrown - they are not visibly hurt. This is stylized cinematic action like classic wuxia films, not graphic violence.

Output: 16:9 final canvas, 3x3 grid of 9 equal panels, thin black grid borders with small white margins, panel numbers "01"-"09" visible in top-left corners.
```

**🀄 中文版**

```
一幅 3x3 的九宫格，由 9 帧电影感黑白剧照组成，整张画布宽高比为 16:9。9 个画格中的每一格本身都是一帧 16:9 的电影画面，画格之间以干净纤细的黑色边框（2-3px）分隔，画格与画格之间留有细窄的白色间距。每个画格的左上角用干净的无衬线字体标注小小的画格编号「01」到「09」。阅读顺序：从左到右，从上到下。

关键渲染指令：这些不是线稿，不是漫画分格，不是速写，不是概念插画。它们是纯黑白灰度的照片级电影感静帧照片——仿佛由一台黑白数字电影摄影机拍摄。每一格都必须看起来像是从一部真人武打电影中截取的画面。从纯黑到纯白、涵盖所有中间调的连续灰度层次。真实的摄影景深、镜头特性、必要处的运动模糊、空气中的雾霭、皮肤的次表面散射、金属/皮革/织物/沙砾上准确的材质反射、细腻的胶片颗粒纹理叠加。唯一缺失的只有色彩信息——其余一切都必须读起来像是来自真实电影制作的真实照片。

摄影规格：以等同于 ARRI Alexa Mini LF 的机器拍摄，变形宽银幕镜头，T2.8 光圈在适宜处带来电影感的浅景深，柯达 Double-X 5222 黑白胶片质感并带细微颗粒，自然的定向日光作为主光源（9 个画格中光照方向一致——太阳来自左上方），完全不带风格化的排线或图形化效果。

主角（在全部 9 个画格中必须完全一致——锁定造型设计）：
精瘦的亚洲年轻女战士。黑色短发波波头，齐刘海平直地盖住额头，发丝纤细可见。发饰：一条小小的红色编织流苏从她左侧靠近发簪处垂下。苍白皮肤，带真实的照片级肌理。锐利的深色眼眸。

服装：
- 白色破旧短上衣，下摆磨损撕裂，露出小腹，布料呈现真实的棉织纹理
- 一件磨旧的黑色长皮风衣（不是斗篷），长及脚踝，翻领处以及风衣随动作扬起时可见暗红色内衬。风衣有厚重的做旧质感，背后印有一个褪色的红色汉字书法，肩部和背部有金属链条装饰，挂着多个皮革小包和小扣具
- 破烂的深色短裙，下缘磨损脱线，内层叠着深红色布料，腰间系金属链腰带，垂挂着刻有褪色汉字的木质护身符牌
- 黑色撕破的渔网袜（腿部其余裸露——没有护胫，腿上没有任何护甲片）
- 黑色大腿绑带缠绕在大腿上部
- 黑色长皮手套，长度超过手肘
- 黑色皮革高跟短靴，配厚实的叠层鞋跟（不要臆造关于靴子做工的额外细节——按真实的深色皮革高跟靴渲染）

武器：一把超大号的重剑，长度大致与她本人身高相当。这把剑采用工业机械风格设计——剑脊沿线裸露着金属管路与导管，黄铜铆钉，做旧的深色金属表面带可见磨痕，护手区域厚重并嵌有机械组件，剑身平面上有褪色的红漆文字标记。剑身本身宽阔而厚重。这是一件独一无二的标志性武器——其设计在所有画格中务必保持绝对一致。

角色整体美学：东方机械朋克，饱经使用与风霜，真实女演员身上的真实戏服——照片级写实而非风格化。

对手（在出现它们的所有画格中保持一致设计）：7 名沙漠劫掠者，皆为男性，精瘦而饱经风霜。脸部用破烂的防尘布完全包裹，只露出狭窄的眼缝。层叠的护甲由缝合的皮革片覆盖在粗糙布料缠绕之上，挂着骨制护符和金属小饰物，系着做旧的皮带。每人持一把单刃弯刀，配粗糙缠绕的握柄和简单的金属护手。他们体格筋健。请把他们渲染为真实戏服中的真实演员，而非图形化设计。

环境：广袤的风蚀荒漠。层叠的沙丘脊向远处的地平线退去。零散的风蚀岩石半埋于沙中。远处的岩层在地平线上呈剪影。辽阔天空中有高空稀薄的卷云。来自左上方的强烈定向阳光投下长而硬边的阴影，在全部 9 个画格中保持一致。风将沙粒持续地从右向左吹过所有画格。真实的摄影空气雾霭随距离增加而加重。

灰度渲染要求：
- 肤色：中间调灰色配细微的高光渐变，真实的次表面光散射，无图形化轮廓线
- 黑色皮风衣 / 靴子 / 手套：深黑色，在光线掠过表面处带真实的高光过渡，特写中可见皮革纹理
- 白色短上衣：从中间调到高光的明亮区间，真实的棉布纹理
- 重剑：深色金属灰，沿剑刃边缘有锐利的镜面高光，黄铜配件渲染得略浅
- 红色元素（风衣内衬、风衣背后书法、裙下布料）：渲染为中-深灰色，但与黑色保持足够的色调区分以保持可辨
- 沙地：前景为明亮中间调并带细腻颗粒纹理，中景为平滑的渐变表面，背景为空气雾霭
- 天空：较浅的灰色渐变，云层细节渲染为柔和的色调变化
- 阴影：深暗但保留细节——不要压死为纯黑
- 高光：明亮但保留细节——不要过曝为纯白
- 快速运动元素上的运动模糊（挥剑、扬沙、运动中的布料）：真实的摄影运动模糊，而非风格化速度线
- 整幅图像叠加细腻的胶片颗粒，营造电影质感

画格 01 ［极远景 / 高空俯角］：
女战士站在一道高沙丘的脊顶上，从背后略微俯视拍摄，她的全身在明亮天空的映衬下呈剪影。重剑垂直地插在她右侧的沙中，左手松松地搭在剑柄首端。风掀起她风衣的左侧向外扬开，露出风衣的暗红色内衬。在她下方，沙丘的斜坡戏剧性地下降，通向一片宽阔的谷地。在谷底极远处，7 个微小的身影呈散开队形推进——虽小但清晰可辨为持械的人形剪影。天空占据画面上方三分之二，有稀薄的条状卷云。她又长又硬的影子横铺在沙丘脊上。空中的沙粒划过画面。深景深——从前景到地平线一切皆清晰。

画格 02 ［中景 / 平视，极轻微的仰角］：
对首领对手的紧凑中景，从胸部以上取景。他裹布的脸填满画面，只有透过裹布可见的狭窄锐利眼睛。他的弯刀横搁在右肩上。他的左手抬起，处于手势之中，手指张开，示意手下包抄。来自左上方的强烈逆光为他头巾和肩部的边缘勾出明亮的轮廓光，正面处于阴影中但保留细节。在他身后柔焦的背景里，另有 4 个身影呈散开队形可见，为失焦的剪影。尘土条从右向左吹过画面。浅景深将首领孤立出来。

画格 03 ［极低仰角 / 垂直向上构图］：
极低的摄影机角度，贴近地面。画面下部：女战士靴子的极特写，稳稳地踩在沙中，靴边周围被压实与挤开的沙粒清晰可见。靴子是深色高跟款（依既定角色造型渲染——不要臆造编造的细节）。从靴子旁垂直升起、贯穿整个画面的：那把重剑，剑身平面正对摄影机，沿其长度的每一颗铆钉和机械管路都清晰可辨，表面磨痕与刻印的标记反射出来自左上方阳光的锐利镜面高光。在画面顶缘：女战士下巴和下半脸的局部底面，大部分在画外。背景：失焦的沙丘斜坡，带空气雾霭。

画格 04 ［宽景 / 角色背后四分之三角度 / 动态运动］：
从右后四分之三角度拍摄的女战士，捕捉于冲锋之中，身体随一记强力的水平挥剑而旋转。重剑朝她左方以宽大的弧线伸出，剑身在完全伸展的瞬间被定格，挥砍后拖曳着真实的摄影运动模糊。剑的轨迹掀起一道弯曲的沙粒之墙——渲染为一团密集的、空中飞扬并反光的沙粒。挥砍路径上的三名对手被被推开的气流和沙墙击退——身体翻滚着远离女战士，弯刀脱手在空中翻飞，伴随着从他们破烂裹布上松脱的布片。她的风衣在身后完全展开，红色内衬翻飞敞开，真实的布料动态。背景中可见其他对手在反应并后退。低地平线，天空压缩为顶部一道窄边。视觉重点在沙墙和挥砍的力量上，而非身体伤害——没有伤口，没有血，没有撕裂的皮肉，只有对手被力量击退。

画格 05 ［低角度 / 严格侧面跟拍 / 锐利的对角线构图］：
女战士呈她左侧的严格正侧面，身体倾入一记强力的对角挥剑。重剑从画面右上挥向左下，剑身在恰好迎上一名前景对手举起的弯刀的瞬间被捕捉——那名对手把弯刀防御性地横举在胸前。金属相击的接触点迸出火花，呈一个个向外辐射的摄影级粒子。对手的弯刀在这一击之下弯曲，身体被冲击力震退，双脚在沙中滑动。背景中清晰对焦处：第二名对手举刀冲来，他的裹脸布随动作飘动。女战士的脸局部可见：冷峻的表情，克制，嘴微抿，头发随动作扬起。低摄影机角度。重点在金属对金属的撞击与力量——没有身体伤害，没有伤口。

画格 06 ［极特写 / 仅眼部 / 在 16:9 画格内的横向信箱式构图］：
仅女战士双眼的极特写，占满画格的整个宽度。黑色波波头的发丝垂落在上额，一根根发丝以照片级细节渲染。双眼以极致细节渲染：真实的虹膜纹理与图案、瞳孔、下睫线、眼外角的微小张力、眼下细微的肌肤纹理。肤色渲染为柔和的中间调灰。在右眼瞳孔中，有一处微小却清晰可辨的倒影：一名处于运动中、举刀的对手剪影，渲染得足够锐利以可辨认。表情：绝对的冷峻专注，没有愤怒，没有恐惧，没有用力——只有精准。背景柔焦为抽象的黑暗，将双眼完全孤立。

画格 07 ［中景 / 正面四分之三角度 / 对称压迫式构图］：
女战士以右膝深跪的姿态，左腿前撑，双手将重剑水平横持于胸前作为格挡。两把弯刀同时从左上和右上方砍向她的剑身平面——她正同时格挡两记攻击。两个接触点都迸发出向外辐射的摄影级火花粒子。两名进攻的对手在画面左右两缘可见，身体倾入劈砍，肌肉用力清晰可见，脸上透过裹布显出吃力。另有两名对手从画面最左与最右缘挤入，举刀准备劈砍。女战士的表情：专注，下颌紧绷，克制。她的风衣堆叠在她周围的沙上，真实的布料重量与褶皱细节。她身下的沙地在其站姿下显出受压位移。重点在防御的瞬间，金属格挡金属的火花。

画格 08 ［极低仰角 / 贴地向上 / 爆发式垂直构图］：
摄影机置于沙面高度向上仰拍。女战士从跪姿向上爆发起身成站姿，重剑以一记强力的向上半圆弧挥出，剑身在画面上半部划出一道轨迹，带真实的运动模糊。原本压制她的两名对手此刻被她起身反击的力量向上向后抛飞，身体在空中翻滚后仰，四肢张开呈翻滚之势，弯刀脱手旋飞而去。她的风衣在动作顶点处于身后完全展开，红色内衬大幅翻开。她的脸从下方可见：用力时牙齿微露，头发被上扬的动作掀起，表情显出克制的强烈。背景：戏剧性的天空带云层细节填满画面上部。沙粒在她脚边扬起成团。重点在她起身动作的爆发力——对手是被抛飞，而非受伤。看不到任何身体伤害。

画格 09 ［极远景 / 高空俯角 / 与画格 01 镜像呼应］：
与画格 01 完全相同的摄影机位置、角度与镜头。女战士此刻站在画面的正中央、沙丘脊顶上，重剑垂直插在她右侧的沙中，左手搭在剑柄首端——与画格 01 完全相同的姿态，但在画框内的位置呈镜像。她的风衣此刻静垂，几乎没有风动。在她周围沙地上一个松散的半径范围内：7 名倒下的对手以各种安息的姿势一动不动地躺在沙丘上——他们已被击败，静静地躺在沙中。每个倒下的身影都以清晰的照片级细节渲染，但没有可见伤口，没有血，没有损伤——他们只是静静地躺在沙上，仿佛睡着或昏迷，宽松的衣物在身体周围摊落，弯刀掉在他们身旁。漫长的落日影子戏剧性地横铺在沙丘上——影子方向已经偏移，以暗示一天将尽的光线（更低的太阳角度，比画格 01 更长的影子）。天空此刻压缩在画面上部，云层更为浓密，暗示一轮更低的太阳。空气雾霭以照片级的纵深感渲染。与画格 01 的视觉呼应必须精确：相同的战士姿态、相同的剑的位置、相同的沙丘脊顶、相同的摄影机。差异在于：她此刻居于画面中央，画格 01 中向她逼近的那些身影此刻静止倒在她脚边，且光线已经改变。

关键最终指令：每一个画格都必须看起来像是来自真实真人武打电影制作的真实照片。仅纯黑白摄影灰度。所有画格上都有细腻的胶片颗粒纹理。所有画格的光照方向一致。所有画格的角色设计一致。动作是设计编排的武打——重点在力量、运动与撞击，而非伤害或损伤。没有血。没有伤口。没有撕裂的皮肉。没有图形化的伤害细节。对手是被击退、被格挡、被抛飞——他们没有可见的受伤。这是如经典武侠片般风格化的电影动作，而非血腥暴力。

输出：16:9 最终画布，3x3 九宫格共 9 个等大画格，纤细的黑色网格边框配细窄白色间距，画格编号「01」-「09」显示在各自左上角。
```

1×5 grid layout vertical stack with five equal storyboard panels arranged top to bottom, each panel landscape 16:9 ratio with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. Total sheet aspect ratio 16:45 vertical.

Top header text on the sheet (handwritten style, centered): "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG8 - SAND HUNTERS HOOK PROTAGONIST SHIP STORYBOARD"

Each panel must include:
