PANEL 1 sketch — HIGH ANGLE ESTABLISHING AERIAL:
- Bird's eye view roughly 60 to 70 degrees down angle looking at the desert battlefield
- Center of frame: large protagonist ship as a long rectangular silhouette with nine stick-leg shapes underneath striding forward, sand plume trailing behind in cross-hatched cloud
- Three small sand skimmer triangular shapes positioned around the ship: one trailing left rear, one trailing right rear, one approaching from rear center, all aiming converging trajectories toward the ship's legs
- Each skimmer has a stick figure rider visible on top
- Red pencil arrows: one large arrow showing the ship moving deep into frame toward upper area, three smaller arrows showing each skimmer converging toward the ship
- Background: empty desert horizon line with light cross-hatching for sand texture
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 1 · HIGH ANGLE AERIAL ESTABLISHING / 35mm wide / SFX: ship legs galloping crash, three skimmer engine roars closing in"

PANEL 2 sketch — MEDIUM WIDE THREE SAND HUNTERS THROW HOOKS SIMULTANEOUSLY:
- Side angle medium-wide framing showing three sand skimmers in a row across the frame at slight diagonal arrangement, each skimmer in profile
- Each skimmer has one stick figure sand hunter on top in mid-throwing pose: arm extended forward, body leaning back for momentum, hook rope coiled then extending forward in arc
- Three rope arcs drawn as curved lines extending from each hunter forward and upward into the frame, ropes converging toward upper frame area where the ship leg silhouettes are partially visible at top of frame
- Three small grappling hook shapes visible mid-air at the tips of the rope arcs
- Red pencil arrows: three arrows along each rope showing throw direction toward upper frame
- Background: simple desert horizon line, sand plume cross-hatching beneath each skimmer
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 2 · MEDIUM WIDE 3 HUNTERS THROW HOOKS / 35mm / SFX: three rope whooshes layered, hooks clank-click locking onto metal"

PANEL 3 sketch — LOW ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL ROPES PULL TAUT:
- Extreme low angle camera positioned at sand level looking up at three massive ship leg shapes in foreground occupying most of frame, legs as thick cylindrical stick shapes towering up out of top of frame
- Three taut ropes drawn as straight rigid diagonal lines stretched tight from upper portion of legs (where hooks have locked on) angling down and out toward the lower edges and corners of frame, disappearing off frame edge toward the off-screen sand skimmers
- Vibration marks (small cross-hatch zigzag) drawn along each rope to indicate extreme tension and trembling
- Sand particles drawn as small dots flying around the leg bases
- Red pencil arrows: three arrows along each rope showing pulling tension force pointing outward and down off-frame, one upward arrow on each leg showing the legs still moving forward despite resistance
- Background: small portion of sky visible at top corners, mostly filled with the towering leg shapes
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 3 · LOW ANGLE GROUND ROPES TAUT / 24mm wide / SFX: rope creak under tension, metallic strain groans, sand crunch"

PANEL 4 sketch — WIDE TRACKING SIDE PROFILE TUG OF WAR:
- Side profile wide shot showing the entire battlefield in a horizontal strip
- Center frame: the protagonist ship in side view as a long rectangular silhouette with nine leg shapes underneath striding forward into deep frame direction (toward right side of frame)
- Behind and to the rear of the ship: three sand skimmers visible trailing behind, each connected to the ship by a long rope rendered as taut diagonal line from skimmer to ship leg
- The skimmers are tilted forward-downward indicating they are being dragged forward against their own engine reverse-thrust attempts to brake
- Sand plumes erupting from beneath both the ship legs and the dragging skimmers, the skimmer plumes drawn as larger thicker cross-hatched dust clouds indicating heavier dragging friction
- Stick figure hunters visible on each skimmer leaning back hard, arms extended gripping their rope ends, bodies in maximum effort tug pose
- Red pencil arrows: large arrow showing the ship continuing forward direction at top, three smaller opposing arrows on each skimmer showing the futile reverse pull, all the smaller arrows being overridden by the dominant ship-direction arrow
- Background: desert horizon line stretching across frame
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 4 · WIDE SIDE TRACKING TUG OF WAR / 50mm / SFX: ropes humming under load, skimmer engines whining in reverse, ship leg crash continuous"

PANEL 5 sketch — MEDIUM CLOSE-UP SAND HUNTER TURNS HEAD AT VOICE:
- Medium close-up framing showing one stick figure sand hunter from chest up, on top of his sand skimmer
- The hunter's pose: still gripping the taut rope with both hands leaning back, but his head is in mid-rotation turning sharply over his shoulder toward off-frame direction (model decides which side)
- Facial expression on the simple stick figure head: surprise marks, eyes drawn as wide circles, mouth as small open oval
- Speed lines around the hunter's head indicating sudden head turn motion
- Behind hunter: blurred suggestion of the taut rope still extending off-frame toward upper area where the ship leg connects, blurred sand plume background
- Red pencil arrow: one arrow showing the hunter's head rotation direction toward off-frame
- A small label/note in the panel margin: "off-screen voice: 'WO LAI!' (here I come)"
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 5 · MCU HUNTER REACTION TO OFFSCREEN VOICE / 85mm / SFX: distant offscreen shout 'WO LAI!', rope tension continues, hunter sharp head turn whoosh"

Art direction: classic animation-school storyboard aesthetic — pencil line-work for primary subjects, grey marker shading for shadows and atmosphere (sand, dust, smoke), red colored pencil for arrows and motion annotations only. Loose expressive strokes throughout, intentionally rough, NOT polished, NOT clean digital line art, NOT colored illustration, NOT painted artwork.

Constraints: All five panels must remain inside one single sheet image. Maintain the 1x5 vertical grid layout with each panel landscape 16:9. Keep stick figure abstraction consistent across all panels — NO detailed faces, NO realistic anatomy, NO detailed mecha designs, NO color other than red pencil for arrows and black/grey for sketch. Handwritten panel info strips must be readable plain typography. NO logos, NO watermarks, NO extra decorative elements.

一张专业的手绘素描故事板单页，铅笔与墨水插画风格，粗放的艺术线条，用交叉排线表现阴影，松散而富有表现力的笔触，绘制在泛黄奶油／米白纸张纹理背景上的单色黑白图像。

1×5 网格纵向堆叠布局，五个等大的故事板分镜自上而下排列，每个分镜为横向 16:9 比例，带有粗重的手绘黑色墨水画框边框。整页宽高比为 16:45 纵向。

分镜 2 草图——中近景三名沙猎者同时抛出钩索：

- 每个滑橇顶部有一个火柴人沙猎者处于抛掷中途的姿势：手臂前伸，身体后倾蓄力，钩索绳先盘绕后呈弧线向前延展
- 三条绳索弧线绘成曲线，从各猎者向前向上延伸入画面，绳索朝画面上部汇聚，那里飞船腿部剪影在画面顶部部分可见
- 三个抓钩小形状可见于绳索弧线尖端的半空中
- 红色铅笔箭头：沿每条绳索三个箭头，表现抛掷方向朝向画面上部

分镜 3 草图——低角度贴地绳索绷紧：

- 沿每条绳索绘有振动记号（细小的交叉排线锯齿），表现极度张力与颤动

- 红色铅笔箭头：沿每条绳索三个箭头，表现拉拽张力向外、向下指出画面，每条腿上一个向上箭头，表现腿部不顾阻力仍在前行
- 背景：顶部角落可见一小片天空，其余大部分被高耸的腿部形状填满

分镜 4 草图——宽景跟拍侧面拔河：

- 飞船后方与尾部：三个沙地滑橇尾随可见，每个都由一条绳索连向飞船，绳索绘成从滑橇到飞船腿的绷紧对角线
- 滑橇向前下方倾斜，表明它们正被向前拖拽，与自身引擎为制动而反向推进的尝试相抗
- 沙尘羽云从飞船腿和被拖拽的滑橇下方喷涌而出，滑橇的尘羽绘成更大更厚的交叉排线尘云，表明更重的拖拽摩擦
- 每个滑橇上可见火柴人猎者奋力后仰，手臂前伸紧握各自绳索末端，身体呈竭尽全力的拔河姿势
- 红色铅笔箭头：顶部一个大箭头表现飞船持续前行方向，每个滑橇上三个较小的反向箭头表现徒劳的反向拉拽，所有较小箭头都被占主导的飞船方向箭头压过
- 背景：横贯画面的沙漠地平线
- 下方手写标签："PANEL 4 · WIDE SIDE TRACKING TUG OF WAR / 50mm / SFX: ropes humming under load, skimmer engines whining in reverse, ship leg crash continuous"

分镜 5 草图——中近景沙猎者闻声转头：
- 中近景取景，呈现一个火柴人沙猎者的胸部以上，立于他的沙地滑橇之上
- 猎者的姿势：仍双手紧握绷紧的绳索后仰，但他的头部正在中途急剧转动，越过肩膀朝向画面外方向（由模型决定哪一侧）
- 简单火柴人头部上的面部表情：惊讶记号，眼睛绘成圆睁的圆圈，嘴绘成微张的小椭圆
- 猎者头部周围的速度线表现突然转头的动作
- 猎者身后：模糊地暗示绷紧的绳索仍朝画面外上部延伸、连向飞船腿，背景为模糊的沙尘羽云
- 红色铅笔箭头：一个箭头表现猎者头部朝画面外的转动方向
- 分镜边缘一个小标签／批注："off-screen voice: 'WO LAI!' (here I come)"
- 下方手写标签："PANEL 5 · MCU HUNTER REACTION TO OFFSCREEN VOICE / 85mm / SFX: distant offscreen shout 'WO LAI!', rope tension continues, hunter sharp head turn whoosh"

艺术指导：经典动画学院故事板美学——主要主体用铅笔线条，阴影与氛围（沙、尘、烟）用灰色马克笔明暗，红色彩铅仅用于箭头和运动标注。通篇松散而富有表现力的笔触，刻意粗放，不要精致打磨，不要干净的数字线稿，不要彩色插画，不要绘画作品。

约束：全部五个分镜必须保留在同一张单页图像内。维持 1x5 纵向网格布局，每个分镜为横向 16:9。在所有分镜中保持一致的火柴人抽象——不要细致面容，不要写实解剖，不要细致的机甲设计，除箭头用红色彩铅、素描用黑／灰外不要其他颜色。手写分镜信息条必须是可读的朴素排版。不要 logo，不要水印，不要多余的装饰元素。

### 8（17609 字）

```
A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, in the style of professional film production storyboards. Total image aspect ratio strictly 21:9.

Layout: 2 columns x 2 rows grid on aged paper background. Each of the 4 cells contains one element. Top row (left to right): Panel 1, Panel 2. Bottom row (left to right): Panel 3, Shooting Plan diagram. Each of Panels 1-3 is strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. The Shooting Plan cell in the bottom-right is a top-down bird's eye view of the desert battlefield aftermath with camera positions and 180-degree axis line marked.

Beneath each storyboard panel inner frame is a hand-lettered caption strip showing panel number, lens specification, shot type, and brief SFX/action notes in serif block letters slightly uneven.

The drawing technique is loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective and clear cinematic composition. Sketches are deliberately gestural but every spatial relationship and prop detail is clearly readable. Selective red colored pencil arrows and emphasis marks indicate movement direction, force vectors, and key dramatic beats.

LIGHTING NOTE FOR ALL PANELS: late afternoon desert golden hour, low sun key light from upper-left 30 degrees. Dense cross-hatching dominates most surfaces representing the shadow base of the warm desert atmosphere. Selective bright untouched paper zones represent the directional sun-key highlights on metal armor surfaces and rim-lit silhouettes. Strong tonal contrast throughout — deep blacks in shadow zones, bright untouched paper for highlights, NOT evenly lit.

Style references: Wes Anderson production storyboards, traditional Hollywood film storyboard aesthetic, animator's pre-visualization sketches, Mad Max Fury Road action sequence boards, Coen Brothers character beat storyboards.

Drawing rules:
- Pencil graphite on warm off-white aged paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use
- Black ink for panel borders and hand-lettered captions
- Red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera position triangles, force vectors, and 180-degree axis line
- Cross-hatching for shading; bright highlight zones use untouched paper
- Hand-lettered captions in serif-style block letters, slightly uneven

DIRECTIONAL AXIS RULE: continuing from prior segment 9B, the protagonist ship has fallen into the deep distance of the battlefield (toward one consistent direction, henceforth "deep direction"). Liu Hui stands as the anchor in the foreground area (opposite the ship's direction). Three sand skimmers have all been thrown off in the aftermath chaos — two of them lie tipped over in the foreground-middle area near Liu Hui. The action axis runs from Liu Hui through the fallen ship; cameras stay on one consistent side of this axis throughout the sequence.

Title at top center of entire image, hand-lettered in confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10A — AFTERMATH & LEAP"

Below title, hand-lettered subtitle in smaller letters: "MULTI-SHOT HARD CUT / 3 SHOTS / 3 STORYBOARD PANELS + SHOOTING PLAN / RAIDER REACTION BEATS + LIU HUI'S LEAP"

PANEL 1 (top-left cell) — SHOT 1: OLD RAIDER + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED AGAINST OVERTURNED SKIMMER, SHARED LOOK:
Scene composition: medium-wide two-shot framing. In foreground-middle of frame: a sand skimmer lies tipped on its side on the desert ground — the triangular streamlined hull is rolled roughly 80-90 degrees onto one side, the bottom of the skimmer with its jet-propulsion vents now exposed to the side facing the camera, the upper edge of the hull pointing skyward. Two Sand Hunter raiders sit on the desert sand leaning their backs against the upturned underside of this skimmer:

- LEFT raider — old raider — a middle-aged man with a long braided ponytail of dark hair hanging over one shoulder, a long full dark beard, a weathered scarf wrapped around his neck, a leather and brown fabric tunic, his back propped against the skimmer's underside, knees drawn up, his bow slung across his back partly visible, one hand resting on his knee. Face streaked with sand and dust. Expression: stunned blank stare into middle distance.

- RIGHT raider (sitting slightly to the right of the old raider, both leaning against the same overturned skimmer) — young raider — a younger man with short cropped dark hair, short stubble beard, a scarf around his neck, leather vest with crossed strap rigging, his mechanical bronze lasso-launcher gauntlet visible on one forearm, back propped against the skimmer, sand and dust on his face and clothing. Expression: stunned blank stare into middle distance, same direction as the old raider.

Both raiders are looking off-frame toward the upper-right direction (toward the unseen fallen ship in the deep distance, but the ship itself is NOT in this panel's frame — the panel is tightly composed on just the two characters and the overturned skimmer). Their two heads are at slightly different heights but their eye-lines are aimed at the same off-frame point. They have just witnessed something enormous.

Background: desert sand floor extends behind them with some scattered debris pieces (a snapped piece of rope, a torn fragment of fabric, small sand-dust still hovering in the air). In the far distance behind the raiders the horizon line is visible with golden hour atmospheric haze cross-hatched densely. The background middle-distance contains a faint suggestion of the second tipped-over skimmer (heavily abstracted, partial sketch only — just a triangular shape lying on its side).

Late afternoon sun comes from upper-left at low angle, casting strong rim-lighting on the right edge of both raiders' silhouettes, leaving the left side of their faces in deep cross-hatched shadow. The overturned skimmer's exposed underside catches the sun on its upper edge.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow indicating the static medium-wide camera framing. Red pencil dashed eye-line vectors from BOTH raiders extending toward the upper-right edge of frame, converging on a single off-frame point — labeled "shared off-frame eye-line." A small red pencil curved arrow on each raider's head indicating a slow simultaneous turn motion at the end of the shot — labeled "slow simultaneous head-turn / look at each other."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — TWO-SHOT MEDIUM-WIDE / OLD + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED AGAINST TIPPED SKIMMER / 50mm prime / static framing with handheld micro-shake / SFX: distant settling dust, residual far-off creaking from fallen ship, soft desert wind, two simultaneous synchronized exhales / ACTION: both look off-frame in shared direction, then slow simultaneous head-turn to look at each other"

**HARD CUT.**

PANEL 2 (top-right cell) — SHOT 2: FEMALE RAIDER CRAWLS OUT OF FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER, COUGHING:
Scene composition: medium-wide framing. In the middle of frame: a sand skimmer lies completely overturned on the desert sand — fully inverted, the bottom-side now pointing skyward, the open-top deck pressed down into the sand. One side of the skimmer's hull has crumpled inward from the impact, creating an opening near the sand level. Through this opening at the lower edge of the skimmer's hull, the female raider is in the process of crawling out — her upper body is already free of the skimmer, propping herself up on one forearm on the sand, her other hand pushing against the skimmer's hull edge for leverage. Her lower body is still partly inside the wreck.

The female raider has dark hair pulled back in a high ponytail with steampunk-style brass goggles pushed up on her forehead, her face and hair are heavily covered in sand and dust, sand streaks down her face, her leather vest is dirty and partly torn at the shoulder, the cartridge-belt rigging across her chest is rumpled. Her face is in the act of coughing — head turned slightly down and to one side, mouth open, eyes squeezed shut, one shoulder hunched up as her body shakes from the cough.

Around the female raider's emergence point: a thin cloud of sand-dust hangs in the air from the recent impact. Small debris scattered nearby — a broken piece of skimmer hull, some scattered cartridge shells, a strap of leather. The desert sand under the wreck shows deep furrows from where the skimmer skidded before tipping fully.

Background: desert floor extends behind the wreck. In the deep distance, far off-frame to the upper edge of the panel, the silhouette of the fallen protagonist ship is faintly suggested — a low collapsed shape on the horizon, partially obscured by lingering dust plume rising. Dense cross-hatched golden hour sky.

Late afternoon sun from upper-left rim-lights the curve of the overturned skimmer's hull and the female raider's shoulder and the loose strands of her hair. Her face is mostly in cross-hatched shadow with the rim-lit edge catching the highlight.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow indicating low-medium camera angle (slightly lower than the female raider's torso level — looking slightly up at her emergence). Red pencil emphasis marks around the cough — short radial lines around her mouth labeled "cough burst + dust puff." Red pencil arrow on her arm-push gesture labeled "leverage out of wreck."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — MEDIUM-WIDE / FEMALE RAIDER CLIMBS OUT OF FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER / 50mm prime / handheld with light shake / SFX: muffled metallic creak from skimmer hull settling, fabric scrape, sharp coughing burst from female raider, sand grains pattering off her hair and clothes / ACTION: pushes upper body free of wreck, coughs hard, dust briefly visible around her face"

**HARD CUT.**

PANEL 3 (bottom-left cell) — SHOT 3: LIU HUI'S CROUCH-AND-LEAP, LOW-ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL TO OVERHEAD VANISH:
Scene composition: extreme low-angle ground-level shot — the camera is placed flat on the desert sand, looking straight up. Liu Hui stands directly above the camera, looking down toward it with the lens. The entire frame is dominated by the underside view of Liu Hui's massive fusion-form armored body — the camera sees up between his legs and up at his chest, neck, and the underside of his jaw and chin.

The framing shows: the desert sand floor at the very bottom edge of frame extending outward (the ground around Liu Hui's planted feet). Liu Hui's massive bronze-mechanized armored boots planted on either side of the camera position, occupying the lower-left and lower-right corners of the frame. His armored legs rise upward through the frame in dramatic foreshortening — the legs' bronze armor segments visible getting wider near the hips as they recede into perspective. His armored torso, chest, and the bronze spiral horns on his shoulders are visible higher up in the frame, dramatically foreshortened with the chest mass dominating the middle of the frame. His face — looking downward toward the camera — is visible at the top of the frame, the chin and lower jaw most prominent, eye-patch over the right eye, the un-patched left eye narrowed in concentration, his face only partially visible due to the extreme perspective.

Liu Hui is in the crouch-prep position: knees bent, body lowered into a deep loaded crouch — like a standing-long-jump pre-leap stance, both feet planted shoulder-width apart, weight loaded into the legs, arms lowered to his sides slightly behind his body. The cyan core at his chest is visible from below as a strong glow point on the underside of his chest plate. The cyan cores at his shoulders also glow.

This is the MOMENT BEFORE the leap — the storyboard captures the crouch-prep pose. A small inset diagram in the upper-right corner of the panel (drawn as a tiny secondary sketch, smaller than 1/5 of the panel area) shows the SAME camera looking up an instant later — Liu Hui has launched into the air, leaving only the empty sky frame with his receding silhouette small and going up toward the upper portion of the inset's frame, with a massive sand-dust eruption blooming from where his feet just left the ground. Below the inset's frame is a tiny caption: "AFTER LEAP — VANISHES UPWARD."

Background sky visible in the gaps between Liu Hui's body and limbs: dense cross-hatched golden hour atmosphere, the late afternoon sun visible as a small bright disc in the upper-left region of the sky where his shoulder doesn't block it.

Camera annotation: red pencil triangle at the very bottom of the panel indicating "camera planted flat on sand / looking straight up." Red pencil downward arrow on Liu Hui's loaded crouch labeled "weight loaded / pre-leap crouch / Hulk-style standing jump prep." Red pencil upward arrows around Liu Hui's body indicating the imminent leap direction labeled "leap vector upward + toward fallen ship direction." Red pencil radial burst marks around Liu Hui's feet labeled "imminent sand eruption."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 3 / PANEL 3 — EXTREME LOW-ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL / LIU HUI'S CROUCH-AND-LEAP / 18mm ultra-wide / camera planted flat on sand looking straight up / SFX: deep loaded armor compression groan, low gear-grinding from shoulder spiral horns, cyan core building hum, then explosive sand THOOM burst as legs release, body whoosh upward / ACTION: deep crouch loaded prep, then leap straight up — Liu Hui vanishes out the top of the frame, leaving sand eruption"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right cell) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW:
Drawn as a clean top-down schematic diagram on the aged paper. Layout uses the entire cell width effectively. Upper portion of the diagram (deep distance direction): the fallen protagonist ship drawn as a rectangular schematic silhouette tilted forward with several leg-marks splayed in collapsed kneeling positions — marked with a small dust-plume cross-hatched cloud above it labeled "FALLEN SHIP / SETTLING DUST." 

Middle-foreground area of the diagram: Liu Hui drawn as a small figure-outline standing firm on the sand, his orientation facing toward the fallen ship in the upper direction. Liu Hui's position labeled "LIU HUI ANCHOR / CROUCH-LEAP POSITION."

Around Liu Hui's position, scattered in the middle-foreground area: three small schematic outlines of tipped-over sand skimmers. One labeled "TIPPED SKIMMER A / OLD + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED HERE" with two tiny figure-outlines beside it. Another labeled "FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER / FEMALE RAIDER CRAWLING OUT" with one tiny figure-outline at its edge. A third labeled "TIPPED SKIMMER B (FAR)" drawn smaller and further back.

Three red pencil triangles around the action area marking the camera positions for each of the 3 shots: 
- Shot 1 (medium-wide on tipped skimmer with old + young raider) positioned facing the raiders on the side opposite their eye-line direction
- Shot 2 (medium-wide on fully overturned skimmer with female raider) positioned at the side of her emergence point
- Shot 3 (extreme low-angle ground-level under Liu Hui) marked as a red triangle pointing straight up from a position directly underneath Liu Hui's standing point
Each red triangle is labeled with the shot number. 

A dashed red pencil straight line running from Liu Hui upward through the diagram toward the fallen ship — this is the 180-degree axis line, labeled "180° AXIS LINE / LIU HUI → FALLEN SHIP DIRECTION."

A red pencil curved arrow from Liu Hui's position curving upward and toward the fallen ship direction labeled "LIU HUI LEAP TRAJECTORY → toward ship deck (next segment)."

Hand-lettered labels on the diagram: "180° AXIS LINE", "S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS", "LIU HUI ANCHOR", "FALLEN SHIP DEEP DIRECTION", "3 TIPPED/OVERTURNED SKIMMERS WITH 3 RAIDERS", "LEAP TRAJECTORY OUTGOING."

Caption strip below the shooting plan cell: "SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED FROM SEG 9B / 3 hard-cut shot positions / S1 + S2 are reaction beats on raiders / S3 is Liu Hui's transition into next segment"

PRODUCTION NOTES at bottom edge of overall image (hand-lettered serif block letters along the bottom margin of the aged paper, outside the panel grid): "WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 10A — AFTERMATH & LEAP / multi-shot hard cut sequence / continuity: prior segment 9B closes on the fallen ship and Liu Hui standing firm after release / this segment opens on the raider aftermath beats and ends with Liu Hui's crouch-leap toward the fallen ship / next segment 10B opens on the ship deck POV as Liu Hui lands"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting style, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red colored pencil for arrows and annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO AI-style smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour desert high-contrast lighting in all panels — dense cross-hatch shadows + selective bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each storyboard panel's inner frame strictly 16:9 aspect ratio
- Total image strictly 21:9 aspect ratio
- All hand-lettered text in confident serif block letters with slight unevenness — readable but not machine-printed
- Aged off-white paper texture throughout with subtle edge wear
- Old raider has long braided ponytail + long full beard; Young raider has short cropped hair + short stubble — these two are visually distinct from each other in PANEL 1
- Female raider has high ponytail + brass goggles on forehead — clearly different from the other two
- Liu Hui's crouch in PANEL 3 must read as Hulk-style standing-long-jump preparation — loaded but not strained or laboring
- PANEL 3's extreme low-angle ground-level perspective must be unambiguous — the camera is on the sand looking straight up, Liu Hui towers over the camera
```

**🀄 中文版**

```
一张绘制在泛黄米白纸张上的手绘铅笔素描故事板，采用专业电影制作故事板的风格。整张图像宽高比严格为 21:9。

排版：泛黄纸张背景上的 2 列 x 2 行网格。4 个格子各包含一个元素。上排（从左到右）：分镜 1、分镜 2。下排（从左到右）：分镜 3、拍摄方案示意图。分镜 1–3 各自严格为 16:9 横向横画幅，带有粗重的手绘黑色墨水画框边框。右下角的拍摄方案格子是沙漠战场余波的俯视鸟瞰图，标注了机位与 180 度轴线。

每个故事板分镜内框下方都有一条手写说明文字条，以略微参差的衬线粗体字母标出分镜编号、镜头规格、景别，以及简短的音效／动作备注。

绘制技法是松散的石墨铅笔，带有自信的建筑式透视和清晰的电影感构图。素描刻意带有手势感，但每一处空间关系和道具细节都清晰可辨。有选择地用红色彩铅箭头和强调记号标示运动方向、力的矢量和关键戏剧节拍。

所有分镜的光照备注：午后晚些时候的沙漠黄金时刻，低位太阳主光从左上方 30 度照入。密集的交叉排线主导大部分表面，表现温暖沙漠氛围的阴影底色。有选择地保留明亮、未涂抹的纸面区域，表现金属铠甲表面的定向太阳主光高光和轮廓光勾勒的剪影。整体色调对比强烈——阴影区是深黑，高光是明亮、未涂抹的纸面，绝非均匀打光。

风格参考：韦斯·安德森（Wes Anderson）的制作故事板、传统好莱坞电影故事板美学、动画师的预演素描、《疯狂的麦克斯：狂暴之路》（Mad Max Fury Road）的动作段落分镜板、科恩兄弟（Coen Brothers）的人物节拍故事板。

绘制规则：
- 在温暖的米白泛黄纸张背景上以石墨铅笔绘制，边缘略有磨损、带有淡淡的污渍，暗示大量制作使用的痕迹
- 黑色墨水用于分镜边框和手写说明文字
- 红色彩铅仅用于箭头、强调记号、机位三角形、力的矢量和 180 度轴线
- 交叉排线用于明暗处理；明亮高光区使用未涂抹的纸面
- 手写说明采用衬线风格的粗体字母，略微参差

方向轴规则：承接前一段落 9B，主角飞船已坠入战场的深远处（朝向一个一致的方向，此后称"深远方向"）。刘辉作为锚点站立在前景区域（与飞船方向相反）。三艘沙地滑橇在余波的混乱中全部被甩翻——其中两艘倾覆躺在靠近刘辉的前景中段区域。动作轴从刘辉穿过坠落的飞船；整个段落中机位始终保持在这条轴线一致的一侧。

整张图像顶部居中的标题，以自信的粗体衬线字母手写："WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10A — AFTERMATH & LEAP"

标题下方，以较小字母手写的副标题："MULTI-SHOT HARD CUT / 3 SHOTS / 3 STORYBOARD PANELS + SHOOTING PLAN / RAIDER REACTION BEATS + LIU HUI'S LEAP"

分镜 1（左上格）——镜头 1：老掠夺者 + 年轻掠夺者倚靠翻倒的滑橇，共同望向同一处：
画面构图：中近景双人取景。画面前景中段：一艘沙地滑橇侧翻倒在沙漠地面上——三角形流线型船体大致翻滚了 80–90 度倒向一侧，滑橇底部连同其喷射推进口此刻朝向镜头一侧暴露在外，船体上缘指向天空。两名沙猎掠夺者坐在沙漠沙地上，背靠着这艘翻倒滑橇朝上的底面：

- 左侧掠夺者——老掠夺者——一名中年男子，深色头发编成长长的辫子马尾垂在一侧肩上，留着浓密的深色长络腮胡，颈间裹着一条饱经风霜的围巾，身穿皮革与棕色织物的束腰外衣，背靠在滑橇底面，双膝收起，背上斜挎的弓部分可见，一只手搭在膝上。脸上沾满沙土。表情：呆滞茫然地凝视中景远处。

- 右侧掠夺者（坐在老掠夺者略偏右处，两人都倚靠同一艘翻倒的滑橇）——年轻掠夺者——一名较年轻的男子，深色短发剪得很短，留着短短的胡茬，颈间围着围巾，皮革背心配交叉绑带索具，一只前臂上可见他那机械青铜套索发射器护臂，背靠滑橇，脸上和衣服上沾满沙土。表情：呆滞茫然地凝视中景远处，与老掠夺者方向相同。

两名掠夺者都望向画面外右上方向（朝向深远处看不见的坠落飞船，但飞船本身并不在本分镜画面内——本分镜紧凑地只构图于这两个角色和翻倒的滑橇）。他们两人的头部高度略有不同，但视线对准同一个画面外的点。他们刚刚目睹了某种巨大的事物。

背景：沙漠沙地在他们身后向外延伸，散落着一些残骸碎片（一段折断的绳索、一片撕裂的织物碎片、仍悬浮在空中的细小沙尘）。掠夺者身后的远处可见地平线，黄金时刻的大气雾霭以密集交叉排线表现。背景中段隐约暗示第二艘倾覆的滑橇（高度抽象化，仅为局部素描——只是一个侧躺的三角形轮廓）。

午后晚些时候的太阳从左上方低角度照入，在两名掠夺者剪影的右缘投下强烈的轮廓光，使他们脸部左侧落入深沉的交叉排线阴影。翻倒滑橇暴露在外的底面上缘承接阳光。

机位标注：红色铅笔箭头标示静态中近景取景。从两名掠夺者引出的红色铅笔虚线视线矢量延伸至画面右上缘，汇聚于一个画面外的单一点——标注为"shared off-frame eye-line"（共同的画面外视线）。每名掠夺者头部各有一个红色铅笔弧形小箭头，标示镜头末尾缓慢的同步转头动作——标注为"slow simultaneous head-turn / look at each other"（缓慢同步转头／彼此对视）。

分镜下方说明文字条："SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — TWO-SHOT MEDIUM-WIDE / OLD + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED AGAINST TIPPED SKIMMER / 50mm prime / static framing with handheld micro-shake / SFX: distant settling dust, residual far-off creaking from fallen ship, soft desert wind, two simultaneous synchronized exhales / ACTION: both look off-frame in shared direction, then slow simultaneous head-turn to look at each other"

**硬切。**

分镜 2（右上格）——镜头 2：女掠夺者从完全翻倒的滑橇中爬出，咳嗽：
画面构图：中近景取景。画面正中：一艘沙地滑橇完全翻倒在沙漠沙地上——彻底底朝天倒置，底面此刻指向天空，敞口的顶部甲板被压进沙中。滑橇船体的一侧因撞击向内凹陷，在贴近沙面处形成一个开口。女掠夺者正从船体下缘的这个开口处爬出——她的上半身已脱离滑橇，一只前臂撑在沙地上，另一只手抵住船体边缘借力。她的下半身仍有部分留在残骸内。

女掠夺者深色头发束成高马尾，蒸汽朋克风格的黄铜护目镜推在额头上，脸和头发上沾满厚厚的沙土，沙痕沿脸颊流下，皮革背心肮脏、肩部部分撕裂，横跨胸前的弹药带索具皱乱不堪。她正在咳嗽——头略微低下偏向一侧，张着嘴，双眼紧闭，一侧肩膀因咳嗽而身体颤抖、耸起。

女掠夺者爬出的位置周围：因刚才的撞击，空中悬着一层薄薄的沙尘云。附近散落着小块残骸——一块折断的滑橇船体碎片、几枚散落的弹壳、一条皮革绑带。残骸下方的沙漠沙地上有深深的犁沟，是滑橇彻底翻倒前滑行留下的。

背景：残骸后方沙漠地面向外延伸。在深远处、画面上缘以外的远方，隐约暗示坠落主角飞船的剪影——地平线上一个低伏、塌陷的形体，被仍在升腾的残留尘羽部分遮挡。黄金时刻的天空以密集交叉排线表现。

午后晚些时候的太阳从左上方为翻倒滑橇的船体曲面、女掠夺者的肩膀和她散乱的发丝勾勒轮廓光。她的脸大部分处于交叉排线阴影中，轮廓勾边承接高光。

机位标注：红色铅笔箭头标示中低机位角度（略低于女掠夺者躯干高度——略微仰视她爬出）。咳嗽处周围的红色铅笔强调记号——她嘴部周围的短放射状线条，标注为"cough burst + dust puff"（咳嗽爆发 + 尘埃喷出）。她手臂撑推动作上的红色铅笔箭头，标注为"leverage out of wreck"（借力爬出残骸）。

分镜下方说明文字条："SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — MEDIUM-WIDE / FEMALE RAIDER CLIMBS OUT OF FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER / 50mm prime / handheld with light shake / SFX: muffled metallic creak from skimmer hull settling, fabric scrape, sharp coughing burst from female raider, sand grains pattering off her hair and clothes / ACTION: pushes upper body free of wreck, coughs hard, dust briefly visible around her face"

**硬切。**

分镜 3（左下格）——镜头 3：刘辉蹲伏蓄力一跃，从贴地低角度到向上消失于头顶：
画面构图：极低角度贴地镜头——摄影机平放在沙漠沙地上，笔直向上仰拍。刘辉正站在摄影机正上方，连同镜头一起朝下看向它。整个画面被刘辉巨大融合形态铠甲身躯的仰视景象主导——摄影机从他双腿之间向上看去，仰视他的胸膛、脖颈以及下颌和下巴的底面。

取景所示：画面最底缘是向外延伸的沙漠沙地（刘辉双脚踏稳处周围的地面）。刘辉巨大的青铜机械化铠甲战靴踏在摄影机位置两侧，占据画面左下角和右下角。他的铠甲双腿在戏剧化的透视缩短中向画面上方升起——腿部的青铜铠甲分段可见，随着向纵深退去、在臀部附近愈发变宽。他的铠甲躯干、胸膛，以及双肩上的青铜螺旋角在画面更高处可见，戏剧化地透视缩短，胸部体量主导画面中央。他的脸——朝下看向摄影机——在画面顶部可见，下巴和下颌最为突出，右眼戴着眼罩，未戴眼罩的左眼因专注而眯起，由于极端透视，他的脸仅部分可见。

刘辉处于蹲伏蓄力姿势：双膝弯曲，身体降低成深深蓄力的蹲姿——如立定跳远前的预跃姿态，双脚与肩同宽踏稳，重心压入双腿，双臂垂落于身体两侧略偏后。他胸前的青色核心从下方可见，是胸甲底面上一个强烈的发光点。他双肩的青色核心也在发光。

这是起跳前的那一刻——故事板捕捉的是蹲伏蓄力的姿态。分镜右上角有一个小型插入示意图（绘成一幅微小的次级素描，小于分镜面积的 1/5），展示同一摄影机在片刻之后向上仰拍——刘辉已腾空跃起，只留下空荡的天空画面与他渐渐远去的小小剪影朝插图画面上部升去，他双脚刚刚离地之处涌起巨大的沙尘爆发。插图画面下方有一行微小说明："AFTER LEAP — VANISHES UPWARD."（起跳之后——向上消失）

刘辉身体与四肢的缝隙间可见背景天空：黄金时刻的密集交叉排线大气，午后晚些时候的太阳在天空左上区域、他肩膀未遮挡之处显现为一个明亮的小圆盘。

机位标注：分镜最底部的红色铅笔三角形标注"camera planted flat on sand / looking straight up"（摄影机平放在沙地上／笔直向上仰拍）。刘辉蓄力蹲姿上的红色铅笔向下箭头，标注"weight loaded / pre-leap crouch / Hulk-style standing jump prep"（重心压入／起跳前蹲伏／绿巨人式立定跳准备）。刘辉身体周围的红色铅笔向上箭头标示即将到来的起跳方向，标注"leap vector upward + toward fallen ship direction"（起跳矢量向上 + 朝向坠落飞船方向）。刘辉双脚周围的红色铅笔放射状爆发记号，标注"imminent sand eruption"（即将爆发的沙土）。

分镜下方说明文字条："SHOT 3 / PANEL 3 — EXTREME LOW-ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL / LIU HUI'S CROUCH-AND-LEAP / 18mm ultra-wide / camera planted flat on sand looking straight up / SFX: deep loaded armor compression groan, low gear-grinding from shoulder spiral horns, cyan core building hum, then explosive sand THOOM burst as legs release, body whoosh upward / ACTION: deep crouch loaded prep, then leap straight up — Liu Hui vanishes out the top of the frame, leaving sand eruption"

拍摄方案（右下格）——俯视鸟瞰总览：
绘成泛黄纸张上一张清晰的俯视示意图。布局有效利用整个格子宽度。示意图上部（深远方向）：坠落的主角飞船绘成一个矩形示意剪影，向前倾倒，数条腿部痕迹以塌陷跪伏的姿态张开——上方标注一小团交叉排线尘羽云，标注为"FALLEN SHIP / SETTLING DUST"（坠落飞船／沉降尘埃）。

示意图中段-前景区域：刘辉绘成一个站立稳固在沙地上的小型人形轮廓，朝向上方坠落飞船的方位。刘辉位置标注为"LIU HUI ANCHOR / CROUCH-LEAP POSITION"（刘辉锚点／蹲伏起跳位置）。

刘辉位置周围，散布在中段-前景区域：三艘倾覆沙地滑橇的小型示意轮廓。一艘标注"TIPPED SKIMMER A / OLD + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED HERE"（翻倒滑橇 A／老 + 年轻掠夺者倚靠于此），旁边有两个微小人形轮廓。另一艘标注"FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER / FEMALE RAIDER CRAWLING OUT"（完全翻倒滑橇／女掠夺者爬出），其边缘有一个微小人形轮廓。第三艘标注"TIPPED SKIMMER B (FAR)"（翻倒滑橇 B〔远处〕），绘得更小、更靠后。

动作区域周围三个红色铅笔三角形，标记 3 个镜头各自的机位：
- 镜头 1（中近景拍倾覆滑橇上的老 + 年轻掠夺者）置于面向掠夺者、与他们视线方向相反的一侧
- 镜头 2（中近景拍完全翻倒滑橇上的女掠夺者）置于她爬出位置的一侧
- 镜头 3（刘辉脚下极低角度贴地）标记为一个从刘辉站立点正下方笔直向上指的红色三角形
每个红色三角形都标注镜头编号。

一条红色铅笔虚直线从刘辉向上穿过示意图朝向坠落飞船——这是 180 度轴线，标注为"180° AXIS LINE / LIU HUI → FALLEN SHIP DIRECTION"（180° 轴线／刘辉 → 坠落飞船方向）。

一条红色铅笔弧形箭头从刘辉位置向上、朝坠落飞船方向弯曲，标注为"LIU HUI LEAP TRAJECTORY → toward ship deck (next segment)"（刘辉起跳轨迹 → 朝向飞船甲板〔下一段落〕）。

示意图上的手写标签："180° AXIS LINE"（180° 轴线）、"S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS"（S1／S2／S3 机位）、"LIU HUI ANCHOR"（刘辉锚点）、"FALLEN SHIP DEEP DIRECTION"（坠落飞船深远方向）、"3 TIPPED/OVERTURNED SKIMMERS WITH 3 RAIDERS"（3 艘翻倒／倾覆滑橇与 3 名掠夺者）、"LEAP TRAJECTORY OUTGOING"（向外起跳轨迹）。

拍摄方案格子下方说明文字条："SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED FROM SEG 9B / 3 hard-cut shot positions / S1 + S2 are reaction beats on raiders / S3 is Liu Hui's transition into next segment"

整张图像底缘的制作备注（沿泛黄纸张底部边距、在分镜网格之外手写的衬线粗体字母）："WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 10A — AFTERMATH & LEAP / multi-shot hard cut sequence / continuity: prior segment 9B closes on the fallen ship and Liu Hui standing firm after release / this segment opens on the raider aftermath beats and ends with Liu Hui's crouch-leap toward the fallen ship / next segment 10B opens on the ship deck POV as Liu Hui lands"

硬性约束：
- 不要数字绘画风格，不要 3D 渲染，除箭头和标注用的红色彩铅外不要任何颜色叠加
- 不要摄影元素，不要 AI 风格的平滑渐变
- 所有分镜都采用强烈的黄金时刻沙漠高对比光照——密集交叉排线阴影 + 有选择保留的明亮未涂抹纸面高光，绝非均匀打光
- 每个故事板分镜的内框严格为 16:9 宽高比
- 整张图像严格为 21:9 宽高比
- 所有手写文字采用自信的衬线粗体字母，略带参差——可读但非机器印刷
- 通篇泛黄米白的纸张纹理，带有细微的边缘磨损
- 老掠夺者有长辫子马尾 + 浓密长络腮胡；年轻掠夺者有剪短的短发 + 短胡茬——这两人在分镜 1 中彼此外形分明
- 女掠夺者有高马尾 + 额头上的黄铜护目镜——与另外两人明显不同
- 刘辉在分镜 3 中的蹲伏必须读作绿巨人（Hulk）式立定跳远准备——蓄力但不显紧张或吃力
- 分镜 3 的极低角度贴地透视必须毫不含糊——摄影机在沙地上笔直向上仰拍，刘辉高耸于摄影机之上

=== GPT-Image-2 提示词 - 万物生第1集 段6主角反击+副手变身 抽象故事板 1x4竖排版 ===

【用途】段6主角反击+副手变身15秒分镜图+4个panel故事板+1x4竖排布局+每panel保持16:9横构图+确保每格构图与最终视频画面比例一致

【提示词】

一份4格电影故事板，排布为4行1列的竖向网格。整体画布比例为16:36竖版（极度高瘦的竖向方向）。网格中的每一个单独分格都是一个16:9横向矩形。4个分格自上而下竖向堆叠。每个分格都是一幅带白色边距框的铅笔加马克笔粗略草图，每格下方有一小条信息栏。

手绘动画学院故事板美学，带铅笔线条、灰色马克笔铺调、偶有红色铅笔箭头标注指示运镜或角色运动弧线、米白色纸张质感背景。刻意呈现粗糙抽象的草图质感——不是完成度高的插画——所有人物均以极简火柴人剪影或简单身体形状呈现，无面部特征、无服装细节、无配饰可见，所有物体均以基本几何形状表示（火炮为圆柱、炮弹为实心圆、船只为简单矩形、沙爆为膨胀的云团形状、机械鸟为简单的鸟形剪影轮廓、护甲碎片为悬于空中的小几何碎片）。目的仅在于传达构图与空间关系，而非视觉细节。

[PANEL 1 顶部 - 船体外景 低仰角 主角开炮]
下方标签："PANEL 1 · SHIP HULL EXT LOW UP-ANGLE / 50mm / 4s / SFX: cannon BOOM at 4s"
分格比例严格为16:9横向。
构图：从船体外侧、以略微低角度仰望甲板栏杆边缘的镜头。船体栏杆作为一条带竖向支柱的水平线，占据画面底部三分之一。栏杆后方、中景偏前处，火柴人主角正起身举着一根扛在右肩上的圆柱形炮管。火炮画成一个长长的简单圆柱形状，朝向画面左侧前外方倾斜。主角身形显出紧绷用力的姿态——身体因炮重而略向后仰，双臂吃力地扛着炮管。炮口处画一小团烟雾与红铅笔的闪光标记，指示开炮瞬间。背景天空以柔和的灰色马克笔铺调，占据画面上半部。一条红铅笔箭头指示镜头从外侧朝船只轻微推进。

[PANEL 2 第二格 - 子弹时间 180度环绕至刘辉 沙地命中]
下方标签："PANEL 2 · BULLET TIME ORBIT / 50mm / 5s / SFX: low whoosh, BOOM impact"
分格比例严格为16:9横向。
构图：极致的子弹时间镜头。前景中央一枚黑色炮弹画成简单的实心圆，凝固在飞行中途，身后拖着一条逗号形的弯曲烟迹。炮弹周围画有运动拖痕线，指示慢动作的空气。画面在同一构图中捕捉两个空间时刻：中景偏前为带烟迹凝固的炮弹，纵深远处、靠画面右侧为一处地面沙地命中区，画成膨胀的圆形云爆式沙爆并带放射状线条。命中区附近，一个火柴人骑在四足机械生物形状之上（代表刘辉骑在赛加羚羊上）呈反应姿态——生物前腿抬起急停，骑手火柴人向后仰。慢动作大气雾霭以柔和灰色马克笔铺调。一条红铅笔弯曲箭头指示镜头从炮弹到命中区的180度环绕路径。

[PANEL 3 第三格 - 副手 中近景 嘶吼]
下方标签："PANEL 3 · LIEUTENANT MCU YELLING / 85mm / 2s / SFX: shout 'LAO DA!'"
分格比例严格为16:9横向。
构图：副手身形的中近景，置于画面中央。该身形画成一个火柴人，带宽肩的融合形态剪影，头顶有鸟头头盔形状、肩后可见部分翅膀边缘。身形仅取胸部以上——半身取景。身体略侧向一边，鸟头以侧面或3/4视角转向画外方向。其姿态传达愤怒嘶吼——张开的嘴画成一个小小的深色椭圆。背景为简单天空，以柔和马克笔铺调占据画面大部，画面一侧边缘以淡淡线条暗示出船尾结构的一小段局部剪影。一条小红铅笔箭头指示镜头朝身形轻微推进。

[PANEL 4 底部 - 行走变身 英雄落地姿]
下方标签："PANEL 4 · WALK-TRANSFORM TO HERO POSE / 50mm to 35mm / 4s / SFX: armor disengage, bird-mount lock"
分格比例严格为16:9横向。
构图：本格展示一段行走变身序列的最终结束姿态。画面中景偏前居中处，副手以人类身体形态（无融合外骨骼）的火柴人立于中央，画成低角度英雄姿——镜头位于身形腰部高度以下向上仰望，故仅身形的上半身与头部入画，画面下部显示地面/甲板平面。身形以半身取景占据中央前景，呈具压迫感的剪影姿态——一手叉腰或松弛垂于身侧，头略向下俯视镜头、隐含权威感。身形后方约2米处，另画一个四足鸟形生物剪影立于甲板上，略偏向身形一侧、而非正后方。该鸟形生物画成一个简单的长颈四足机械鸟轮廓，带长尾与部分折叠的翅膀形状。两个身形周围与之间，散布若干小几何碎片形状悬于空中，仿佛护甲碎片仍在变身最后一瞬归位定位。背景以柔和马克笔铺调表现甲板与天空，并以简单直线淡淡暗示船尾结构。画面底部一条红铅笔箭头指示镜头相对于站立身形的低仰角位置。

[整体艺术指导]
经典动画学院故事板美学。铅笔线条带可见草图线、偶有结构辅助线与粗略修正。灰色马克笔铺调用于影调区域（天空、阴影、烟雾、沙爆）。4格上均有红铅笔箭头标注以指示运镜与关键运动弧线。米白色纸张质感背景。不是完成度高的插画。不是照片写实。不是精细角色美术。不是上色的。整份故事板刻意保持单色（铅笔灰 + 红箭头点缀），以强调这是一份仅用于构图的规划文件，而非用于最终渲染的视觉参考。

画布顶部居中标题为 "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG6 - PROTAGONIST CANNON RETALIATION + LIEUTENANT TRANSFORM STORYBOARD"，用小巧干净的黑色无衬线文字。

[关键限制]
布局铁律：整体画布是16:36竖向比例+4个分格自上而下竖向堆叠为4行1列网格+绝对不要横向排列+绝对不要2x2网格+绝对不要任何其他布局。

每格比例铁律：网格内每一个单独分格都必须是16:9横向矩形+每一个分格都是宽多于高的横向矩形+绝对不要竖向分格+绝对不要正方形分格+4个分格高度相等+4个分格宽度相等+4个分格都严格保持16:9横向比例。

风格铁律：纯手绘铅笔素描风格+铅笔加马克笔粗略草图+动画学院故事板美学+可见草图线+粗略结构修正+灰色马克笔铺调+红铅笔运镜箭头标注+米白纸张背景质感+刻意粗糙抽象+不是完成度高的插画+不是照片写实+不是上色的+不是精细角色渲染+不是干净线稿+不是数字化完美干净的线条+所有线条都必须看起来手绘、带自然变化。

简笔画铁律：所有人物都必须以极简火柴人剪影或基本身体形状呈现+无面部特征+无服装细节+无配饰+无装备细节+无武器细节+无头发细节+人物上无颜色+绝对无角色设计渲染+这些身形只是指示位置与姿态的占位形状+不是角色插画。

几何形状铁律：所有物体都必须以基本几何形状呈现+火炮为简单圆柱+炮弹为实心圆+船体栏杆为带竖向的水平线+沙爆为带放射状线条的膨胀圆形云团+机械鸟形生物为简单长颈四足轮廓+护甲碎片为悬空的小几何碎片+任何物体都不作细节渲染+所有物体都只是指示空间位置的占位形状。

不要横向布局+不要1行4列网格+不要2x2网格+不要正方形分格+不要网格内竖向9:16分格+不要照片写实+不要3D渲染+不要CGI+不要电子游戏画面+不要精细概念设定图+不要完成度高的角色插画+不要上色人物+不要写实布光+不要电影感摄影+不要精细戏服设计+不要精细武器设计+不要带铺调的角色细节+不要干净的数字线稿+不要漫画式精细美术+不要美漫式精细美术+不要品牌式吉祥物插画+无字幕水印+无装饰元素除铅笔箭头与镜头标签外。
```

A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, in the style of professional film production storyboards. Total image aspect ratio strictly 21:9.

Layout: 2 columns x 3 rows grid on aged paper background. Top-left and top-right are PANEL 4 and PANEL 5. Middle-left and middle-right are PANEL 6 and PANEL 7. Bottom-left is a shooting plan diagram (top-down bird's eye view of the desert with camera positions and 180-degree axis line marked). Bottom-right is a production notes text block. All storyboard panel cells strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format with thick hand-drawn black ink frame borders.

Beneath each storyboard panel inner frame is a hand-lettered caption strip showing panel number, lens specification, shot type, and brief SFX/action notes in serif block letters slightly uneven.

The drawing technique is loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective and clear cinematic composition. Sketches are deliberately gestural but every spatial relationship and prop detail is clearly readable. Selective red colored pencil arrows and emphasis marks indicate movement direction, force vectors, and key dramatic beats.

LIGHTING NOTE FOR ALL PANELS: late afternoon desert golden hour, low sun key light from upper-left 30 degrees. Dense cross-hatching dominates most surfaces representing the shadow base of the warm desert atmosphere. Selective bright untouched paper zones represent the directional sun-key highlights on metal armor surfaces and rim-lit silhouettes. Strong tonal contrast throughout — deep blacks in shadow zones, bright untouched paper for highlights, NOT evenly lit.

Style references: Wes Anderson production storyboards, traditional Hollywood film storyboard aesthetic, animator's pre-visualization sketches, Mad Max Fury Road action sequence boards.

Drawing rules:
- Pencil graphite on warm off-white aged paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use
- Black ink for panel borders and hand-lettered captions
- Red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera position triangles, and 180-degree axis line
- Cross-hatching for shading; bright highlight zones use untouched paper
- Hand-lettered captions in serif-style block letters, slightly uneven

DIRECTIONAL AXIS RULE: the protagonist ship continues moving deep into frame (forward vector). The chieftain catches up from behind to the female sand hunter's skimmer, takes her rope, plants himself in the sand and pulls backward against the ship's forward motion. The pulling vector is the REVERSE of the ship's forward vector along the same axis line. Cameras stay on one consistent side of this action axis throughout the sequence to maintain spatial continuity.

Title at top center of entire image, hand-lettered in confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9B — ROPE HANDOFF, PULL, AND SHIP COLLAPSE"

Below title, hand-lettered subtitle in smaller letters: "18 SECONDS / 4 PANELS / SHOOTING PLAN / PRODUCTION NOTES"

PANEL 4 (top-left cell) — OVER-SHOULDER HANDOFF — CHIEFTAIN TAKES ROPE FROM FEMALE SAND HUNTER:
Scene composition: over-the-shoulder shot from behind the female sand hunter. Foreground (lower-left of frame): the back of the female sand hunter's head and shoulder, her braided dark hair and red tribal ribbon headdress sketched in pencil silhouette, her hand visible in mid-frame still gripping the taut rope that extends out of frame toward the upper-right (toward the off-frame ship). Mid-ground (center-right of frame): the fusion-form chieftain figure has just arrived alongside her skimmer — towering massive bronze armored figure with shoulder spiral horns and glowing cyan chest core, extending one massive armored hand toward the rope in the female hunter's grip. The female hunter is in profile, turning her head to look up at the chieftain — her expression shows recognition and deference (eyes wide, mouth slightly open). The chieftain's face (small human face visible above the armor collar) is set in calm focused expression, looking down at the rope as he reaches for it. Background: the rear of the female hunter's skimmer visible at the lower edges of frame, sand plume from the dragging skimmer, distant silhouette of the protagonist ship's rear visible in the upper-right corner of frame still being dragged into frame.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow from behind the female hunter's shoulder pointing forward toward the chieftain figure, labeled "OTS / female hunter foreground." Small red pencil arrow on the chieftain's hand indicating his reaching motion toward the rope.

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 4 — OTS HANDOFF / 50mm prime / hand-held with subtle drift / SFX: chieftain heavy footfall arrival, female hunter sharp gasp, rope creak under shifting hands, drag-friction sand grind continuous"

PANEL 5 (top-right cell) — MEDIUM WIDE FRONT — CHIEFTAIN PULLS WITH FULL BODY POWER:
Scene composition: medium-wide front-quarter framing of the chieftain figure planted in the sand desert ground, pulling on the rope. The chieftain occupies the central two-thirds of the frame from feet to head. His body posture: massive bronze armored legs spread wide in a stable wide-base stance with both feet planted deep into the sand (heels visibly cratered into the ground), entire body leaning backward at a steep angle (about 30 degrees from vertical), both armored arms extended forward gripping the rope with both massive hands, the rope visible extending taut into the upper-frame edge toward the off-frame ship. His face: visible at the top of the chest armor, jaw clenched, single visible eye narrowed in focused exertion, the right eye-patch dark in shadow, NOT shouting NOT roaring just contained heavy strain. The cyan chest core glows brighter than baseline (drawn with bright untouched paper radiating outward more strongly than other panels indicating power surge). The rope extends from his grip toward the upper edge of frame, drawn as a perfectly rigid straight diagonal line under maximum tension, with subtle vibration cross-hatching along its length. Beneath his planted feet: the desert sand surface is fractured outward in a radial starburst pattern of deep cracks fanning out in all directions from his feet, sand particles visibly displaced and lifted in clouds around his ankles. Background: faint sketch of the female hunter's skimmer visible behind him at lower frame edges, sand plume from drag, golden hour sky above.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow from the chieftain's grip along the rope pointing toward the upper frame edge labeled "tension vector / ROPE TAUT TO SHIP." Red pencil radial burst marks around his planted feet labeled "sand fracture under load." Red pencil emphasis at the cyan core labeled "POWER SURGE."

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 5 — MED-WIDE FRONT-QUARTER / 35mm prime / hand-held with locked-off-feel impact stabilize / SFX: massive armor mechanism groan under load, rope humming at maximum tension, sand cracking under planted feet, low-frequency power throb from chest core, distant ship leg crashes"

PANEL 6 (middle-left cell) — WIDE SIDE SHOT — SHIP SLOWING DOWN UNDER REVERSE PULL:
Scene composition: wide side-profile shot showing the entire battlefield horizontally. Center of frame: the protagonist ship in side view as a long rectangular silhouette with nine mechanical leg shapes underneath — but three of the legs (positioned mid-ship and rear) are visibly stretched and pulled backward at extreme angles, their forward-stride motion compromised. The other six legs continue their galloping forward motion but their rhythm is visibly broken — some mid-stride and faltering. The overall ship body is tilted slightly backward (rear-heavy posture) showing the deceleration. Three taut ropes drawn as rigid diagonal lines extending from the three stretched legs backward and outward to the lower frame corners where the three sand skimmers are dragged trailing behind. The sand plume erupting from beneath the ship is visibly thinner and more broken/sporadic than in earlier galloping shots — the dust output is decreasing as forward speed drops. Behind the ship at lower-left frame: the chieftain figure visible in middle distance still planted and pulling, his rope visible connecting to one specific leg. The other two skimmers behind him also pulling but their effect was negligible — now amplified by the chieftain's force. Foreground (lower frame edge): a strip of cracked sand desert ground with deep tire-track ruts from the dragging skimmers.

Camera annotation: large red pencil arrow showing the ship's diminishing forward direction (drawn shorter than in earlier panels suggesting reduced velocity). Three red pencil opposing arrows along the three taut ropes showing the pulling tension force pointing rearward. Small red pencil annotation at the chieftain figure: "CHIEFTAIN PULL = DECISIVE."

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 6 — WIDE SIDE SHIP DECELERATION / 50mm prime / lateral hand-held tracking with reduced speed / SFX: ship leg crashes becoming irregular and slowing, mechanical leg-strain metallic groans, rope tension humming at climax, sand plume reducing"

PANEL 7 (middle-right cell) — MASSIVE SLOW COLLAPSE — SHIP TIPPING FORWARD INTO SAND:
Scene composition: wide three-quarter angle shot capturing the moment the protagonist ship loses balance and begins tipping forward into the desert. The ship occupies the central portion of frame, body tilted forward at a steep angle (about 25 degrees from horizontal) with the bow plunging downward toward the sand surface. Three mechanical legs (the ones held by ropes) have collapsed at their knee joints — their joints buckled with armor segments visibly splayed outward at the bending points, the legs splayed in different awkward kneeling angles. The six remaining legs are mid-collapse, unable to compensate, some still mid-stride with feet not yet grounded. The ship's bow has plowed nose-first into the sand surface, ejecting an enormous plume of sand and dust upward and outward in a massive cross-hatched cloud that fills the lower-third of the frame and obscures much of the lower ship body. Sand displaced by the impact erupts in massive radial bursts from the bow contact point. Behind the ship at left frame edge: the chieftain figure visible still holding the now-slackening rope, his pose shifting from straining-backward to standing-firm as the ship goes down. Camera framing should convey the slow weight and inevitability — the ship is going down as if in a controlled fall, not a crash. Background sky: golden hour upper atmosphere with dense cross-hatching, dust cloud rising into the upper sky from the impact.

Camera annotation: red pencil curved arrow showing the ship's tipping motion arcing forward and downward into the sand, labeled "SLOW COLLAPSE." Red pencil radial burst lines at the bow impact point with sand explosion vectors. Small wavy red pencil lines across the entire panel border to indicate "CAMERA SHAKE — ground tremor seismic-level." Red pencil arrow at the chieftain labeled "stance settling."

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 7 — WIDE 3/4 ANGLE COLLAPSE / 28mm wide / hand-held with seismic shake then settle / SFX: deafening THOOM of bow impact, massive sustained sand-explosion roar, mechanical leg-collapse buckling clanks, sub-bass earth-rumble, end with eerie wind silence"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-left cell) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW OF THE 9B SEQUENCE:
Drawn as a clean top-down schematic diagram on the aged paper. Center of cell: a sketched simplified outline of the desert terrain as seen from above. Upper portion of cell: the protagonist ship drawn as a rectangular schematic silhouette with nine small leg-marks underneath, with a red pencil directional arrow showing its forward vector (the arrow drawn shorter than in 9A's shooting plan to indicate slowing) and a curved red pencil arc showing where it tips forward and collapses (with a small starburst symbol at the ship's bow indicating the impact point labeled "COLLAPSE POINT"). Behind and to one side: three small triangular schematic outlines representing the three sand skimmers, each connected by a thin line (rope) to one of the ship's leg-marks. One of the three skimmers (the female hunter's) has a small armored figure-mark next to it representing the chieftain who has joined her position. From the chieftain's mark: a thicker red pencil tension arrow pointing rearward along the rope line indicating the dominant pulling force. Four small red pencil triangles marking the camera positions for each of PANEL 4/5/6/7 (P4 OTS camera positioned behind the female hunter pointing at the chieftain; P5 front-quarter camera positioned in front of the chieftain pointing at his torso; P6 wide side camera positioned perpendicular to the action axis pointing at the ship; P7 wide 3/4 camera positioned at a side-rear angle to the ship pointing at the collapse). A dashed red pencil straight line running across the diagram along the ship's forward vector and the rope tension axis — this is the 180-degree axis line. Labels in hand-lettered serif block letters: "180° AXIS LINE", "P4 / P5 / P6 / P7 CAMERA POSITIONS", "SHIP DECELERATION", "CHIEFTAIN ANCHOR POINT", "COLLAPSE POINT", "FEMALE HUNTER SKIMMER", "OTHER 2 SKIMMERS DRAGGED".

Caption strip below the shooting plan cell: "SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED ACROSS ALL 4 SHOTS / cameras stay on one side of action axis / spatial continuity maintained / collapse sequence covered with progressively wider angles"

PRODUCTION NOTES BLOCK (bottom-right cell) — A hand-lettered text-only cell containing key production memos in confident serif block letters across multiple paragraphs:
Block heading: "PRODUCTION NOTES — SEGMENT 9B"
Paragraph 1: "Continuity: Segment 9A closes with chieftain charging forward in orbit power run. Segment 9B opens with chieftain arriving at female sand hunter's skimmer to take the rope. No establishing shot needed — Panel 4 OTS framing communicates the handoff geometry directly."
Paragraph 2: "Pacing notes: Panel 5 is the longest single shot in this segment (approximately 6 seconds) to allow the audience to feel sustained physical strain. Panel 7 is also approximately 6 seconds — the slow collapse must convey enormous weight; rushing the collapse defeats the purpose. Total runtime 18 seconds."
Paragraph 3: "Sound design philosophy: NO musical score. Sound design only — ambient desert wind, mechanical groans, rope tension humming, ship leg crashes becoming irregular and stopping, climactic bow-impact THOOM, end with eerie wind silence after the dust settles. Chieftain has NO dialogue throughout."
Paragraph 4: "Character continuity: chieftain in 9B is the same fusion-form bronze mechanical chieftain from 9A — identical armor, same shoulder spiral horns, same cyan chest core, same right-eye eye-patch. Female sand hunter visible in PANEL 4 must match the established female hunter character design."
Paragraph 5: "Next segment hook: After ship collapses in Panel 7, the dust slowly settles to reveal the new tactical situation — protagonists trapped inside a downed ship surrounded by sand hunters and the chieftain. Segment 10 begins with this reveal."

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting style, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red colored pencil for arrows and annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO AI-style smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour desert high-contrast lighting in all panels — dense cross-hatch shadows + selective bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each storyboard panel's inner frame strictly 16:9 aspect ratio
- Total image strictly 21:9 aspect ratio
- All hand-lettered text in confident serif block letters with slight unevenness — readable but not machine-printed
- Aged off-white paper texture throughout with subtle edge wear
- Maintain consistent fusion-form chieftain character appearance across PANEL 4, 5, 6, 7 — same bronze mechanical armor, shoulder spiral horns, cyan chest core, right-eye eye-patch
- Female sand hunter character appearance consistent in PANEL 4
- Protagonist ship appearance consistent across PANEL 6 and PANEL 7 — same nine-leg rectangular silhouette design

**🀄 中文版**

```
一幅绘制在做旧米白纸张上的手绘铅笔素描故事板，采用专业影视制作故事板的风格。整幅图像宽高比严格为 21:9。

版式：做旧纸张背景上的 2 列 × 3 行网格。左上和右上分别是分镜 4 和分镜 5。中左和中右分别是分镜 6 和分镜 7。左下是一张拍摄方案示意图（沙漠的俯视鸟瞰图，标出机位和 180 度轴线）。右下是一段制作笔记文字块。所有故事板分格严格为 16:9 横向横幅格式，带有粗重的手绘黑色墨水画框边线。

每个故事板分镜内框下方都有一条手写说明文字条，以略微参差的衬线方块字标注分镜编号、镜头规格、景别以及简短的音效/动作备注。

绘画技法为松弛的石墨铅笔，配以自信的建筑透视和清晰的电影化构图。素描刻意带有手势感的随性笔触，但每一处空间关系和道具细节都清晰可辨。有选择地使用红色彩铅箭头与强调标记，指示运动方向、力矢量以及关键的戏剧节拍。

所有分镜的光照说明：午后晚些时分的沙漠黄金时刻，低位太阳主光来自左上方 30 度。密集的交叉排线主导大部分表面，表现温暖沙漠氛围的阴影基底。有选择地保留明亮的留白纸张区域，表现金属盔甲表面的定向阳光主光高光与轮廓逆光剪影。整体色调对比强烈——阴影区为深黑，高光处为明亮留白，绝非均匀打光。

风格参考：韦斯·安德森（Wes Anderson）制作故事板、传统好莱坞影视故事板美学、动画师的预演素描、《疯狂的麦克斯：狂暴之路》（Mad Max Fury Road）动作场面分镜板。

绘画规则：
- 在温暖的米白做旧纸张背景上用石墨铅笔作画，边缘略有磨损、隐约带有污渍，暗示历经繁重的制作使用
- 黑色墨水用于分镜边框和手写说明
- 红色彩铅仅用于箭头、强调标记、机位三角形以及 180 度轴线
- 用交叉排线表现阴影；明亮高光区域使用留白纸张
- 手写说明采用衬线风格方块字，略微参差

方向轴规则：主角飞船继续向画面纵深方向移动（前进矢量）。首领从后方追上女沙猎手的滑沙艇，夺过她的绳索，将自己钉进沙地里，沿着同一条轴线、与飞船前进矢量相反地向后拉拽。拉拽矢量是飞船前进矢量沿同一轴线的反向。在整个序列中，机位始终保持在这条动作轴线的同一侧，以维持空间连贯性。

整幅图像顶部居中标题，以自信的方块衬线手写："WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9B — ROPE HANDOFF, PULL, AND SHIP COLLAPSE"

标题下方，以较小字体手写副标题："18 SECONDS / 4 PANELS / SHOOTING PLAN / PRODUCTION NOTES"

分镜 4（左上格）——过肩交接——首领从女沙猎手手中夺过绳索：
画面构图：从女沙猎手身后拍摄的过肩镜头。前景（画面左下方）：女沙猎手的后脑勺与肩膀，她编成辫子的深色头发和红色部落缎带头饰以铅笔剪影勾勒，她的手在画面中部可见，仍紧握着那根绷紧的绳索，绳索向画面右上方延伸出框（朝向画外的飞船）。中景（画面中右部）：融合形态的首领刚刚来到她的滑沙艇旁——高耸而庞大的青铜装甲身形，肩部带有螺旋犄角，胸口核心发出青色辉光，正伸出一只巨大的装甲手去够女猎手手中的绳索。女猎手呈侧面，转头抬眼望向首领——她的表情流露出认出对方后的恭敬（双眼圆睁，嘴微张）。首领的脸（装甲领圈上方可见的小小人脸）神情平静而专注，低头看着他正去够的绳索。背景：女猎手滑沙艇的尾部在画面下缘可见，被拖拽的滑沙艇扬起沙尘羽流，主角飞船尾部的远处剪影出现在画面右上角，仍在被拖入画面。

机位标注：一支红铅箭头从女猎手肩后向前指向首领身形，标注 "OTS / female hunter foreground"。首领手部有一支小红铅箭头，指示他伸手去够绳索的动作。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 4 — OTS HANDOFF / 50mm prime / hand-held with subtle drift / SFX: chieftain heavy footfall arrival, female hunter sharp gasp, rope creak under shifting hands, drag-friction sand grind continuous"

分镜 5（右上格）——中近景正面——首领以全身之力拉拽：
画面构图：以中近景前四分之三角度框取钉在沙漠地面上、正拉拽绳索的首领身形。首领从脚到头占据画面中部三分之二。他的身体姿态：庞大的青铜装甲双腿大幅叉开成稳固的宽底站姿，双脚深深扎进沙中（脚跟明显在地面砸出凹坑），整个身体以陡峭角度向后倾（约偏离垂直 30 度），两条装甲手臂向前伸展，双手紧握绳索，绳索向上延伸出画面上缘绷紧、朝向画外的飞船。他的脸：在胸甲顶部可见，下颌紧咬，唯一可见的那只眼在专注的用力中眯起，右眼的眼罩隐没在阴影里，没有呐喊也没有咆哮，只有内敛的沉重发力。胸口青色核心比基准更亮地发着光（用明亮留白绘出，向外辐射得比其他分镜更强烈，指示力量涌动）。绳索从他的握处向画面上缘延伸，画成一条在最大张力下完全僵直的笔直对角线，沿其长度带有细微的振动交叉排线。在他扎稳的双脚之下：沙漠沙面以放射状星爆图案向外碎裂，深裂缝向四面八方扇形散开，沙粒明显被掀起、在他脚踝周围扬成尘云。背景：他身后画面下缘可见女猎手滑沙艇的淡淡素描，拖拽扬起的沙尘羽流，上方是黄金时刻的天空。

机位标注：一支红铅箭头从首领握处沿绳索指向画面上缘，标注 "tension vector / ROPE TAUT TO SHIP"。他扎稳的双脚周围有红铅放射状爆裂标记，标注 "sand fracture under load"。青色核心处有红铅强调标记，标注 "POWER SURGE"。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 5 — MED-WIDE FRONT-QUARTER / 35mm prime / hand-held with locked-off-feel impact stabilize / SFX: massive armor mechanism groan under load, rope humming at maximum tension, sand cracking under planted feet, low-frequency power throb from chest core, distant ship leg crashes"

分镜 6（中左格）——宽幅侧面镜头——飞船在反向拉拽下减速：
画面构图：宽幅侧面剖视镜头，水平展示整个战场。画面中央：主角飞船以侧视呈一道长方形剪影，下方有九条机械腿形——但其中三条腿（位于船身中部和后部）明显被向后拉扯到极端角度，其前迈的运动受阻。另外六条腿继续奔腾向前，但节奏明显被打乱——有的迈到一半正在踉跄。飞船船体整体略微后倾（尾部偏重的姿态），显出减速之势。三根绷紧的绳索画成僵直的对角线，从三条被拉扯的腿向后向外延伸到画面下方的两角，那里拖拽着三艘尾随其后的沙地滑沙艇。从飞船下方喷涌而出的沙尘羽流明显比早先奔腾镜头里更稀薄、更断续/零星——随着前进速度下降，扬尘输出正在减少。飞船身后画面左下方：首领身形在中景距离上可见，仍钉在原地拉拽，他的绳索可见连接到某一条特定的腿上。他身后另外两艘滑沙艇也在拉拽，但其作用本可忽略不计——如今被首领之力放大了。前景（画面下缘）：一条龟裂的沙漠地面，带有被拖拽滑沙艇碾出的深深辙印。

机位标注：一支大红铅箭头表示飞船日渐减弱的前进方向（画得比早先分镜更短，暗示速度降低）。三根绷紧绳索上各有红铅反向箭头，表示指向后方的拉拽张力。首领身形处有红铅小标注："CHIEFTAIN PULL = DECISIVE"。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 6 — WIDE SIDE SHIP DECELERATION / 50mm prime / lateral hand-held tracking with reduced speed / SFX: ship leg crashes becoming irregular and slowing, mechanical leg-strain metallic groans, rope tension humming at climax, sand plume reducing"

分镜 7（中右格）——庞然缓慢的倾覆——飞船向前栽入沙中：
画面构图：宽幅四分之三角度镜头，捕捉主角飞船失去平衡、开始向前栽入沙漠的瞬间。飞船占据画面中部，船体以陡峭角度向前倾斜（约偏离水平 25 度），船首向下扎向沙面。三条机械腿（被绳索拉住的那三条）在膝关节处已经垮塌——关节屈曲，装甲段在弯折点明显向外撇开，三条腿以各种别扭的跪姿叉开。其余六条腿正处于垮塌之中，无力补偿，有的还迈到一半、脚尚未着地。飞船船首已经一头扎进沙面，将一道巨大的沙尘羽流向上向外喷出，化作一团庞大的交叉排线尘云，填满画面下三分之一，遮住下半部船体的大部分。撞击掀起的沙土从船首接触点以巨大的放射状爆裂迸发。飞船身后画面左缘：首领身形可见，仍握着此刻正松弛下来的绳索，他的姿态从向后发力转为站定不动，看着飞船倒下。镜头取景应传达那种缓慢的重量感与必然感——飞船是如同受控坠落般倒下，而非猛烈撞毁。背景天空：黄金时刻的高空，带密集交叉排线，撞击扬起的尘云升入上方天际。

机位标注：一支红铅曲线箭头表示飞船向前向下弧入沙中的倾覆运动，标注 "SLOW COLLAPSE"。船首撞击点有红铅放射状爆裂线条，带沙土爆裂矢量。整个分镜边框上画有红铅波纹细线，表示 "CAMERA SHAKE — ground tremor seismic-level"。首领处有红铅箭头，标注 "stance settling"。

分镜下方说明条："PANEL 7 — WIDE 3/4 ANGLE COLLAPSE / 28mm wide / hand-held with seismic shake then settle / SFX: deafening THOOM of bow impact, massive sustained sand-explosion roar, mechanical leg-collapse buckling clanks, sub-bass earth-rumble, end with eerie wind silence"

拍摄方案（左下格）——9B 序列的俯视鸟瞰总览：
在做旧纸张上画成一张干净的俯视示意图。格子中央：从上方俯视的沙漠地形的简化轮廓素描。格子上部：主角飞船画成一道长方形示意剪影，下方带九个小腿标，配一支红铅方向箭头表示其前进矢量（箭头画得比 9A 拍摄方案中更短，以指示减速），以及一道红铅曲线弧，表示它向前倾覆垮塌之处（船首处带一个小星爆符号指示撞击点，标注 "COLLAPSE POINT"）。在其后方并偏向一侧：三个三角形小示意轮廓代表三艘沙地滑沙艇，每个都由一条细线（绳索）连到飞船的某个腿标。其中一艘滑沙艇（女猎手的那艘）旁有一个装甲身形小标记，代表已加入她位置的首领。从首领的标记处：一支较粗的红铅张力箭头沿绳线指向后方，指示占主导的拉拽之力。四个红铅小三角形标出分镜 4/5/6/7 各自的机位（P4 过肩机位位于女猎手身后，指向首领；P5 前四分之一机位位于首领前方，指向其躯干；P6 宽幅侧面机位垂直于动作轴线，指向飞船；P7 宽幅四分之三机位位于飞船的侧后角度，指向倾覆）。一条红铅虚线笔直贯穿示意图，沿飞船前进矢量与绳索张力轴——这便是 180 度轴线。手写衬线方块字标签："180° AXIS LINE"、"P4 / P5 / P6 / P7 CAMERA POSITIONS"、"SHIP DECELERATION"、"CHIEFTAIN ANCHOR POINT"、"COLLAPSE POINT"、"FEMALE HUNTER SKIMMER"、"OTHER 2 SKIMMERS DRAGGED"。

拍摄方案格下方说明条："SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED ACROSS ALL 4 SHOTS / cameras stay on one side of action axis / spatial continuity maintained / collapse sequence covered with progressively wider angles"

制作笔记文字块（右下格）——一个纯文字格，以自信的衬线方块字分多段写有关键制作备忘：
块标题："PRODUCTION NOTES — SEGMENT 9B"
第 1 段："Continuity: Segment 9A closes with chieftain charging forward in orbit power run. Segment 9B opens with chieftain arriving at female sand hunter's skimmer to take the rope. No establishing shot needed — Panel 4 OTS framing communicates the handoff geometry directly."
第 2 段："Pacing notes: Panel 5 is the longest single shot in this segment (approximately 6 seconds) to allow the audience to feel sustained physical strain. Panel 7 is also approximately 6 seconds — the slow collapse must convey enormous weight; rushing the collapse defeats the purpose. Total runtime 18 seconds."
第 3 段："Sound design philosophy: NO musical score. Sound design only — ambient desert wind, mechanical groans, rope tension humming, ship leg crashes becoming irregular and stopping, climactic bow-impact THOOM, end with eerie wind silence after the dust settles. Chieftain has NO dialogue throughout."
第 4 段："Character continuity: chieftain in 9B is the same fusion-form bronze mechanical chieftain from 9A — identical armor, same shoulder spiral horns, same cyan chest core, same right-eye eye-patch. Female sand hunter visible in PANEL 4 must match the established female hunter character design."
第 5 段："Next segment hook: After ship collapses in Panel 7, the dust slowly settles to reveal the new tactical situation — protagonists trapped inside a downed ship surrounded by sand hunters and the chieftain. Segment 10 begins with this reveal."

硬性约束：
- 不要数字绘画风格，不要 3D 渲染，除红色彩铅用于箭头和标注外不要任何彩色叠加
- 不要摄影元素，不要 AI 风格的平滑渐变
- 所有分镜都采用强烈的黄金时刻沙漠高对比光照——密集交叉排线阴影 + 有选择的明亮留白高光，绝非均匀打光
- 每个故事板分镜的内框严格为 16:9 宽高比
- 整幅图像严格为 21:9 宽高比
- 所有手写文字采用自信的衬线方块字，略带参差——可读但非机器打印
- 通篇做旧米白纸张质感，带细微边缘磨损
- 在分镜 4、5、6、7 之间保持融合形态首领角色外观一致——同样的青铜机械装甲、肩部螺旋犄角、青色胸口核心、右眼眼罩
- 女沙猎手角色外观在分镜 4 中保持一致
- 主角飞船外观在分镜 6 和分镜 7 之间保持一致——同样的九腿长方形剪影设计
```

```
A professional 13-color palette design sheet for film production, in the style of a cinematographer's color script reference card. Image aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format.

Layout: pure clean off-white background. Centered at top: large bold black sans-serif title "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG10C - BIRD-DRAGON HUNTS CRAB BALL ON DECK - 13 COLOR PALETTE" in clean industrial typography.

Below the title: 13 vertical color swatch bars arranged in a single horizontal row, evenly spaced, each swatch is a tall vertical rectangle. Each color swatch is a flat solid color rectangle with crisp clean edges, no gradient, no texture inside the swatch itself. All 13 swatches are the same size and aligned on the same baseline.

Below each color swatch, hand-set typographic labels in two lines:
- Line 1: color name in clean bold sans-serif uppercase, two short lines if needed
- Line 2: HEX code in monospace sans-serif

The 13 colors in left-to-right order with their exact HEX values and labels:

1. SUNSET SKY ORANGE / #C8581F (visible sunset sky beyond deck edges)
2. WORN DECK WOOD BROWN / #6B4830 (weathered deck planks main wood color where the crab ball rolls)
3. BRONZE OLIVE MECH / #7A6845 (Bird-Dragon body armor + Crab outer shell + Bronze Ship Wheel shared mech color)
4. ANCIENT DARK GOLD / #846035 (ancient gold etched glyph plates on bird-dragon armor and crab symbol-engraved facets)
5. LOST-TECH CYAN GLOW / #4FC3D9 (Bird-Dragon cyan electric arc eye + Crab joint seam glow)
6. GOLDEN HOUR DIRECT LIGHT / #F0B670 (warm golden direct sunlight tint on lit surfaces)
7. COOL SHADOW CYAN-BLUE / #4A5870 (cool blue-tinted shadows on deck and under bird-dragon's wings)
8. WING FEATHER OCHRE CRIMSON / #6B4A2E (Bird-Dragon wing feather main color, ochre brown + red + dark gold blend)
9. WING FEATHER TIP DARK BROWN / #2E1F15 (Bird-Dragon wing feather tip darker brown + feather shadow recesses)
10. DUST PARTICLE HAZE / #C0B299 (dust kicked up by crab ball rolling + lingering deck dust + atmospheric haze)
11. POLISHED BRONZE BEAK HIGHLIGHT / #8E6F3D (Bird-Dragon mechanical beak polished bronze highlight on lit edge)
12. BEAK SHADOW BRONZE / #3A2A1F (Bird-Dragon beak shadow side dark bronze recess)
13. PITCH LIGHTLESS BLACK / #1A1610 (deep shadow anchor for crab ball silhouette and ship cabin deep shadow recess)

Each color swatch must render the exact HEX value with full color fidelity — flat solid color with no shading, gradient, or texture overlay. The label text below must be perfectly readable.

Color #6 GOLDEN HOUR DIRECT LIGHT is a warm peachy gold #F0B670 representing the actual color cast of golden hour sunlight on illuminated surfaces.
Color #7 COOL SHADOW CYAN-BLUE #4A5870 is a desaturated muted blue-grey representing the actual color tint of shadows during golden hour.
Color #10 DUST PARTICLE HAZE #C0B299 is a warm pale dust gray representing atmospheric haze and rolling dust particles in the desert deck environment.

The overall composition is a clean professional color reference sheet, similar to color scripts used in film production for cinematographers and colorists.

Hard constraints:
- All 13 color swatches must be exact HEX-accurate flat solid colors
- Strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape aspect ratio
- White or off-white pure background, NO illustrations, NO photographic elements, NO sample scenes
- Clean typographic labels in sans-serif font, fully readable
- Color swatches arranged in a single horizontal row with even spacing
- Each swatch is tall vertical rectangle with crisp clean edges
- No watermarks, no extra decorative elements
- The title at top is the largest text element
- The overall design feels professional, industrial, production-grade, like an Adobe Color or Pantone reference card crossed with a film production color script
```

**🀄 中文版**

```
一张用于影视制作的专业 13 色色板设计图，风格为摄影指导的配色脚本参考卡。图像比例严格为 16:9 横向格式。

版式：纯净干净的米白色背景。顶部居中：大号粗体黑色无衬线标题 "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG10C - BIRD-DRAGON HUNTS CRAB BALL ON DECK - 13 COLOR PALETTE"，采用干净的工业字体。

标题下方：13 条竖向色样条排成单一水平行，等间距排列，每条色样都是一个高高的竖向矩形。每个色样都是一个平涂的纯色矩形，边缘清晰利落，色样内部无渐变、无质感。13 个色样大小相同，对齐在同一条基线上。

每个色样下方，手工排版的字体标签分两行：

13 种颜色按从左到右顺序及其确切 HEX 值与标签：

5. LOST-TECH CYAN GLOW / #4FC3D9（鸟龙青色电弧眼 + 蟹关节缝隙辉光）
6. GOLDEN HOUR DIRECT LIGHT / #F0B670（受光表面上的暖金色直射阳光色调）
7. COOL SHADOW CYAN-BLUE / #4A5870（甲板上以及鸟龙翼下冷蓝色调的阴影）
8. WING FEATHER OCHRE CRIMSON / #6B4A2E（鸟龙翼羽主色，赭棕 + 红 + 深金混合）
9. WING FEATHER TIP DARK BROWN / #2E1F15（鸟龙翼羽尖端更深的棕色 + 羽毛阴影凹处）
10. DUST PARTICLE HAZE / #C0B299（蟹球滚动扬起的尘土 + 甲板上残留的灰尘 + 大气霾）
11. POLISHED BRONZE BEAK HIGHLIGHT / #8E6F3D（鸟龙机械喙受光边缘的抛光青铜高光）
12. BEAK SHADOW BRONZE / #3A2A1F（鸟龙喙背光侧的深青铜凹处）
13. PITCH LIGHTLESS BLACK / #1A1610（蟹球剪影与船舱深阴影凹处的深阴影锚定色）

每个色样都必须以完全的色彩保真度渲染出确切的 HEX 值——平涂纯色，无明暗、无渐变、无质感叠加。下方的标签文字必须完美清晰可读。

颜色 #6 GOLDEN HOUR DIRECT LIGHT 是一种暖桃金色 #F0B670，代表黄金时刻阳光照在受光表面上的实际色偏。
颜色 #7 COOL SHADOW CYAN-BLUE #4A5870 是一种去饱和的柔和蓝灰色，代表黄金时刻阴影的实际色调。
颜色 #10 DUST PARTICLE HAZE #C0B299 是一种暖调的淡尘灰色，代表沙漠甲板环境中的大气霾与滚动的尘土颗粒。

整体构图是一张干净的专业配色参考图，类似于影视制作中供摄影指导与调色师使用的配色脚本。

硬性约束：
- 全部 13 个色样都必须是 HEX 精确的平涂纯色
- 比例严格为 16:9 横向
- 白色或米白色纯背景，无插画、无照片元素、无示例场景

- 整体设计感觉专业、工业、制作级，像是 Adobe Color 或 Pantone 参考卡与影视制作配色脚本的结合体

- Clean typographic labels in sans-serif font, fully readable
- Color swatches arranged in a single horizontal row with even spacing
- Each swatch is tall vertical rectangle with crisp clean edges
- No watermarks, no extra decorative elements
- The title at top is the largest text element
- The overall design feels professional, industrial, production-grade, like an Adobe Color or Pantone reference card crossed with a film production color script
```

13 个色样自左至右为：

6. Aged Gold 做旧金 #846035（船体亮青铜点缀 + 远古失落科技金属高光，分段 1 与分段 5 共用）
7. Late Golden Hour Sun 黄金时刻末段之阳 #F5C870（低角度落日核心高光）
8. Burnt Orange Dusk Sky 焦橙暮空 #C8581F（日落天空主色，主导性的大气暖调）
9. Amber Dust Haze 琥珀尘霾 #D9A55C（弥散全画面的金色悬浮尘霾）

13. Sand Hunter Tribal Crimson 沙猎部落绯红 #6B2419（沙猎部落布料束带的干血红，所有沙猎角色设计共用）

【构图】
每个色样宽高大致相等，彼此之间以细而等距的间隔排列。标签（HEX 代码）以小巧洁净的等宽黑色文字置于各色样正下方。整套色板占据画布中央 80% 的区域，四周留有白色边距。顶部居中标题文字："WANWU SHENG EP1 OPENING - DESERT CHASE COLOR REFERENCE"，为小巧洁净的黑色无衬线文字。

通体平整均匀的中性照明，色样上无阴影、无高光，色彩还原完全均匀。

【风格】
专业平面设计色板参考卡，色彩理论图表美学，洁净极简的设计，如同 Pantone 配色指南或设计参考表。

- 江流深棕皮革#3D2820（皮带+皮靴+挎包+斜挎带）
- 江流机械义体灰#2D2A28（颈部机械结构深灰金属）
- 萌螃蟹眼睛蓝#5BB8E0（萌螃蟹两个圆形大眼睛+区别于电弧青）
- 大漠土橘#8E5A2A（背景大漠地面流动，承段1）
- 沙猎血红#6B2419（远景沙艇追兵布条，承段1）

【视觉影视参考预案】
- 江流屏脸+机械义体：《攻壳机动队》Tachikoma机械感+《魂斗罗》Aibou机器人+《Ex Machina》面具感+战锤40k Skitarii面甲
- 江流古代织物服装：《加勒比海盗》Will Turner破旧衣+《长安十二时辰》游侠+王家卫《东邪西毒》荒漠破衣
- 萌螃蟹：Boston Dynamics Spot机械+宫崎骏《天空之城》机器人兵青铜质感+《风之谷》王虫但缩小为可爱版
- 整体调性：王家卫沙漠色调+Mad Max Fury Road kinetic+Wes Anderson构图

【提示词】

一张干净的色板色样参考卡设计。16:9 横向版式。白色背景。

【主体】
13 个等大的矩形色样横向排列成单行，横贯整张画布。每个色样都是一个干净的矩形纯平色块，边缘锐利干净，无渐变、无纹理、无噪点、无阴影。每个色样下方有一个小标签，以干净的等宽黑色字体显示 HEX 色值代码。

【色彩规格】
从左到右的 13 个色样为：

1. 漆黑无光黑 #1A1610（江流屏脸面具黑 + 深阴影锚点，全段共享）

2. 机改铜青绿 #7A6845（机械飞船甲板 + 船舵 + 青铜螃蟹伙伴外壳主色，承段1）

3. 古代金属暗金 #846035（船甲板装饰金属件 + 青铜螃蟹符号刻面，承段1）

4. 青色电弧 #4FC3D9（江流屏脸发光眼 + 青铜螃蟹关节缝隙辉光，所有失落科技装备共享的点缀色）

5. 黄金时刻晚阳 #F5C870（低角度落日侧光轮廓，承段1）

6. 焦橙黄昏天空 #C8581F（背景日落天空色，承段1）

7. 江流深酒红 #4A1F1F（江流外袍背心主色，带紫调的深酒红，明显比部落绯红更深更偏紫）

8. 江流米色亚麻 #E8DCC8（江流泡泡袖内衬衣，做旧的米色亚麻色）

9. 江流深棕皮革 #3D2820（江流皮带 + 皮靴 + 挎包 + 斜挎带）

10. 江流机械义体灰 #2D2A28（江流颈部机械义体结构，深枪铁灰）

11. 青铜螃蟹眼睛蓝 #5BB8E0（青铜螃蟹伙伴的两只圆形灯泡眼，比青色电弧更明亮饱和、与之区分的蓝色）

12. 沙漠土橘 #8E5A2A（甲板外背景中流动的广袤沙漠地面，承段1）

13. 沙猎部落绯红 #6B2419（远景沙艇追兵的部落布条缠带，承段1）

【构图】
每个色样宽高大致相等，以细窄的等距间隔排列。标签（HEX 色值）以小号干净的等宽黑色字体直接置于各色样下方。整组色板占据画布中央 80%，四周留白边。顶部居中的标题文字："WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG2 - JIANGLIU INTRODUCTION COLOR REFERENCE"，以小号干净的黑色无衬线字体呈现。

【光线】
整体平直均匀的中性光，色样上无阴影、无高光，完全均匀的色彩还原。

【风格】
专业平面设计色板参考卡，色彩理论图表美学，干净极简的设计，如同一份潘通(Pantone)色卡指南或设计参考图。

【画质】
超写实的干净数字设计渲染，每个色样的边缘锐利干净，完全均匀的平涂色块，专业色卡参考质量，8k 超干净渲染。

【约束】
不要绘画感，不要艺术化，不要插画，不要装饰性，不要渐变，不要纹理，不要照片场景，不要实体物件，色样上不要阴影，不要 3D 渲染，不要彩色背景，不要色样和标签以外的设计元素，不要赛博朋克霓虹色，不要明亮饱和的未来感色彩。
```

A 4:3 aspect ratio cinematic film poster composition for a Chinese sci-fi mythology epic.

OVERALL COMPOSITION (top to bottom):
The top one-third of the frame is dominated by the colossal lower jaw, neck, and upper chest armor of an ancient towering mecha looming on the far horizon, so massive that even at great distance it fills the entire upper portion of the sky. The middle and lower two-thirds of the frame is the desert landscape with a young Daoist priestess standing in dynamic frozen pose at the center, framed from waist-up. The horizon line sits at roughly 60 percent up the frame.

THE COLOSSAL MECHA (background, upper third of frame, rendered as half-silhouette with edge-light only):
The hulking lower head, neck, and chest segment of a ruined ancient Chinese war-god mecha, partially obscured by atmospheric haze and rising sand-dust. The head itself is cropped out of frame above — only from the lower jaw downward is visible. Visible elements: a heavy mechanical jaw segment with gold-trimmed dark red lacquered armor plating, layered dragon-scale neck guard plates wrapping around a thick neck of exposed brass piping and dark cabling, broad shoulder pauldrons rising at the edges, the upper edge of an ornate breast plate inscribed with faded gold seal-script Chinese calligraphy and dragon-cloud motifs. Tattered red banner cloth fragments hang in shreds from the shoulders, frayed at the edges, gold embroidered characters barely visible. The mecha is DEAD — no glowing eyes (head not visible), no light pulses on the armor seals, the inscriptions weathered and dull, the metal heavily oxidized with rust-bloom and sand-pitting, faint sand-streaks running down the lacquered surfaces from centuries of desert wind. Its facing is straight ahead toward the camera, head pointing skyward beyond the frame, neither looking down nor turning aside — the posture of an enormous ancient statue that has stopped functioning but still stands. The entire mecha is rendered as a deep silhouette with light coming only from camera-left, catching the edge of the jaw, the rim of the shoulder pauldron, and the top edge of the breastplate in warm rim-light. The interior of the form falls into deep cool-blue shadow with only faintest hint of dark red lacquer color and oxidized brass undertones. Strong atmospheric haze and sand-particle dust diffuses the form, making it feel both enormous and distant.

THE YOUNG WOMAN (foreground center, mid-frame to lower-frame, waist-up):
A young East Asian woman in her late teens, framed from waist up, standing alone in the center of the frame against the desert. Her body is caught mid-pose in a dramatic dynamic posture: her torso arches backward as if struck by an unseen force or wind from above, her right arm raised high overhead with elbow bent and forearm crossing over her forehead in a partial shielding gesture, her left hand brought to her hip with fingers splayed slightly. Her face is angled upward toward the sky, chin lifted, mouth slightly open with a small gasp of breath, neck exposed in a long arc. Her eyes are open and glowing a vivid blood-crimson red, sharp and supernatural, the irises bright like lit coals while the whites of her eyes remain natural — this is the only fully saturated red in the entire image, drawing the eye immediately. Her short black bob haircut with straight blunt bangs is being whipped sideways by a strong upward wind, strands of jet-black hair fanning out to camera-right horizontally. From the left side of her hair dangles two thin dark-red braided cord tassels with small knotted ends, also flying outward in the wind. A small wooden hairpin is visible.

Her clothing: a high-collared cream-white linen mandarin-collar tunic top with diagonal cross-front cloth-knot closures, the long blousy sleeves billowing dramatically as wind catches them and pushes them upward, the cuffs gathered at her wrists. Visible at her waist line: the high black waistband of her long dark skirt with a crimson red braided cord sash tied at the front, the two long ends of the sash whipping sideways in the wind. A small worn brown canvas talisman satchel hangs at her right hip, with strips of yellow paper talismans bearing faded red brush-stroke Chinese characters stuck onto its flap, the paper edges fluttering in the wind. Her skin shows natural East Asian young woman texture with visible pores, faint imperfections, soft fine peach fuzz catching the warm side-light, no plastic smoothing, no beauty filter retouching.

THE DESERT ENVIRONMENT:
A vast expanse of golden ochre sand stretching to the horizon, with low wind-sculpted dunes catching warm light on one side and falling into cool shadow on the other. In the deep middle-ground, the silhouettes of the eroded ramparts of an ancient frontier fortress — Yumen Pass — stretch across the horizon as a low jagged line, partially obscured by the standing colossus and atmospheric haze. The sand surface near the camera is rippled by wind, scattered with small fragments of weathered mechanical debris — a half-buried curved blade tip, a corroded gear, a broken finger-joint of giant scale — hinting that more dead mechs lie beneath. Wind-blown dust and sand particles drift across the frame.

THE SKY:
A dramatic late-afternoon sky filling the entire upper portion of the frame around the colossal mecha silhouette. Towering cumulus thunderhead cloud columns rise behind the mecha, their tops painted in warm golden-amber where the lowering sun strikes them, their underbellies and recessed shadows in deep cool blue-grey. Patches of clear sky show a saturated but not oversaturated cobalt blue. The clouds have painterly depth and substance, not flat. Warm golden hour light streams from the camera-left direction at a low angle, casting long shadows from the dunes and from the figure across the sand.

TITLE TYPOGRAPHY:
Across the lower portion of the frame, below the figure and floating above the sand, the Chinese film title "万物生" (three Chinese characters meaning "All Things Born / All Things Arise") rendered in elegant calligraphic seal-script style, large but not overpowering, the characters arranged horizontally. The brushwork is bold and weathered, executed in deep ink-black tone with slight texture of dry-brush effect at the edges. The characters sit naturally in the lower-third sand area as if etched into the desert plane, scaled to read clearly from a distance but not crowding the figure. No other text on the poster.

STYLE LOCK:
35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock aesthetic, Christopher Doyle and Roger Deakins cinematography influence, Frank Frazetta fantasy poster painterly composition, Akira Kurosawa epic-scale landscape framing. Fine organic film grain. Low-medium contrast middle-grey tonality with restrained color palette: warm umber ochre sand (#A88554), dark blood-red lacquer accents (#7A2620), deep oxidized brass (#5A4838), warm golden cloud highlights (#D4A857), cool blue-grey cloud shadows (#3D5266), cobalt sky (#4A6F8A), brilliant crimson red eyes only on the figure (#B91C2A) as the sole high-saturation accent. Slight gray tone overall, NOT oversaturated, NOT HDR-pumped. Anamorphic widescreen lens character with subtle barrel curvature at edges and natural lens flare from the side-light.

PHOTOGRAPHIC TONE: This image is a film poster captured on actual film stock during a real photography session, slightly soft and imperfect like a real photograph from a film set — this softness is intentional and desired. Soft natural lighting with realistic falloff, not punchy HDR. Subtle organic film grain only, not heavy digital noise overlay. Natural lens depth of field with realistic focus falloff, not digitally tack-sharp. Natural human skin texture with visible pores and tiny realistic imperfections, no plastic smoothing, no beauty filter, no airbrushed retouching. Materials should look photographically captured, not 3D rendered or AI stylized. Colors with realistic restraint, slight gray tone, not oversaturated, not HDR-pumped.

Avoid: oversharpening, artificial sharpness, heavy digital grain, HDR effect, beauty-filter skin, AI-generated aesthetic, overpolished studio look, plastic smoothing, oversaturation, glossy highlight blowout, generic AI image quality, default model aesthetic bias, anime style, manga style, digital painting style, CG rendered style, 3D rendered look, doll-like skin, porcelain skin, makeup-perfect face, fashion magazine retouching, video game key art style, generic fantasy poster, oversaturated red eyes glowing too brightly like neon, cartoon character proportions, cyberpunk neon aesthetic.

**🀄 中文版**

```
一幅 4:3 画幅的电影感影片海报构图，为一部中国科幻神话史诗而作。

整体构图（自上而下）：
画面顶部三分之一被一座远古巍峨机甲的庞大下颌、颈部与上胸甲所主宰，它矗立于遥远的地平线上，体量之巨，即便相隔甚远也填满了整个天空的上部。画面中部与下部的三分之二是沙漠景观，一名年轻的女道士立于中央，呈动态定格姿态，取景为半身（齐腰以上）。地平线约位于画面 60% 高处。

那座庞大机甲（背景，画面上部三分之一，渲染为仅带边缘光的半剪影）：
一尊残破的远古中国战神机甲的下半头部、颈部与胸段，部分被大气霾与扬起的沙尘所遮蔽。头部本身在上方被裁出画外——仅自下颌往下可见。可见元素：一段沉重的机械颌部，覆盖金边深红漆甲板；层叠的龙鳞护颈板缠绕着一截粗颈，颈上露出黄铜管路与深色缆线；两侧高耸的宽阔肩甲；一面华丽胸甲的上缘，其上铭刻着褪色的金色篆体中国书法与龙云纹样。残破的红色旗帜布片从肩部垂挂成缕，边缘磨损，金线绣字依稀可辨。这座机甲已**死亡**——双眼无辉光（头部不可见），甲面封印上无光脉搏动，铭文风化暗淡，金属严重氧化并生有锈花与沙蚀，历经数百年沙漠风蚀，漆面上隐隐拖曳着沙痕。其朝向径直正对相机，头部指向画框之外的天空，既不俯视也不侧转——是一尊已停止运转却仍然伫立的远古巨像的姿态。整座机甲渲染为深剪影，光线仅自相机左侧而来，在颌缘、肩甲边沿与胸甲顶缘勾勒出暖调轮廓光。形体内部落入深邃的冷蓝阴影，仅有最微弱的深红漆色与氧化黄铜底调的暗示。强烈的大气霾与沙粒尘埃弥散其形体，使它既显得庞大又显得遥远。

那名年轻女子（前景中央，画面中部至下部，半身）：
一名年约十八九岁的东亚年轻女子，取景为齐腰以上，独自立于画面中央、背向沙漠。她的身体被定格于一个戏剧化的动态姿势之中：躯干向后弓起，仿佛被某种来自上方的无形之力或风所击中，右臂高举过头、肘部弯曲、前臂横过额前作半遮挡之势，左手按于髋部、手指微张。她的脸向上仰望天空，下巴抬起，嘴微张、带一缕轻喘的吸气，颈部以一道长弧线舒展显露。她的双眼睁开，闪烁着鲜明的血红光芒，锐利而超自然，虹膜亮如点燃的炭火，而眼白仍保持自然——这是整幅画面中唯一完全饱和的红色，立刻攫住视线。她的黑色齐耳短发配平齐的厚刘海，被一阵强劲的上扬之风吹向一侧，缕缕乌黑发丝向相机右侧水平地扇开。她头发左侧垂着两条细的深红色编绳流苏，末端打着小结，也在风中向外飞扬。一枚小木簪依稀可见。

她的衣着：一件高领的奶白色亚麻立领上衣，前襟斜向交叠以布结系合，宽松的长袖在风掀之下戏剧化地鼓起、被向上推送，袖口收束于手腕。腰线处可见：她那条深色长裙的高黑色腰头，前方系着一根绯红编绳腰带，腰带的两条长端在风中向一侧甩动。她右髋处挂着一只磨旧的棕色帆布符袋，袋盖上贴着数条黄纸符箓，符上是褪色的红色笔触中国字，纸边在风中翻飞。她的皮肤呈现东亚年轻女子的自然质感，可见毛孔、淡淡的瑕疵，柔软的细绒在暖侧光下被照亮，无塑料般的磨皮，无美颜滤镜修饰。

沙漠环境：
一片金赭色的广袤沙地一直延伸至地平线，低矮的风蚀沙丘一侧承接暖光、另一侧落入冷阴影。在纵深的中景里，一座远古边塞要塞——玉门关——的侵蚀残垣以一道低矮锯齿状的轮廓线横亘于地平线上，部分被伫立的巨像与大气霾所遮蔽。靠近相机的沙面被风吹出涟漪，散落着风化机械残骸的细小碎片——半埋的弯刃尖端、锈蚀的齿轮、巨型尺度的断裂指节——暗示着更多死去的机甲长眠于沙下。风卷的尘与沙粒飘过画面。

天空：
一片戏剧化的午后晚段天空，环绕着庞大机甲的剪影、填满整个画面上部。高耸的积雨云团柱在机甲身后升腾，其顶部被低垂的太阳染成暖金琥珀色，云的下腹与凹陷的阴影处则呈深冷蓝灰。几处晴空显露出饱和却不过饱和的钴蓝。云层具有绘画般的纵深与质感，并不扁平。暖金的黄金时刻光线自相机左侧以低角度倾泻而下，从沙丘与人物处投下长长的影子横过沙地。

标题排版：
在画面下部、人物下方并悬浮于沙地之上，是中文片名「万物生」（三个汉字，意为"All Things Born / All Things Arise / 万物生发"）以优雅的篆体书法风格呈现，字号大而不压迫，三字横向排列。笔触雄浑而沧桑，以深墨黑色调写就，边缘略带飞白枯笔的质感。三字自然地落于下三分之一的沙地区域，仿佛镌刻于沙漠平面之上，尺度足以在远处清晰辨读，又不至于挤迫人物。海报上无其他文字。

风格锁定：
35mm Kodak Vision3 500T 胶片质感，受 Christopher Doyle 与 Roger Deakins 摄影影响，Frank Frazetta 奇幻海报的绘画式构图，黑泽明（Akira Kurosawa）史诗尺度的风景取景。细腻的有机胶片颗粒。低至中等对比度的中灰影调，配以克制的色板：暖赭褐沙色 (#A88554)、深血红漆点缀 (#7A2620)、深氧化黄铜 (#5A4838)、暖金云高光 (#D4A857)、冷蓝灰云阴影 (#3D5266)、钴蓝天空 (#4A6F8A)、仅人物身上的明亮绯红双眼 (#B91C2A) 作为唯一的高饱和点缀。整体略带灰调，并非过饱和，并非 HDR 强推。变形宽银幕镜头质感，边缘带有细微的桶形畸变，并有来自侧光的自然镜头眩光。

摄影基调：本图是一幅在真实拍摄过程中、捕捉于实体胶片之上的电影海报，如同来自电影片场的真实照片那样略带柔软与不完美——这种柔软是有意为之、且为所求的。柔和的自然光线，带有真实的光线衰减，并非生硬的 HDR。仅有细微的有机胶片颗粒，并非厚重的数码噪点叠加。自然的镜头景深与真实的对焦衰减，并非数码化的锐利到极致。自然的人类皮肤质感，可见毛孔与细小的真实瑕疵，无塑料般磨皮，无美颜滤镜，无喷枪式修饰。材质应看起来如摄影捕捉，而非 3D 渲染或 AI 风格化。色彩带有真实的克制，略偏灰调，并非过饱和，并非 HDR 强推。

避免：过度锐化、人工锐度、厚重数码颗粒、HDR 效果、美颜滤镜皮肤、AI 生成感、过度抛光的影棚质感、塑料般磨皮、过饱和、高光溢出泛白、千篇一律的 AI 图像质感、默认模型审美偏向、动漫风格、漫画风格、数字绘画风格、CG 渲染风格、3D 渲染外观、玩偶般皮肤、瓷器般皮肤、妆容完美的脸、时尚杂志式修图、电子游戏 key art 风格、千篇一律的奇幻海报、过饱和而亮如霓虹般刺眼的红眼、卡通人物比例、赛博朋克霓虹美学。
